. Busy-write leg. Run the bring-up if the chain is not up. Reset OTBN and wait for `STATUS` = IDLE. Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.done`. Read `CTRL` and expect 0.
. Load a program that reads the RND source into IMEM. The leaf builds it from the base instruction set of OTBN.
. Assert the TRNG reset (bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` low) and hold it, so that the RND request gets no acknowledge. Write `CMD` = EXECUTE. Wait for `STATUS` = 0x01 (a bounded wait).
. This is the error injection. Point: `CTRL`. Kind: `software_errs_fatal` = 1 at `STATUS` 0x01. Expected response: the write is ignored. Write `CTRL.software_errs_fatal` = 1, k times. After each write read `CTRL` and expect 0, and read `STATUS` and expect 0x01. This is a second error injection. Point: `CMD`. Kind: `CMD` = SEC_WIPE_DMEM (0xc3) at `STATUS` 0x01. Expected response: the write is ignored. Write `CMD` = 0xc3, j times. After each write read `STATUS` and expect 0x01 (CHK-OTBN-CMD-IGNORE).
. Release the TRNG reset. Run the bring-up. Wait for `STATUS` = IDLE (a bounded wait). Read `ERR_BITS` and log it as `OBS-OTBN-BUSYWR-ERR`. Read `STATUS` and expect it is not 0xFF.
. Write `CTRL.software_errs_fatal` = 1 while OTBN is idle. Read `CTRL` and expect 1. Reset OTBN.
. Ignored-command leg. Wait for `STATUS` = IDLE (a bounded wait) and write 1 to `INTR_STATE.done`. Read `INTR_STATE.done` as 0. This is the error injection. Point: `CMD`. Kind: the drawn unrecognised value at `STATUS` IDLE. Expected response: the write is ignored. Write `CMD` with the drawn value. Poll `STATUS` back to back for a window of 4 times t_cmd (logged). Read `INTR_STATE.done`, `ERR_BITS` and `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE`. Log `ERR_BITS` as `OBS-OTBN-CMDIGN-ERR`.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-OTBN-CMD-END
|After each of EXECUTE (0xd8), SEC_WIPE_DMEM (0xc3) and SEC_WIPE_IMEM (0x1e), `STATUS` reaches IDLE (0x00), no value read on the way is 0xFF, `INTR_STATE.done` reads 1, and `ERR_BITS` and `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE` read 0 (`otbn.adoc`, `CMD`, `STATUS`, `INTR_STATE`, `ERR_BITS`, `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE`). A wrong DUT hangs (the bounded wait times out), locks (0xFF), raises no `done`, or sets an error bit. Anchor: `STATUS`, `INTR_STATE`, `ERR_BITS` and `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE` readback. Control: `INTR_STATE.done` reads 0 before the command, so the 1 comes from the command. Evidence: `CHK-OTBN-CMD-END PASS seed=<n> idx=<i> cmd=0x<c> final=0x00 done_state=1 err_bits=0x0 fatal_cause=0x0 ctrl_done_before=0`.
|CHK-OTBN-CMD-BUSY
|After each command the first non-IDLE `STATUS` code after the `CMD` write is the busy code of that command: 0x01 for EXECUTE, 0x02 for SEC_WIPE_DMEM and 0x03 for SEC_WIPE_IMEM. The code 0x04 may follow it before IDLE (`otbn.adoc`, `STATUS`: each busy value represents an operation started by a write to `CMD`). A wrong DUT shows another busy code, or goes to IDLE with no busy code. Anchor: the OTBN status probe (an observation tap), sampled on every clock, because a polled read can miss a short code. Control: `INTR_STATE.done` reads 0 before the command. The leaf logs the polled values as `OBS-OTBN-STATUS-SEQ seed=<n> idx=<i> cmd=0x<c> polled=<codes>`. Evidence: `CHK-OTBN-CMD-BUSY PASS seed=<n> idx=<i> cmd=0x<c> first_busy=0x<b> expect=0x<b>`.
|CHK-OTBN-INSN
|For the programs of a and b instructions `INSN_CNT` differs by b minus a, and the DMEM result of each run equals the Python golden (`otbn.adoc`, `INSN_CNT`: the number of executed instructions). After each OTBN reset of the leg the OTBN status probe shows 0x04 at the first clock after the gated reset releases, and then 0x00 within the bounded wait for `STATUS` = IDLE (`otbn.adoc`, `STATUS`: reset value 0x4; 0x00 is IDLE). A wrong DUT with a stuck counter shows a difference of 0, and one that does not reset to 0 shows a non-zero read after the OTBN reset. A DUT that leaves the reset in another state, or never reaches IDLE, fails the reset arc. The pre-seeded complement of the golden makes a program that never stores its result fail. Anchor: `INSN_CNT` and DMEM readback, and the OTBN status probe (an observation tap) for the reset arc. Evidence: `CHK-OTBN-INSN PASS seed=<n> a=<na> b=<nb> cnt_reset=0 cnt_a=<ca> cnt_b=<cb> diff=<d> expect=<nb-na> result==golden reset_arc=0x04,0x00`.
|CHK-OTBN-DONE-LINE
|Vector bit 29 (source 30) equals `INTR_STATE.done` AND `INTR_ENABLE.done` at every sample (`interrupts.adoc`, `otbn.adoc`, `INTR_STATE`, `INTR_ENABLE`, `INTR_TEST`). With the enable clear a finished command sets the state bit and bit 29 stays low for the window. After the enable write bit 29 rises, and after a write of 0 to the enable it falls. After the write-one clear of the state bit it falls, with the enable set and with the enable clear. With the enable set, bit 29 rises with the state bit. An `INTR_TEST.done` write sets the state bit and the line follows the enable in the same way; the enable-set `INTR_TEST` cell is a control that the open owner grades. A wrong DUT that raises the line without the enable, ignores the enable, or keeps the line after the clear shows it at one of these samples. Anchor: `INTR_STATE` readback and the vector probe. Control: the rise after the enable write in the same run, and the enable-set run of the same seed. Evidence: `CHK-OTBN-DONE-LINE PASS seed=<n> enable=0 state=1 vec29=0 then enable=1 vec29=1 then w1c vec29=0`, with the variants `enable=1 state=1 vec29=1 then w1c vec29=0` for the enable-set control and `via=test` for the `INTR_TEST` runs, which add `then enable=0 vec29=0 then w1c(enable=0) vec29=0`.
|CHK-OTBN-CMD-IGNORE
|A `CMD` write with an unrecognised value at `STATUS` IDLE leaves `STATUS` at IDLE at every poll of a window of 4 times t_cmd and `INTR_STATE.done` at 0, and a `CMD` write of SEC_WIPE_DMEM while `STATUS` is 0x01 leaves `STATUS` at 0x01 after each of the j writes (`otbn.hjson`, `CMD`: writes are ignored if OTBN is not idle; unrecognized commands are ignored). A wrong DUT that starts a command from an unrecognised code shows a busy status or a `done`; a DUT that accepts a command while busy shows a `STATUS` other than 0x01. `ERR_BITS` is logged and not graded. Anchor: `STATUS` and `INTR_STATE` readback. Control: the recognised commands of the command leg of the same seed end with `INTR_STATE.done` = 1 from a `done` read as 0 before the command (CHK-OTBN-CMD-END), so the `done` read of this check can show a started command, and `STATUS` reads 0x01 before each busy write (CHK-OTBN-BUSYWR). Evidence: `CHK-OTBN-CMD-IGNORE PASS seed=<n> case=<idle_unrecognised or busy_wipe> cmd=0x<c> status=<0x00 or 0x01> done=<0 or n/a> window_clk=<w or n/a> writes=<j>` and `OBS-OTBN-CMDIGN-ERR seed=<n> err_bits=0x<e>`.
|CHK-OTBN-BUSYWR
|A write of `CTRL.software_errs_fatal` = 1 while `STATUS` is 0x01 is ignored: `CTRL` reads 0 after each of the k writes, `STATUS` still reads 0x01, and after the chain returns `STATUS` is not 0xFF (`otbn.adoc`, `CTRL`: writes are ignored if OTBN is not idle). The busy hold relies on the stall of an `RND` read (`csr.yml`, `rnd`) and on the missing acknowledgement during the TRNG adapter clear (`trng.adoc`, Reset and Recovery Boundaries); if `STATUS` does not reach 0x01 the bounded wait fails the check. `ERR_BITS` is logged and not graded, because no cited text states an error for an ignored write. A wrong DUT that stores the write shows `CTRL` = 1. Anchor: `STATUS` and `CTRL` readback. Control: the same write while idle reads back 1, so the register can hold the bit. Evidence: `CHK-OTBN-BUSYWR PASS seed=<n> busy_status=0x01 busy_writes=<k> ctrl_after_busy_write=0 ctrl_after_idle_write=1` and `OBS-OTBN-BUSYWR-ERR seed=<n> err_bits=0x<e>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades the command and status sequence, the done line, `INSN_CNT` and the busy-write rule of `CTRL`. The following are not claimed. The `ERR_BITS` value after an ignored `CMD` or `CTRL` write is logged and not graded. A `done` from the initial wipe after reset is logged only. The way into LOCKED, the LOCKED hold and the unlock are not stated (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The clear of `ERR_BITS` by a write is stated (`otbn.hjson`), but the text states no way to set a non-zero `ERR_BITS` other than the Key Manager `KEY_INVALID` path, so no check grades it (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); the leaf reads `ERR_BITS` only to expect 0 after a command and to log it after an ignored write. The ADD, XOR and AND results belong to `sep_otbn_compute_rand_test`, the `INTR_TEST` walk of the done line with the enable set belongs to `sep_pic_irq_source_map_delivery_test`, and the line gate of the other sources belongs to the extension leg of `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test`.

'''''

[[sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 1.39 `sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (ABR)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_abr_command_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and write of the ABR window (approved); published NIST ACVP known-answer vectors as the expected values of every graded result (ML-DSA key generation, signature generation and verification, ML-KEM key generation, encapsulation and decapsulation). Vector source: `hw/sys/sep/dv/fw/tests/common/gen_abr_nist_vectors.py` extracts the vectors from the NIST ACVP-Server internalProjection files of the ML-DSA-87 keyGen, sigGen and sigVer groups (FIPS 204; the sigGen and sigVer groups with external mu) and of the ML-KEM-1024 keyGen and encapDecap groups (FIPS 203; the decapsulation group), and `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/env/sep_abr_nist.py` and `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/env/sep_abr_kem_nist.py` parse them. Implementation requirement: this test needs a vector table that holds every external-mu sigVer reject case (`testPassed` false) of the published group with its ACVP `reason`, and every `modified ciphertext` decapsulation case of the published group with its expected shared key K. `hw/sys/sep/dv/fw/tests/common/gen_abr_nist_vectors.py` emits one reject case of each kind (lines 150-151, `next(...)`), so the generator and the two parsers are extended to emit and load the full reject tables, from one pinned ACVP-Server snapshot that also holds the cases the open KAT tests use. The expected keys K of the decapsulation cases in the table are pairwise different, and the table loader checks this. No other reference model. The ABR reset bit of `SW_RESET_N`. No observation tap. No inject beyond the register writes of the scenario.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the single-step cell of the seed (one of the six), the order of the 3 trials (the two combined cells and the single-step cell; a first trial has the predecessor "none"), the re-arm of each trial (ZEROIZE or an ABR reset), the vector row of the single-step cell (a table with one row gives that row), the reason class of the ML-DSA reject trial (one of the `reason` values of the reject cases in the published external-mu sigVer group: `modified message`, `modified signature - commitment`, `modified signature - hint`, `modified signature - z`) and then one reject row of that class, and two different `modified ciphertext` decapsulation rows. The draws exclude the reject row that `sep_abr_mldsa_sign_verify_kat_test` grades and the modified-ciphertext row that `sep_abr_mlkem_kat_test` grades. `reseed = 4` samples different cells, orders, re-arms, reason classes and rows. The two combined cells, one single-step cell, one ML-DSA reject trial and two ML-KEM reject trials run on every seed. Each trial is an ML-DSA-87 or ML-KEM-1024 operation with real-size window transfers, so the leaf draws the single-step cell, the re-arms and the reject rows and does not walk them. The single-step cell, the re-arm of each combined cell and the reason class close on a merge of regressions. The predecessor of a cell, the row inside a reason class and the ML-KEM rows have no bin.

*Objective*: ML-DSA-87 runs key generation, signature generation and verification, and ML-KEM-1024 runs key generation, encapsulation and decapsulation. After an operation ABR parks with `STATUS.VALID` set, and `STATUS.READY` returns only after a zeroize or a reset. The command field also has the codes KEYGEN+SIGN and KEYGEN+DECAPS. ML-DSA-87 verification (FIPS 204) writes to `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES` the c~' that the algorithm computes from the public key, mu and the signature, and a signature verifies only when c~' equals c~. ML-KEM-1024 decapsulation (FIPS 203) returns the key that the algorithm computes from the decapsulation key and the ciphertext; for a ciphertext that fails the re-encryption check this is the implicit-rejection key. Spec: `hw/sys/sep/doc/adams_bridge.adoc` (Adams Bridge (ABR), OCAH Modifications); `integration/rdl/sep/abr_reg.rdl` (`MLDSA_CTRL`, `MLKEM_CTRL`, `MLDSA_STATUS`, `MLKEM_STATUS`, `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES`, `MLDSA_SIGNATURE`, the ruling source for the command encodings and the window sizes).

*Test Coverage*: On every seed the two combined cells (ML-DSA KEYGEN+SIGN, ML-KEM KEYGEN+DECAPS) and one drawn single-step cell (ML-DSA KEYGEN, SIGNING or VERIFYING; ML-KEM KEYGEN, ENCAPS or DECAPS), in drawn order, each with a drawn re-arm (ZEROIZE or an ABR reset). Over a merge of regressions every single-step cell and both re-arms of each combined cell run. Inputs come from published vectors. One ML-DSA-87 reject trial of the VERIFYING operation per seed: a drawn ACVP external-mu sigVer case whose expected result is fail, of a drawn reason class; and two ML-KEM-1024 reject trials of the DECAPS operation: two different drawn ACVP `modified ciphertext` cases. Error injection: the modified input of the published case (the mu input, or the commitment, hint or z part of the signature; the ciphertext). Extends `sep_abr_mldsa_sign_verify_kat_test`, `sep_abr_mlkem_kat_test` and `sep_abr_mldsa_keygen_kat_test`, which drive the six single-step cells and grade the valid status, the zeroize clear, the self-reset and READY after zeroize and after an ABR reset (`CHK-STATUS-VALID`, `CHK-ZEROIZE`, `CHK-RESET-STATUS`, `CHK-KEM-ZEROIZE`, `CHK-ABR-SELF-RESET`). The first of them grades one ACVP sigVer reject case and the second one ACVP modified-ciphertext case. This entry grades only the new cells: the two combined cells (park and re-arm), READY low after the single-step operation, the order independence, and the drawn ACVP reject cases that the open tests do not drive. The owners of the open tests keep the rest. Functional coverage: `sep_abr_completion_cg` (`x_op_protocol`, `cp_combined_cmd`, `cp_verify_outcome`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set `SW_RESET_N` to the baseline of the common rules with the bits of KMAC, AES, HMAC and OTBN clear and the ABR bit (bit 6) set. Log the baseline.
. Draw the plan with `SepSeededRng`. Log `PLAN seed=<n>` with the single-step cell, the trial order, the re-arm of each trial, the vector row, the reason class and ACVP `tcId` of the ML-DSA reject row, and the ACVP `tcId` of the two ML-KEM reject rows.
. Trials. For each of the 3 trials in the drawn order, run steps 4 to 7 and then the re-arm of the trial: step 8 (ZEROIZE) or step 9 (ABR reset).
. Wait for `READY` = 1 of the block of the cell (a bounded wait). Write the inputs of the cell from the vector row, as the open helper of that cell does: ML-DSA KEYGEN, the seed; SIGNING, the private key and mu with `EXTERNAL_MU` set; VERIFYING, the public key, mu and the signature with `EXTERNAL_MU` set; KEYGEN+SIGN, the seed and mu with `EXTERNAL_MU` set; ML-KEM KEYGEN, the seeds d and z; ENCAPS, the encapsulation key and the message m; DECAPS, the decapsulation key and the ciphertext; KEYGEN+DECAPS, the seeds d and z and the ciphertext of the DECAPS row.
. Write the command code to `MLDSA_CTRL` or `MLKEM_CTRL`: 1 KEYGEN, 2 SIGNING or ENCAPS, 3 VERIFYING or DECAPS, 4 KEYGEN+SIGN or KEYGEN+DECAPS. Wait for `STATUS.VALID` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Read `STATUS`.
. For a single-step cell, read the result windows that the open helper of that cell compares (ML-DSA: the public key for KEYGEN, the signature for SIGNING, `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES` for VERIFYING; ML-KEM: the encapsulation key and the decapsulation key for KEYGEN, the ciphertext and the shared key for ENCAPS, the shared key for DECAPS) and compare each with the vector. In the KEYGEN+SIGN trial, read the signature window and keep it for step 11. No check grades a result of a combined cell.
. Read `STATUS` again, after the result reads and before the re-arm. In a ZEROIZE trial of a combined cell, then read the secret output windows of step 8 and log for each window whether it holds a non-zero word (`CTRL-ABR-SECRET-PRE seed=<n> cell=<c> window=<w> nonzero=<0 or 1>`).
. ZEROIZE trial. Write `ZEROIZE` of the block of the cell. Wait for `READY` = 1 (a bounded wait). Read `VALID` and log it. For a combined cell read the secret output windows (`MLDSA_PRIVKEY_OUT`, `MLKEM_DECAPS_KEY` and `MLKEM_SHARED_KEY`) and expect every word to read 0. A window that held no non-zero word in step 7 logs `OBS-ABR-SECRET-EMPTY` and is not graded.
. ABR reset trial. After step 7 reset ABR (bit 6 of `SW_RESET_N` low, then high). Wait for `READY` = 1 (a bounded wait). Read `VALID` and log it. For a combined cell read the secret output windows and log them.
. Combined signature, logged only. Wait for `READY` = 1. Run ML-DSA KEYGEN with the seed of the KEYGEN+SIGN cell and read the public key. Write `ZEROIZE` and wait for `READY` = 1.
. Write the public key, mu and the signature kept in step 6 and write VERIFYING with `EXTERNAL_MU` set. Wait for `VALID` = 1. Compare `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES` with c~ of the signature as written and log `accept` or `reject`. Write `ZEROIZE` and wait for `READY` = 1.
. Tamper control. Write the public key, mu and the signature of the ACVP accept case (`testPassed` true) of the vector table, and write VERIFYING with `EXTERNAL_MU` set. Wait for `VALID` = 1 and expect `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES` to equal c~ of the signature as written. Write `ZEROIZE` and wait for `READY` = 1.
. This is the error injection. Point: the input that the drawn ACVP case modifies (the mu input, or the commitment, hint or z part of the signature). Kind: a published sigVer reject case. Expected response: reject (ACVP `testPassed` false). Wait for `READY` = 1, write the public key, mu and the signature of the drawn reject row, write VERIFYING with `EXTERNAL_MU` set, and wait for `VALID` = 1 (a bounded wait). Read `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES` and `ERROR`, log `ERROR`, compare the result with c~ of the signature as written (the first 16 words of the signature window), then write `ZEROIZE` and wait for `READY` = 1.
. KEM control. Write the decapsulation key and the ciphertext of the ACVP `valid decapsulation` case of the vector table, write DECAPS, wait for `VALID` = 1, read the shared key and compare it with the expected key K of the case. Write `ZEROIZE` and wait for `READY` = 1.
. This is the error injection of the KEM leg. Point: the ciphertext of the drawn ACVP case. Kind: a published `modified ciphertext` decapsulation case. Expected response: the expected key K of the case (the implicit-rejection key). Write the decapsulation key and the ciphertext of the first drawn row, write DECAPS, wait for `VALID` = 1 (a bounded wait), read the shared key and compare it with K of the row. Write `ZEROIZE` and wait for `READY` = 1. Repeat with the second drawn row.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-ABR-COMBO
|The combined commands KEYGEN+SIGN (`MLDSA_CTRL.CTRL` = 4) and KEYGEN+DECAPS (`MLKEM_CTRL.CTRL` = 4) are accepted and park: when `VALID` first reads 1, `READY` reads 0 and `ERROR` reads 0 (`abr_reg.rdl`, the command encodings; `adams_bridge.adoc`, OCAH Modifications: the sequencer parks with `STATUS.VALID` set). A wrong DUT that ignores the combined code never shows `VALID` (the bounded wait times out), or sets `ERROR`, or shows `READY` with `VALID`. Anchor: `MLDSA_STATUS` and `MLKEM_STATUS` readback with the field masks of the RDL. Control: the single-step cell of the same seed matches its vector (CHK-ABR-ORDER), so the trial flow of inputs, command and wait produces a result. The leaf also logs the outcome of verifying the combined signature, which no check grades. Evidence: `CHK-ABR-COMBO PASS seed=<n> cell=<dsa_keygen_sign or kem_keygen_decaps> valid=1 ready=0 error=0` and `OBS-ABR-COMBO-VERIFY seed=<n> verify=<accept or reject or n/a>`.
|CHK-ABR-PARK
|At the end of every cell, after the result reads and before the re-arm, a combined cell shows `VALID` = 1, `READY` = 0 and `ERROR` = 0, and a single-step cell shows `READY` = 0 (`adams_bridge.adoc`: `STATUS.READY` re-asserts only after a zeroize or a reset). A wrong DUT that returns to READY by itself shows `READY` = 1. For a single-step cell `VALID` and `ERROR` are logged, because the open tests grade them. Anchor: `STATUS` readback. Control: `READY` reads 1 after the re-arm of the same cell (CHK-ABR-REARM), so the bit can be read as 1. Evidence: `CHK-ABR-PARK PASS seed=<n> cell=<c> valid=<v> ready=0 error=<e>`.
|CHK-ABR-REARM
|After a combined operation a ZEROIZE or an ABR reset makes `READY` 1 (the re-arm of each combined cell is a seed draw, so both re-arms of each cell close on a merge of regressions), and after a ZEROIZE each secret output window that held a non-zero word before the ZEROIZE reads 0 (`adams_bridge.adoc`: READY returns only after a zeroize or a reset; `abr_reg.rdl`, `ZEROIZE`: zeroize all internal registers and memories). A wrong DUT that keeps `READY` at 0, or leaves a secret window readable, shows it. `VALID` after the re-arm and the windows after a reset are logged and not graded. Anchor: `STATUS` and window readback. The re-arm of the single-step cells is a control of the open checks. Control for the zero windows: the read of the same window before the ZEROIZE (`CTRL-ABR-SECRET-PRE`) shows a non-zero word, so a window that reads 0 whatever the ZEROIZE does is not graded; such a window logs `OBS-ABR-SECRET-EMPTY seed=<n> cell=<c> window=<w>`. Evidence: `CHK-ABR-REARM PASS seed=<n> cell=<combined> via=<zeroize or reset> ready=1 secret_window=<0, empty or n/a>` and `OBS-ABR-VALID seed=<n> via=<zeroize or reset> valid=<v>`.
|CHK-ABR-ORDER
|The result of the single-step cell equals the published vector whatever operation ran before it (the cell and its predecessor are seed draws) (`abr_reg.rdl`; the vectors of the open helpers). A wrong DUT whose result depends on leftover state of the earlier operation shows a mismatch in the cell with a different predecessor. Anchor: result window readback against the vector. The log lists the operations in the order they ran. Evidence: `CHK-ABR-ORDER PASS seed=<n> ops=<list> all_match=1`.
|CHK-ABR-TAMPER
|For the drawn ACVP external-mu sigVer case whose expected result is fail (`testPassed` false), `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES` differs from c~ of the signature as written, which is the first 16 words of the signature window (`abr_reg.rdl`, `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES`: the signature is verified when the register equals c~; FIPS 204, ML-DSA.Verify_internal: the signature verifies only when the computed c~' equals c~, z is inside its bound and the hint is well formed). The ACVP verdict is the expected result, and the register is the verdict of the device, so for every published reject case the register differs from c~, whatever the reason class. A wrong DUT that does not read the modified part, or that copies c~ into the result, shows c~. `ERROR` is logged, because the text does not state it for a rejected signature. Anchor: `MLDSA_VERIFY_RES` readback against the signature words as written. Control: the ACVP accept case of the same seed gives a result equal to c~ (tamper control), so the flow can produce an accept. Evidence: `CHK-ABR-TAMPER PASS seed=<n> tcid=<id> reason=<class> verify_res_eq_c=0 first_diff_word=<w> res=<hex> c=<hex> control_accept=1`.
|CHK-ABR-KEM-FLIP
|For each of the two drawn ACVP `modified ciphertext` decapsulation cases the shared key equals the expected key K of the case, all 8 words (FIPS 203, ML-KEM.Decaps_internal: a ciphertext that fails the re-encryption check returns the implicit-rejection key J(z, c); ACVP publishes K for each case). Each case carries its own decapsulation key. The expected keys of the vector table and the key of the valid case are pairwise different, so a wrong DUT that skips the implicit rejection, returns a key cached from an earlier operation, or returns a constant, fails the exact compare. Anchor: shared key readback against the vector. Control: the ACVP `valid decapsulation` case gives its expected key (KEM control). Evidence: `CHK-ABR-KEM-FLIP PASS seed=<n> tcid=<id> ss==K=1 ss=<hex>`, one line for each case.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades the combined cells, READY after the single-step operation, the order independence and the drawn ACVP reject cases that the open tests do not drive. The following are not claimed. A start without a re-arm is blocked and never driven. The output registers and the signing randomness of the combined commands have no independent expected value. The verify outcome of a combined signature is logged only (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). Verify and decapsulation inputs other than the published ACVP cases are not driven, because the leaf has no expected value for them. Whether a reset clears the secret output windows is blocked. A zero window after a ZEROIZE proves that the window is not readable, because the read port depends on `VALID`, and does not prove that the memory is wiped. The single-step results, the valid status, the zeroize clear and the self-reset belong to the open ABR tests. The ABR interrupt sources and the key-vault seed path are not driven.

'''''
[[sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test]]
===== 1.40 `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS (the X check of the reset readback runs on VCS only)

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); register lists from the generated IP-XACT (`sep_reg_meta.register_fields`) and the RDL reset column. Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the VCS X check on the readback; `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset); `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (logged, not graded). Approved inject: the noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` in the mode `balanced` on all 12 lanes (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"; the `+esrc_noise_force` plusarg routes this port into the decorrelators and generates nothing). No other inject. No PROPOSED item.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the register order of the read-only leg and of the restore leg; the restore value v1 of each field (it differs from the reset value in at least one bit; `HT_WATERMARK_NUM` takes a supported encoding from 1 to 4 and `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.LANE_SEL` takes a lane from 1 to 11); the order of the two `MODULE_ENABLE` trials; the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern; the `INTR_ENABLE` mask E and the `INTR_TEST` mask T over the eight defined bits (each with at least one 0 and one 1; T holds bits 8 and 12 and at least one other set bit), the bit of the one-bit control and the clear subset C (it holds bits 8 and 12 and leaves at least one other set bit of T out, so it is a proper subset of T). `reseed = 4` samples different orders, values and masks; the plan shape (the registers, the cells and the legs) is the same on every seed.

*Objective*: Read-only ESRC registers ignore writes, including `COMPONENT_ID`. After a TRNG reset every read-write field outside the `FIPS_LOCK` list and outside `CTRL` reads its reset value. Once `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` is set, a write of 0 to `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` is ignored and the module keeps running; without the lock the same write stops it. With all eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` set, the eight `INTR_STATUS` bits follow `INTR_TEST`, are independent of each other and clear on write-one; a write to `INTR_TEST` leaves the stored `INTR_ENABLE` unchanged. The specification does not state whether the status latches with an enable bit clear; the test grades `INTR_ENABLE` as a stored value only and logs `INTR_STATUS` for a drawn mask. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`COMPONENT_ID`, `CTRL`, `FIPS_LOCK`, `HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE`, `MAIN_SM_STATUS`, `FIFO_STATUS`, `INTR_STATUS`, `INTR_ENABLE`, `INTR_TEST`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Reset).

*Test Coverage*: Read-only registers and `COMPONENT_ID` after a write of the complement (error case); the restore of every unlocked read-write field by a TRNG reset; the `MODULE_ENABLE` write of 0 after the lock and without it; `INTR_TEST` and the write-one clear over the eight defined `INTR_STATUS` bits with `INTR_ENABLE` all ones, with a drawn test mask, the corner masks all ones and all zeros, and the clear of a proper subset that holds bits 8 and 12 (`FIFO_OVERFLOW` and `FIFO_UNDERFLOW`); the one-bit corner runs as a control only, because `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test` grades each source alone; `INTR_ENABLE` as a stored value for a drawn mask and for its complement (the status for a drawn enable mask is logged and not graded). This test extends `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`, `sep_trng_reset_recovery_test` and `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test`. It grades only the cells that those tests do not grade; the open owners keep the rest. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_startup_health_cg` (`cp_component_id`), `sep_crypto_reg_lock_cg` (`cp_module_enable_locked`, the locked bin), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`) and `sep_crypto_reset_cg` (`cp_reset_evidence` bin `esrc_regs`). The single-source `INTR_TEST` raise and the unlocked `MODULE_ENABLE` trial are controls and fill no bin.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Build the register lists from the generated IP-XACT and the RDL at run time. A read-write field has software access rw and is not RW1C, RW1S, W1C or W1P. The locked list holds the fields whose RDL text says "FIPS_LOCK.LOCK prevents software changes". A read-only register has only read-only fields. Log the size of the locked list as `OBS-LOCK-COUNT`.
. Exclude from the walk `FIFO_RDATA`, `BIW_OBS_RDATA`, `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`, `INTR_TEST`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH`, `FIPS_LOCK` and every register that hardware updates while the module runs (status, count and total-failure registers).
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). Log the baseline.
. Run the reset sequence.
. Write `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` = 0, then `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, then poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). This is the quiet state.
. Read-only leg. In seeded order, read each read-only register twice. Log a register whose two reads differ in its RDL field bits as `OBS-RO-DYNAMIC` and do not write it.
. Error injection. Write the 32-bit complement of the value read to each read-only register whose two reads are equal, with all byte strobes set. The master does not treat the response code as an error. Expected response: the register reads its first-read value; the response code is logged only, as `OBS-RO-RESP`.
. Read each written register again and compare its RDL field bits with those of its first read. Log its Reserved bits (for example `COMPONENT_ID` bits 23:16) as `OBS-RO-RSVD`; the specification states no read value for them. `COMPONENT_ID` is one of the read-only registers of this leg and is always written.
. Interrupt leg, clean-up. Write 1 to the eight defined `INTR_STATUS` bits and read `INTR_STATUS`. The eight defined bits read 0; a bit that stays set fails the leaf.
. Corner case all zeros. Write 0 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE`, write 0 to `INTR_TEST`, then read `INTR_STATUS` and `INTR_ENABLE`.
. Corner case all ones. Write all ones to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE`, write all ones to the defined bits of `INTR_TEST`, then read `INTR_STATUS` and `INTR_ENABLE`. Write 1 to the eight defined `INTR_STATUS` bits and read `INTR_STATUS`.
. One-bit control. Write all ones to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE`, write the single bit i to `INTR_TEST`, read `INTR_STATUS` and `INTR_ENABLE`, then write 1 to bit i of `INTR_STATUS` and read `INTR_STATUS`. The bit i is drawn from the set bits of the drawn test mask T that the two next steps write, so that the same bit is the control of those steps.
. Drawn enable mask. This step runs twice: with the drawn `INTR_ENABLE` mask E and test mask T, then with the complement of E over the eight defined bits and all ones on the eight defined bits of `INTR_TEST`, so that every source has its enable bit clear under a test write in every leaf. Write the enable mask of the run, write the test mask of the run, and read `INTR_STATUS` and `INTR_ENABLE`. `INTR_ENABLE` is graded as the stored value of the run. Log `INTR_STATUS` as `OBS-STATUS-E` and vector bit 15 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` as `OBS-pic16`. Write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_STATUS` and read `INTR_STATUS`; the eight defined bits read 0.
. Drawn test mask. Write all ones to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE`, write T to `INTR_TEST`, and read `INTR_STATUS` and `INTR_ENABLE`.
. Write 1 to the drawn clear subset C (it holds bits 8 and 12 and is a proper subset of the set bits of T) in `INTR_STATUS`, then read `INTR_STATUS` and `INTR_ENABLE`.
. Write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_STATUS` and read `INTR_STATUS`.
. Restore leg. For every unlocked read-write field outside `CTRL`, in seeded order, write its value v1. Read each field back and compare it with v1.
. Run the reset sequence, then read every field of the restore leg again and compare it with its RDL reset value.
. VCS only: check each of these readbacks for X.
. `MODULE_ENABLE` trials. Run the locked trial and the unlocked trial (control) in drawn order. Each trial runs the steps below.
.. Run the reset sequence, then the clean start (common rule "Clean start"; the window stays 2048), and poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). The `balanced` pattern stays driven to the end of the trial.
.. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0. Then count the strobes of `esrc_compress_vld_o` up to the 24th, and pop `FIFO_RDATA` whenever `LEVEL` reads above 0, so that the FIFO does not fill. Log the clocks from the end of the drain to the 24th strobe as t_ref. With whitening on (the reset value) a digest gives 8 stream words (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening), so 24 strobes span at least two whole digest periods for every start phase.
.. Locked trial only. Write `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` = 1 and read it back as 1. The window size is at its reset value 2048, which is at least 1024. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0.
.. Locked trial only. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, then poll `LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait of 2 t_ref; a timeout fails the check). Read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` after `LEVEL` has risen.
.. Unlocked trial only. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0, write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, and poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
.. Unlocked trial only. Poll `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` until it reads 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check), so that the digest that is in flight has left the conditioner. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0, then poll `LEVEL` for 2 t_ref.
. Log the plan and every check line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-ESRC-RO
|A write of the complement of the value read to a read-only ESRC register leaves its RDL field bits unchanged (`entropy_source.adoc`, access R). Reserved bits have no stated read value; they are logged as `OBS-RO-RSVD` and not compared. `COMPONENT_ID` is one of these registers; its reset value is graded by `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, and this check grades only that the write does not change it. A DUT that stores the write shows a changed read. Only a register that reads the same twice in the quiet state is written. The specification states no response code for the write; OKAY and SLVERR are both accepted and logged. Anchor: readback after the write. Control: at least one register is written; `COMPONENT_ID` is always written. Evidence: one line per written register, `CHK-ESRC-RO PASS seed=<n> reg=<R> wrote=0x<w> read=0x<r> unchanged=1 written_regs=<k>`, and `OBS-RO-RESP seed=<n> reg=<R> resp=<okay_or_slverr>`.
|CHK-ESRC-RESTORE
|After a TRNG reset every unlocked read-write field outside `CTRL` reads its RDL reset value (`architecture.adoc`, Reset: the TRNG reset is a full-IP reset). Before the reset each field read v1, which differs from the reset value, so a reset that leaves a field alone fails. In VCS no readback has X; in Verilator the X check is not run. The hardware-updating registers are not compared. Anchor: readback of each field. Control: each field read back v1 before the reset. Evidence: `CHK-ESRC-RESTORE PASS seed=<n> fields=<k> pre=v1 post=reset mismatches=0 x_free=<1_or_na>`.
|CHK-LOCK-MEN
|After `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` = 1, a write of `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0 is ignored (`entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL`): `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` reads 0 and, after a drain before the write, `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` rises above 0 within the bounded wait of 2 t_ref. A running module writes a digest within one digest period of the drain, and t_ref covers at least two digest periods, so the bound holds for every digest phase. A DUT that honours the write shows IDLE = 1 and an empty FIFO. Anchor: `MAIN_SM_STATUS` and `FIFO_STATUS` readback. Control: in the unlocked trial of the same seed the same write gives IDLE = 1, and the FIFO stays empty for 2 t_ref after `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` reads 0 and a drain (`entropy_source.adoc`, `SHA256_STATUS`: BUSY is set while SHA-256 processing or digest output is active, so a digest in flight cannot refill the FIFO in the hold); the leaf logs t_ref, the BUSY poll and the polls. Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-MEN PASS seed=<n> t_ref=<t> idle_locked=0 level_after_drain=<l> ctrl_idle_unlocked=1 ctrl_level_after_drain=0`.
|CHK-ESRC-INTR
|With all eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` set, after a write to `INTR_TEST` the eight defined bits of `INTR_STATUS` equal the test mask exactly; `INTR_ENABLE` reads back as written, before and after the clear, for the all-ones mask and for the drawn mask E (the drawn-mask status is logged and not graded, because the cited text does not state the latch with an enable bit clear); a write of 1 to the bits of a proper subset clears those bits only and leaves the other set bits (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS`, `INTR_ENABLE`, `INTR_TEST`). In the quiet state, with `INTR_ENABLE` = 0 and 0 written to `INTR_TEST` (the all-zeros corner), the eight defined bits of `INTR_STATUS` read 0: the module is off and no source has an event, so this cell does not rest on the unstated latch rule. A DUT with a bit that does not set, a bit that aliases another, a bit that sets with no event, or a clear that takes extra bits fails the exact compare. Reserved bits are written 0 and are not compared. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` and `INTR_ENABLE` readback. Control: the one-bit control of the same seed reads exactly its bit; it is not a graded cell. Evidence: `CHK-ESRC-INTR PASS seed=<n> zero_corner_status=0x0 test=0x<t> status=0x<s> enable_rb=0x<e> drawn_enable=0x<E> drawn_enable_rb=0x<d> after_w1c=0x<c> ctrl_single_bit=0x<i> OBS-pic16=<p>` and `OBS-STATUS-E seed=<n> status=0x<s>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage. The open owners keep the rest: `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test` keeps the stored values and the power-on reset values, including the `COMPONENT_ID` value; `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test` keeps the lock behaviour of the locked list and `CTRL.RSVD0`; `sep_trng_reset_recovery_test` keeps the clear of `FIPS_LOCK` by the TRNG reset; `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test` keeps the set, the clear and the PIC line of each ESRC `INTR_STATUS` source alone. Not claimed: the read value of reserved bits beyond `CTRL.RSVD0`; the effect of the ring-oscillator and sample-clock fields (no observation point); the OR of the ESRC status bits onto interrupt source 16 (the leaf logs vector bit 15 and does not grade it). The data registers, `INTR_TEST`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH`, `FIPS_LOCK` and the hardware-updating registers are not in the walk.

'''''

[[sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test]]
===== 1.41 `sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS. The pattern leg runs two trials of three windows each.

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` per lane with the modes `balanced`, `bias70`, `bias30`, `square`, `alternate`, `stuck0` and `stuck1` (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"). Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i`; `esrc_decor_valid_o` (the word count of a window); `esrc_decor_bytes_o` (the lane-byte control); `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` (the whitener input words and the stream words); `esrc_compress_data_o`; `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset). No PROPOSED item.

*Randomization*: RANDCFG. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the lane of the bias70 trial and of the bias30 trial (0 to 11, two different lanes), the window of each of the two trials (two different windows from 1024, 2048 and 4096), the order of the two pattern trials, the order of the three limit trials, the half-period L of the repetition and Markov low trials (8, 16 or 32), the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern, the one positions of the bias patterns, the phases of the `square` lanes and the start values of the `alternate` lanes. The limits follow from the window and from L as the procedure states. `reseed = 4` samples different lanes, windows, trial orders, half-periods and patterns; the plan shape (one bias70 trial, one bias30 trial, the enable legs, three limit trials, the constant-zero and constant-ones legs) is the same on every seed. The lane and the window of a pattern trial are seed draws, so their bins close on a merge of regressions (common rule "Drawn cells").

*Objective*: The adaptive-proportion test runs per bit lane on each generator byte stream and is enabled by its bit of `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE`. Disabling a tester clears its state. A failing window of a generator byte stream latches its `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` bit. The window size and the limits are one register set that every generator shares. Write-one clears the status. With whitening on, the whitener turns each 16 BIW words of a constant-zero 32-bit stream into 8 digest words, also after the gating test fails. A limit beyond the exact statistics of a `square` or `alternate` stream trips its tester alone in every generator: `REPETITION_LIMIT` below the run length of a `square` stream trips the repetition test (status bit 0), `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` below the pair alternation count of an `alternate` stream trips the Markov high test (bit 4) and `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` above the alternation count of a `square` stream trips the Markov low test (bit 5). A constant-zero 32-bit stream (every lane stuck at 0 with BYPASS set; the BIW multiply-add of zero bytes is zero) sets the repetition, adaptive-proportion and Markov low bits of `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, their three total-failure counters and `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED`. A constant 32-bit stream with at least one lane at 1 (every lane stuck at 1 with BYPASS set, so every generator byte is 0xFF under the reset `ENTROPY_BYTE_MASK`, and the BIW output of twelve constant bytes is one constant word) has a one count of W on that lane in every window, above the reset `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` limit of 1200 for the reset window of 2048, so the 32-bit adaptive-proportion high test fails: `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 3 sets and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` counts. The 32-bit test sees the post-BIW stream while the compressor is in use (`CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 0). Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`, `CTRL`, `ENTROPY_BYTE_MASK`, `HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE`, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT`, `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT`, `APT_PROPORTION_LO`, `MARKOV_TEST_PROB_THRESHOLDS`, `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS`, `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `INTR_STATUS`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Health Testing, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing); `hw/sys/sep/doc/trng.adoc` (Entropy Generation).

*Test Coverage*: Two bias patterns on every seed (bias70 trips the high adaptive-proportion tester, bias30 the low one), each on a drawn lane with a drawn window from 1024, 2048 and 4096, with the other 11 lanes `balanced`, the repetition and Markov testers on their pass side and the other 11 generators at 0; the enable cells (adaptive-proportion bit cleared, repetition bit cleared while its count is non-zero); the write-one clear; the limit trips of the repetition, Markov high and Markov low testers in all 12 generators; the constant-zero 32-bit stream with its adaptive-proportion low and Markov low status bits, the two non-zero low-side counters and `HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` (status bit 0 and `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` are logged as control values, and the Markov high bit and the two high-side counters are logged, all not graded); the constant all-ones stream with the 32-bit adaptive-proportion high trip (status bit 3 and a non-zero `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`), with the zero `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` of the constant-zero stream as its control; and the word count of the whitener on the constant-zero stream. This test extends `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test`, which drives a stuck pattern on all lanes with one threshold and one window and grades the repetition failure, the attribution of `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` and the generator status. This test grades only the cells that the open test does not grade: the adaptive-proportion tester alone on one biased lane, the pass side of the other testers under shared limits, the enable and clear legs, the limit trips, the adaptive-proportion and Markov low bits and counters of the constant-zero pattern (not bit 0, not `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` and not the high-side cells), the adaptive-proportion high trip of the constant all-ones pattern, and the word count of the whitener on the constant-zero stream. The open owner keeps the rest. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_startup_health_cg` (`x_enable_boot`, `cp_test_enable_set`, `cp_window_class`, `x_tester_side`, `cp_test_level`, `cp_disable_clears`, `cp_prestage_test`, `cp_top_failure_set`), `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`x_gen_latch`), `sep_esrc_noise_gen_cg` (`cp_lane_pattern`) and `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path` hw bins).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed: for the bias70 trial and the bias30 trial a lane (0 to 11, two different lanes) and a window W (two different windows from 1024, 2048 and 4096), the order of the two pattern trials, the order of the three limit trials, the half-period L and the patterns (common rule "Exact patterns"). Log the plan.
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf).
. Define the start sequence for a window W: run the reset sequence; write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status"); run the clean start with the window W (common rule "Clean start"), with `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 0, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 7 and the settings of the leg among its writes; poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf); read the 12 `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers and require all of them to read 0.
. Define the window count: one window is W strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`. The leaf counts strobes and uses no absolute clock count. In every graded window the leaf runs the lane-byte control of the common rule "Noise injection".
. Pattern trials. Run the two trials in drawn order. Lane k and window W of a trial are the lane and the window that the plan draws for it.
.. Run the start sequence with the window W.
.. Write the trip limit of the trial. Trial bias70: `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` = 19W/32. Trial bias30: `APT_PROPORTION_LO` = 13W/32. The other limits keep their clean values.
.. Error injection. Drive lane k with the trial pattern through `esrc_noise_ext_i` (bias70: 45W/64 ones per window; bias30: 19W/64), and keep `balanced` on the other 11 lanes (W/2 ones per window). Expected response: the adaptive-proportion status bit of lane k, and no other bit of 0, 3, 4 and 5.
.. Count three whole windows (3W strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`) from the start of the pattern.
.. Read the 12 `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers.
.. Read `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` again, write 1 to the bits that it reads as set, and read it a third time.
. Enable leg, adaptive-proportion tester. Repeat the first trial of the drawn order, with its window, trip limit and lane, and with `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 5 (bit 1 clear) written after the start sequence and before the pattern starts. Count three whole windows and read `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`.
. Enable leg, repetition tester. Run the start sequence with the window 2048. Drive stuck1 on all 12 lanes, so every lane of the 32-bit stream is constant and its repetition count rises from the first word. Read `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT` repeatedly (a bounded number of reads within the first 128 strobes of the pattern, below `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200) until it reads non-zero; if it never does, log `CTRL-MISSING` and fail the leaf.
. Write `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 6 (bit 0 clear), count one whole window, and read `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT`.
. Limit trials. Run the three trials in drawn order: repetition, Markov high and Markov low. Every trial uses W = 1024. The trial pattern is `square` L with the half-period L of the plan for the repetition and Markov low trials, and `alternate` for the Markov high trial (convention "Health-test limit trips").
.. Run the start sequence with W = 1024. Drive the trial pattern on all 12 lanes. Count two whole windows, read the 12 `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers and require all of them to read 0 (the clean limits hold).
.. Write the failing-side limit of the trial. Repetition: `REPETITION_LIMIT` = L - 3. Markov high: `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` = W/2 - 3. Markov low: `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` = W/L + 3. Under `square` L every lane has the longest run L and at most W/L alternations per window under both readings; under `alternate` every lane has W/2 pair alternations (W - 1 or W changes from word to word) per window. So the limit lies at least three counts beyond the statistic of every generator. The other limits keep their clean values.
.. Count at least two whole windows after the write, with the trial pattern on all lanes.
.. Read the 12 `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers.
.. Write 1 to each status register that reads non-zero (clean-up, not graded).
. Constant-zero leg. Run the start sequence with the window 2048 and `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 1 (its reset value).
. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` and `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED`. They read 0.
. Write the reset values of `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT`, `APT_PROPORTION_LO`, `MARKOV_TEST_PROB_THRESHOLDS` and `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.REPETITION_LIMIT`, so the leg runs at the reset limits of the window 2048.
. Error injection. Drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes through `esrc_noise_ext_i`. Expected response: the bits and counters of the objective.
. Count three whole windows of the reset size 2048 from the start of the stuck pattern.
. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, the five total-failure counters and `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` again.
. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0. Then count the strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o` and of `esrc_compress_vld_o` until the push count reaches 64 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails CHK-HT-PRESHA). Pop the FIFO words and log them as `OBS-HT-PRESHA-WORDS`.
. Constant-ones leg. Run the start sequence with the window 2048, `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 0 and `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 0, so the FIFO carries the post-BIW words. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`. They read 0. Write the reset values of the limits as in the constant-zero leg.
. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0.
. Error injection. Drive stuck1 on all 12 lanes through `esrc_noise_ext_i`. Expected response: the adaptive-proportion high trip of the objective.
. Count three whole windows of the reset size 2048 from the start of the stuck pattern.
. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` again. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0, poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 4 (a bounded wait of one window of 2048 strobes; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf), and pop four FIFO words. These words enter the FIFO after three windows of the stuck pattern, so none of them is older than the stuck drive (a full FIFO discards newer writes, so the words held at the end of the windows can be older). Precondition: the four words are equal and non-zero (the stream is constant and has a lane at 1); otherwise the trip cannot be predicted from the stimulus, and the leaf logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails. The FIFO words gate the check and are not its expected value.
. Log every check line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-HT-TRIP
|After three whole windows of a bias pattern on lane k, among the status bits 0, 3, 4 and 5 of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` exactly bit 3 is set: the adaptive-proportion tester of the generator latches its status (`architecture.adoc`, Health Testing: bit 3 is the adaptive-proportion failure). The high and low adaptive-proportion testers share bit 3, so the bit of the sibling is not graded. The count is exact in every window (common rule "Exact patterns") and lies 7W/64 beyond the trip limit (19W/32 for bias70, 13W/32 for bias30). A DUT with no adaptive-proportion test per lane, or with the limit applied to another lane, shows bit 3 clear. Anchor: `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` readback. Evidence: `CHK-HT-TRIP PASS seed=<n> pattern=<bias70_or_bias30> lane=<k> window=<W> tester=<apt_hi_or_apt_lo> limit=<L> stat=<s> gen_status=0x<g>`; `stat` is the one count of lane k per window (45W/64 or 19W/64), computed from the drive and not read from the DUT.
|CHK-HT-PASS
|The repetition and Markov testers stay on their pass side: bits 0, 4 and 5 of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` read 0 after the windows are completed (the leaf logs the window count). Their limits keep the clean values, which no window of the trial reaches (common rule "Clean start": no run of the trial patterns is longer than 90 words against `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200, a window holds at most W alternations against `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` = W + 1, and every count meets `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` = 0). A DUT whose testers trip on a lane that has no failing statistic, or that compares a limit on the wrong side, shows one of these bits. Anchor: status readback. Control: the adaptive-proportion trip of the same trial. Evidence: `CHK-HT-PASS PASS seed=<n> pattern=<p> lane=<k> tester=<rep_or_mkv_hi_or_mkv_lo> limit=<L> gen_bit=0 windows=<w>`.
|CHK-HT-OTHERS
|The status of every other generator reads 0 under the shared limits (`architecture.adoc`, Health Testing: the thresholds, the enable bits and the window sizes are programmable through the CSR interface and the 12 `entropy_generator` instances run the 8-bit test; `entropy_source.adoc`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`, for the enable bits only). The other 11 lanes carry `balanced`, W/2 ones in every window, 6W/64 on the pass side of the trip limit; the leaf logs both values. A DUT with limits that are not shared, or a latch that leaks to other generators, shows a non-zero status. Anchor: readback of the 11 other `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers. Control: lane k latches in the same trial. Evidence: `CHK-HT-OTHERS PASS seed=<n> lane=<k> other_generators_status=0x000 other_ones=<W/2> limit=<L>`.
|CHK-HT-ENABLE
|With `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` bit 1 clear the same pattern latches no bit 3 on lane k. With bit 0 clear while `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT` reads non-zero, the count reads 0 after one whole window (`entropy_source.adoc`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`: disabling a test clears its state). A DUT that ignores the enable bit latches bit 3, or keeps a stale count. Anchor: `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` and `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT` readback. Control: for the adaptive-proportion cell, the same trial trips with the bit set in the pattern trials of the same seed; for the repetition cell, the count read non-zero before the write, and the repetition tester trips with its bit set in the repetition limit trial of the same seed. Evidence: `CHK-HT-ENABLE PASS seed=<n> tester=<apt_or_rep> enable_bit=0 latched=<0_or_na> count_before=<c_or_na> count_after_disable=<0_or_na> ctrl_enabled_trips=1`.
|CHK-HT-W1C
|A write of 1 to the set bits of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` clears them (`entropy_source.adoc`, access RW1C). A DUT that does not clear shows the bits set on the third read. Anchor: readback of the register before and after the write. Control: the read before the write shows the bits set. Evidence: `CHK-HT-W1C PASS seed=<n> before=0x<a> after=0x0`.
|CHK-HT-PRESHA
|With stuck0 on all lanes, BYPASS on all lanes, whitening on and the gating test failing, the whitener keeps conditioning the constant-zero stream: over 64 whitener input words (strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o`) the stream port carries 32 words (strobes of `esrc_compress_vld_o`) within ±8 (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening: 16 input words give 8 digest words, and with the whitener disabled the BIW words pass unchanged; `entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE`). A DUT that passes the constant words to the FIFO unconditioned gives as many stream words as pushes, and a DUT that stops the conditioning after the gating test fails gives no pushes, so the bounded wait fails the check. The tolerance is one digest, because a digest can be in flight at each end of the count. The digest values are not graded: the specification does not state the block chaining, the padding, or the word and byte order of the whitener (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), and the leaf logs the words as `OBS-HT-PRESHA-WORDS`. Anchor: the strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o`. Control: `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 0 is 1 and `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` is above 0 in the same run, so the gating test saw the constant stream (the open owner grades that failure). Evidence: `CHK-HT-PRESHA PASS seed=<n> pushes=64 stream_words=<s> expect=32 tol=8 ctrl_status_bit0=1 ctrl_repcnt_total=<f>`.
|CHK-HT-LIMIT
|In each limit trial the status of every generator holds exactly the bit of the tripped tester (bit 0, 4 or 5 for repetition, Markov high and Markov low) and none of the other bits 0, 3, 4 and 5 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`, `MARKOV_TEST_PROB_THRESHOLDS`: `REPETITION_LIMIT` is the maximum run, `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` the maximum and `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` the minimum alternation count). In the repetition and Markov low trials every lane carries `square` L, so its longest run is L and a window holds at most W/L alternations; in the Markov high trial every lane carries `alternate`, so a window holds W/2 pair alternations whatever the phase of the lane (`architecture.adoc`, Health Testing: the Markov test counts bit-pair transitions). The failing limit lies at least three counts beyond under both readings of the count (convention "Health-test limit trips"), and the other limits keep their clean values. A DUT that ignores a limit, swaps the two Markov sides, or trips another tester fails. Anchor: `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` readback of all 12 generators and the word count at `esrc_decor_valid_o`. Control: in the other two trials of the seed the same bit reads 0 over the same count of windows, and the 12 statuses read 0 after two whole windows of the pattern and before the failing limit is written. Evidence: `CHK-HT-LIMIT PASS seed=<n> tester=<rep_or_mkv_hi_or_mkv_lo> pattern=<square_L_or_alternate> limit=<lim> window=<W> words=<w> gen_status_all=0x<s> other_bits=0`.
|CHK-HT-TOP-ZERO
|After three constant-zero windows, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bits 3 and 5 are 1; `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` and `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` are non-zero; `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` is 1. A constant-zero stream has no ones and no alternations, so it fails the low adaptive-proportion test and the low Markov test (`entropy_source.adoc`: `APT_PROPORTION_LO` 0x350, `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` 0x64). A DUT that misses a low-side test fails. Status bit 0 and `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` are logged as control values and are not graded here; the repetition failure of a stuck pattern is graded by the open owner. Status bit 4, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` and `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` are logged as `OBS-HT-TOP-HI` and are not graded here; the zero `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` is the control of CHK-HT-TOP-ONE. Anchor: readback of the registers. Control: the same registers read 0 after the clean startup and before the constant phase. Evidence: `CHK-HT-TOP-ZERO PASS seed=<n> status=0x<s> totals=<rep_apt_lo_mkv_lo> intr_health_failed=1 ctrl_before=0x0`, and `OBS-HT-TOP-HI seed=<n> status_bit4=<b> apt_hi_total=<a> mkv_hi_total=<m>`; the `status` bit 0 and the `rep` total are logged and not graded.
|CHK-HT-TOP-ONE
|After three constant all-ones windows, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 3 is 1 and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` is non-zero. A constant word with a lane at 1 has a one count of 2048 on that lane in each window of the reset size, above the reset `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` limit of 1200 (`entropy_source.adoc`: `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` is the maximum one count per lane; `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` counts the APT high-threshold failures). A DUT whose 32-bit high-side tester is absent, disconnected or compares the wrong side fails. The Markov high bit is not graded: a constant stream has no alternations. Anchor: readback of the registers. Control: the same registers read 0 after the clean startup and before the constant phase, and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` reads 0 after the constant-zero leg of the same seed, so a counter that counts every failure, or the low side, fails. Evidence: `CHK-HT-TOP-ONE PASS seed=<n> fifo_word=0x<w> ones_lanes=<k> window=2048 apt_hi_limit=1200 status=0x<s> apt_hi_total=<a> ctrl_before=0x0 ctrl_zero_leg_apt_hi=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage; `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test` keeps the repetition failure of a constant pattern on all lanes (`CHK-HT-STATUS`, `CHK-FAIL-ATTRIB`, `CHK-GEN-HEALTH`), and `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` keeps the clear of the total-failure counters by a `MODULE_ENABLE` rise. Not claimed: the meaning of status bits 1, 2, 6 and 7; the split of the shared bit 3 into high and low; the position of the 32-bit test before the SHA-256 whitener (`trng.adoc`, Entropy Generation), because the FIFO carries the whitened words wherever the test sits, and the specification does not state the block chaining, the padding or the word order of the whitener, so the whitened words of a constant input are not predictable (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); an absolute time to a trip (the unit of the byte period is blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); the total-failure counters under a generator-level trip (the instance that counts is not stated); the Markov high trip of the 32-bit instance, and its adaptive-proportion high trip for a pattern other than a constant word (the 32-bit statistic of such a pattern is not stated); the exact BIW word of the all-ones stream (the leaf requires only a constant non-zero word); the zero of the 32-bit Markov high bit and counter under the constant-zero stream (logged only).

'''''

[[sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test]]
===== 1.42 `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` per lane with the modes `balanced` and `stuck0` (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"). Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i`; `esrc_decor_valid_o` (the window count); `esrc_decor_bytes_o` (the lane-byte control); `esrc_compress_vld_o` (the stream tap); `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (logged, not graded); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset). No PROPOSED item.

*Randomization*: RANDCFG. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the drawn threshold T_d (4, 5 or 7), the order of the thresholds 0, 3 and T_d, the window of each threshold (256 or 512), the clean-gap length between two bursts (2, 3 or 4 whole windows), the order of the two trips of a threshold (the trip that ends by a `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle first or the trip that ends by a TRNG reset first), and the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern. `reseed = 4` samples different drawn thresholds, orders, windows, gaps and patterns. Every seed runs the thresholds 0 and 3 and every leg. The drawn threshold is a seed draw, so the bin of threshold 5 closes on a merge of regressions.

*Objective*: `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` counts consecutive failing windows. When it reaches `ALERT_THRESHOLD` (reset 4; 0 disables) the main state machine enters AlertHang, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` set and the entropy output halts. AlertHang lasts until a reset or until `MODULE_ENABLE` is cleared. Write-one clears the sticky status bits. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Persistent Failure and AlertHang); `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`INTR_STATUS`, `MAIN_SM_STATUS`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`, `ALERT_THRESHOLD`).

*Test Coverage*: Thresholds 0 and 3 and one drawn threshold from 4, 5 and 7, each with a window of 256 or 512 and the clean limits of that window: the reset value after a TRNG reset, the trip, the no-trip, the non-consecutive burst, the disabled comparator, the output halt, the halt that survives the write-one clear, the exit by a `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle and the exit by a TRNG reset. This test extends `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test`, which fixes the threshold at 3 and grades the trip, `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` and the write-one clear after `MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, which reads the power-on value of `ALERT_THRESHOLD`. This test grades only the cells that those tests do not grade; the open owners keep the rest. The trip at threshold 3 is a control. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`cp_alert_threshold`, `cp_any_fail`, `cp_alert_hang`), `sep_esrc_startup_health_cg` (`x_enable_boot`, `cp_window_class` below 1024) and `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path` hw bins).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed: the drawn threshold T_d (4, 5 or 7), the order of the thresholds 0, 3 and T_d, the window of each threshold, the gap length, and the order of the two trips of each threshold. Log the plan.
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf).
. Define the window count: one window is W strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`. Clean noise is `balanced` on all 12 lanes. A burst of n whole windows is n times W strobes with the noise of the burst, then one break word (every lane at 1 for one strobe), then clean noise. The break word ends the zero run of every lane at the last stuck word, so no repetition count stays above `REPETITION_LIMIT` into the clean noise, and the stuck words of a burst touch only the windows that hold them. In every window the leaf runs the lane-byte control of the common rule "Noise injection".
. Define the limits: the clean limits of the window W (common rule "Clean start"). No window of clean noise reaches them, and every whole window of stuck0 fails them (0 ones against `APT_PROPORTION_LO` = W/4). The leaf logs the values.
. Define the start sequence for a threshold T and a window W: run the reset sequence; run the clean start with the window W, with `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = T and `INTR_ENABLE.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` = 1 among its writes (common rule "Clean start"); poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 and `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf).
. Reset-value leg. Run the reset sequence, write `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 7 and read it back as 7.
. Run the reset sequence again and read `ALERT_THRESHOLD`.
. Run the threshold blocks in drawn order. The thresholds 3 and T_d run the block "T at least 3". The threshold 0 runs the block "T = 0".
. Block "T at least 3". Run the start sequence for T and the drawn window W.
. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0. Then count the strobes of `esrc_compress_vld_o` up to the 24th (a bounded wait; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf), and pop `FIFO_RDATA` whenever `LEVEL` reads above 0, so that the FIFO does not fill. Log the clocks from the end of the drain to the 24th strobe as t_ref. With whitening on (the reset value) a digest gives 8 stream words (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening), so t_ref spans at least two whole digest periods for every start phase. The hold H of the later legs is t_ref.
. No-trip leg. Error injection. Drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes for a burst of T - 2 whole windows, then clean noise. Expected response: no alert. Poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` during the burst and after it, and log the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT`.
. Poll `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` until it reads 0, for at most two whole windows of clean noise after the burst (a timeout fails CHK-PF-NOTRIP).
. Consecutive leg. Error injection. Drive stuck0 for a burst of T - 2 whole windows, clean noise for a gap of g whole windows (g drawn, 2 to 4), then stuck0 for a second burst of T - 2 whole windows, then clean noise. Expected response: no alert. Poll `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` throughout and after the second burst.
. Poll `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` until it reads 0, for at most two whole windows of clean noise after the second burst (a timeout fails CHK-PF-CONSEC).
. Run trip E and trip R in the drawn order. If trip R runs first, run the start sequence again for T and W before trip E, because the TRNG reset restores the reset configuration.
. Trip E (ends by a `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle). Error injection. Drive stuck0 for a burst of T + 1 whole windows, polling `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` back to back. Expected response: AlertHang. The specification states no latency from the T-th failing window to `ALERT`, so after the burst keep stuck0 driven and keep polling `ALERT` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of a few whole windows; a timeout fails the check). At the first poll that reads `ALERT` = 1, read `ANY_FAIL_COUNT`.
. After `ALERT` reads 1, return to clean noise and read `ALERT`, `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` and vector bit 15 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (`OBS-pic16`).
. Halt leg of trip E. Poll `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` until it reads 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check); while `BUSY` reads 1, pop `FIFO_RDATA` between polls, so that a full FIFO cannot hold the digest. Then pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0. For the hold H, poll `LEVEL` back to back and watch `esrc_compress_vld_o`.
. Clear leg of trip E. Write 1 to `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and to `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE`, and read both.
. For the hold H, poll `LEVEL` back to back and watch `esrc_compress_vld_o`.
. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, then 1. Poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 and `LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
. Trip R (ends by a TRNG reset). Error injection. Drive stuck0 for a burst of T + 1 whole windows, then keep stuck0 driven and poll `ALERT` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of a few whole windows; a timeout fails the check), then return to clean noise. Expected response: AlertHang. Read `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE`.
. Run the reset sequence and read `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE`.
. Run the clean start with the window W (the TRNG reset restores the reset configuration). Poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 and `LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
. Equal case. Run the start sequence for T and W, drive stuck0 for a burst of exactly T whole windows, then clean noise, and read `ALERT`. Log it as `OBS-PF`.
. Block "T = 0". Run the start sequence for T = 0 and the drawn window W.
. Disable leg. Error injection. Drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes for 10 whole windows, polling `ANY_FAIL_COUNT`, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` back to back, then return to clean noise. Expected response: no alert.
. After the last threshold, log the `CHK-PF-TRIP` and `CHK-PF-DISABLE` lines, so that the trip at T = 3 is available as their control. Log the other check lines.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-PF-RESET
|`ALERT_THRESHOLD` reads 0x4 after a TRNG reset that follows a write of 7 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_THRESHOLD`: reset 0x4; `architecture.adoc`, Reset: full-IP reset). A DUT that keeps the written value shows 7. The power-on read is graded by `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`. Anchor: readback. Control: the register read 7 before the second reset. Evidence: `CHK-PF-RESET PASS seed=<n> alert_threshold=0x4`.
|CHK-PF-TRIP
|For the drawn threshold T = T_d (4, 5 or 7), after a burst of T + 1 whole windows and a bounded wait for `ALERT` (the specification states no latency from the T-th failing window to the alert), `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` are 1 and `ANY_FAIL_COUNT`, read at the first poll that shows `ALERT` = 1, is at or above T (`architecture.adoc`, Persistent Failure and AlertHang). A burst of T + 1 whole windows covers at least T complete failing windows whatever its start phase; the leaf keeps stuck0 driven until the alert bit reads 1. A DUT that uses a fixed threshold, or that does not set one of the two bits, fails. Anchor: `MAIN_SM_STATUS`, `INTR_STATUS` and `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS` readback. Control: the trip at T = 3 in the same seed. Evidence: `CHK-PF-TRIP PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> win=<W> failing_windows=<n> any_fail=<a> alert=1 pf=1 ctrl_thr3_trip=1`.
|CHK-PF-NOTRIP
|After a burst of T - 2 whole windows, `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` read 0 at every poll. The stuck words of a burst of T - 2 whole windows touch at most T - 1 windows, below the threshold; the break word ends every lane run at the last stuck word, so no repetition failure reaches the next window. After the burst, `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` reads 0 within two whole windows of clean noise (`entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`: the count holds consecutive failing windows; the first window after the burst can still hold stuck words, so the bound is two windows). A DUT that trips early, or that keeps the count after a clean window, fails. Anchor: readback. Control: the burst of T + 1 whole windows of the same threshold trips, and the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` during the burst is non-zero. Evidence: `CHK-PF-NOTRIP PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> failing_windows=<n> alert=0 pf=0 any_fail_peak=<a> any_fail_after=0`.
|CHK-PF-CONSEC
|Two bursts of T - 2 whole windows with a clean gap of 2 to 4 windows between them leave `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` at 0, because the counter holds consecutive failing windows only (`entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`). After the second burst, `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` reads 0 within two whole windows of clean noise. A DUT that accumulates across the gap trips, and a DUT that keeps the count after a clean window fails the read. Anchor: readback. Control: the burst of T + 1 whole windows in the same seed trips. Evidence: `CHK-PF-CONSEC PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> bursts=<b1>,<b2> alert=0 any_fail_after=0 ctrl_single_burst_alert=1`.
|CHK-PF-DISABLE
|With `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 0, ten stuck whole windows leave `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` at 0 at every poll while the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` rises above 8 (`architecture.adoc`: zero disables the comparator and the counter keeps counting; ten whole windows contain at least nine complete failing windows). A DUT that trips at 0, or whose counter does not count, fails. Anchor: readback. Control: T = 3 with the same pattern trips. Evidence: `CHK-PF-DISABLE PASS seed=<n> thr=0 failing_windows=<n> alert=0 any_fail=<a> ctrl_thr3_alert=1`.
|CHK-PF-HALT
|After a trip (`ALERT` read 1), a poll of `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` to 0 and a drain, with clean noise, `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` stays 0 and `esrc_compress_vld_o` is silent for the hold H (`architecture.adoc`: AlertHang halts entropy output; `entropy_source.adoc`, `SHA256_STATUS`: BUSY covers digest output in flight, so a digest in flight when the alert rises cannot refill the FIFO in the hold). A DUT that keeps the output running shows LEVEL rising or strobes on the tap. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS` readback and the stream tap. Control: the clean run before the trip shows 24 strobes on `esrc_compress_vld_o` within t_ref, and H is t_ref, at least two digest periods, so a running output shows a digest in the hold whatever its phase. Evidence: `CHK-PF-HALT PASS seed=<n> sha_busy_clear=1 level_max=0 stream_vld=0 hold_clk=<t_ref> ctrl_stream_vld=24`.
|CHK-PF-RECOVER
|After the write-one clear of `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` both read 0 and the output stays halted for the hold H. After `MODULE_ENABLE` goes 0 then 1, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1 and `LEVEL` rises. After a trip and a TRNG reset both bits read 0, and a clean startup gives `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 and a rising `LEVEL` (`architecture.adoc`: the write-one clear of the sticky bits; AlertHang lasts until a reset or until `MODULE_ENABLE` is cleared). A DUT that resumes the output after the clear alone, that stays in AlertHang after the exit, or that keeps the bits after a reset, fails. Anchor: readback and the stream tap.
