Control: both bits read 1 before the clear and before the reset; for the halt after the clear, the clean run before the trip shows LEVEL rising and strobes on `esrc_compress_vld_o`. Evidence: `CHK-PF-RECOVER PASS seed=<n> exit=<enable_cycle_or_trng_reset> after_w1c_level=<0_or_na> boot_done=1 level_rises=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage; `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test` keeps the trip at threshold 3 with its alert bookkeeping and the write-one clear after leaving AlertHang, and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test` keeps the power-on value of `ALERT_THRESHOLD`. Not claimed: the case of exactly T failing windows (the burst phase inside a window can add or drop one; the leaf logs it as `OBS-PF` only); the OR of the ESRC status bits onto interrupt source 16 (the leaf logs vector bit 15 and does not grade it).

'''''

[[sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test]]
===== 1.43 `sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, detune probe (observation tap), 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS.

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` per lane with the modes `balanced` and `stuck1` (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"). Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i`; `esrc_decor_valid_o` (the window count); `esrc_decor_bytes_o` (the lane-byte control); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset). The detune probe, a read-only probe on the per-lane ring-oscillator detune select sampled on every `clk_i`, is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list (CHK-TUNE-MANUAL, CHK-TUNE-IGNORED, CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE).

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the window (256, 512 or 2048), the non-zero `DETUNE` masks, the clean-gap length after a burst (2, 3 or 4 whole windows) and the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern; the lane k of the manual, off, ignored and three-burst legs (0 to 11). `reseed = 4` samples different windows, masks, gaps, lanes and patterns; the plan shape (the manual, off, ignored and three-burst legs) is the same on every seed. The lane is a seed draw, so the lane bins close on a merge of regressions (common rule "Drawn cells").

*Objective*: With `CTRL.AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 1, a lane that fails its health test is detuned automatically (the tune state machine toggles the lane between its two lengths on a rising edge of a health-test failure) and `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL` sets. With the bit clear, the lane detune follows `RING_OSC_TUNE.DETUNE` bit i. Other lanes do not change. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`CTRL`, `INTR_STATUS`, `RING_OSC_ENABLE`, `RING_OSC_TUNE`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Noise Generation).

*Test Coverage*: Manual detune masks 0 and two drawn non-zero masks with autotune off; a lane failure with autotune off; a drawn non-zero mask ignored with autotune on and clean noise; three failure bursts on the drawn lane k with autotune on (probe bit k changes, other probe bits stay, `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` sets, the lane status latches). This test extends `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`, which grades the stored readback of `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` and `DETUNE` before and after the lock. This test grades their effect. Only the new cells are graded here; the open owner keeps the stored readback. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`cp_autotune`), `sep_esrc_noise_gen_cg` (`cp_detune_mode`, `cp_detune_lane`) and `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path` hw bins).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed: lane k (0 to 11), the window W, the three clean-gap lengths, the non-zero masks m1 and m2, and the non-zero mask m3 that is not all ones (its 12-bit complement m3c is then non-zero). Log the plan.
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). Then write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status").
. Define the window count: one window is W strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`. Clean noise is `balanced` on all 12 lanes. A burst is one whole window (W strobes) of stuck1 on lane k, with `balanced` on the other lanes, then clean noise. In every window the leaf runs the lane-byte control of the common rule "Noise injection".
. Run the reset sequence.
. Run the clean start with the window W, with `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 0 and `CTRL.AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 0 among its writes (common rule "Clean start"). The leaf logs the clean limits. No window of clean noise reaches them, and a burst fails them on lane k (W ones against `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` = 3W/4, and a run of W words, at least 256, against `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200).
. Poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf).
. Manual leg. Write `RING_OSC_TUNE.DETUNE` = 0, read it back, and sample the detune probe.
. Write `DETUNE` = m1, read it back, and sample the detune probe.
. Write `DETUNE` = m2, read it back, and sample the detune probe.
. Off leg. Read `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL` and `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`. Both read 0. Start sampling the detune probe on every `clk_i`.
. Error injection. Drive stuck1 on lane k for one burst, with `balanced` on the other lanes. Expected response: the status of lane k latches and `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` stays 0.
. Return to clean noise for the first clean gap, then stop the probe sampling. Read `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` and `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`.
. Write 1 to the set bits of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`.
. Ignored leg. Write `CTRL.AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 1. Sample the detune probe as p0.
. Write `DETUNE` = m3 and wait one window with clean noise. Sample the detune probe.
. Write `DETUNE` = m3c and wait one window with clean noise. Sample the detune probe. At least one of the two masks differs from p0.
. Failure leg. Run three bursts; burst i (1 to 3) uses clean-gap length i. Before each burst, read `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` and `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` (both read 0) and start sampling the detune probe on every `clk_i`.
. Error injection, each burst. Drive stuck1 on lane k for one burst, with `balanced` on the other lanes. Expected response: the status of lane k latches, `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` sets and probe bit k changes.
. After each burst, return to clean noise for the clean gap, then stop the probe sampling. Count the changes of probe bit k as t_i and log them. Read `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` and `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`.
. Write 1 to the set bits of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` and to `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL`.
. Log every check line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-TUNE-MANUAL
|With autotune off the detune probe vector equals the written `DETUNE` mask for the masks 0, m1 and m2, and does not change on any clock during a failure of lane k and its clean gap (`architecture.adoc`, Noise Generation: the lane detune follows `RING_OSC_TUNE.DETUNE` bit i when `CTRL.AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` is clear). A DUT that ignores the mask, or that lets a failure move the detune with autotune off, fails. Anchor: the detune probe (an observation tap). Control for the no-change leg: in the failure leg of the same seed, with `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 1, the same failure of lane k changes probe bit k (CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE), and in the off leg `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` bit 0 latches (CHK-TUNE-OFF), so the failure is present and the probe can show a change. If either event is missing, the leaf logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-MANUAL PASS seed=<n> detune_reg=0x<m> probe=0x<m> after_fail=0x<m> ctrl_auto_toggle=1 ctrl_off_gen_bit0=1`.
|CHK-TUNE-IGNORED
|With autotune on and clean noise the probe keeps its value p0 and does not take either written mask, m3 or its complement m3c (`architecture.adoc`: with autotune set the lane detune follows the auto-tune state machine). The two masks differ, so at least one of them differs from p0 and would show in the probe. A DUT that ignores `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` follows that mask. Anchor: the detune probe (an observation tap). Control: the manual leg of the same seed follows the mask. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-IGNORED PASS seed=<n> detune_regs=0x<m3>,0x<m3c> p0=0x<p0> probe=0x<p>,0x<p> ctrl_manual_probe=0x<m>`.
|CHK-TUNE-AUTO
|On the failing lane k with autotune on, bit 0 of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` latches and `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL` is 1 in each of the three bursts (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL`; the stuck lane fails the repetition test with a run of W words against `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200). A DUT that does not raise `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` on a lane failure fails. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` and status readback. Control: both read 0 before each burst, and CHK-TUNE-OFF of the same seed leaves `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` at 0 for the same failure. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-AUTO PASS seed=<n> lane=<k> burst=<1_or_2_or_3> autotune_fail=1 gen_bit0=1`, one line for each of the three bursts.
|CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE
|In each autotune burst, the probe bit of the failing lane k changes at least once and every other probe bit is unchanged (`architecture.adoc`: the tune state machine toggles the lane on a rising edge of a health-test failure; other lanes do not change). A DUT that does not detune the failing lane, or that detunes another lane, fails. The number of toggles per burst is logged and not graded. Anchor: the detune probe (an observation tap), sampled on every `clk_i`. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE PASS seed=<n> lane=<k> burst=<1_or_2_or_3> changes_ge1=1 others_unchanged=1`, and `OBS-TUNE-TOGGLES seed=<n> lane=<k> toggles=<t1,t2,t3>`.
|CHK-TUNE-OFF
|With autotune off the same failure latches bit 0 of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` and leaves `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL` at 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL`: set while automatic detuning is enabled). A DUT that raises `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` with autotune off fails. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` and status readback. Control: CHK-TUNE-AUTO of the same seed sets `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` for the same failure, and both registers read 0 before the burst. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-OFF PASS seed=<n> lane=<k> gen_status=0x<g> autotune_fail=0 ctrl_auto_fail=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage; `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test` keeps the stored readback of `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` and `DETUNE` and their lock. Not claimed: the ring-oscillator lane lengths (no observation point); the number of toggles per failing window and whether a failure of one generator counts into `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` (blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>; the leaf logs the toggle count and grades the change of probe bit k only).

'''''

[[sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test]]
===== 1.44 `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` per lane with the modes `balanced` and `stuck0` (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"). Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i`; `esrc_decor_valid_o` (the strobe that paces the noise) and `esrc_decor_bytes_o` (the lane-byte control); `esrc_compress_vld_o` (the stream tap); `esrc_whiten_push_o` (the whitener input); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset). No PROPOSED item.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern, the order of the three toggle moments, the fall instant of each moment (a clock count inside the interval that the procedure states) and whether the BIW observe capture is on in the toggle legs. `reseed = 4` samples different patterns, orders, instants and observe settings; the plan shape (clean start, bad start, restart, idle and the three toggles) is the same on every seed. The BIW observe capture is on in the clean start and in the bad start on every seed, because their checks grade its level.

*Objective*: `MODULE_ENABLE` starts startup health testing. `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reports a passing startup window and permits output. Before it, the whitener input and the BIW observe FIFO receive nothing. Clearing `MODULE_ENABLE` holds the main state machine idle. A `MODULE_ENABLE` rising edge restarts startup testing and the total-failure counters. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`CTRL`, `MAIN_SM_STATUS`, `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source, Reset).

*Test Coverage*: A clean start; a start with stuck0 on all lanes from the first sample; the idle state after `MODULE_ENABLE` = 0; the restart of the total-failure counters by the rising edge; `MODULE_ENABLE` falls at three moments (inside the boot window, after boot, while the bad pattern runs), each followed by a rise with clean noise. This test extends `sep_esrc_ht_watermark_arming_test`, which arms the watermark on a `MODULE_ENABLE` edge. This test grades the boot gate with a failing startup window, the idle state and the counter reset. Only the new cells are graded here; the open owner keeps the watermark arming. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_startup_health_cg` (`x_enable_boot`, `cp_restart_edge`, `cp_startup_fail_hold`, `cp_toggle_moment`) and `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`cp_total_fails`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed: the pattern, the order of the three toggle moments, the fall instants and the BIW observe setting of the toggle legs. Log the plan.
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf).
. Define the start: run the reset sequence; write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0; write `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 0, `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF, the clean limits of the reset window 2048 (common rule "Clean start") and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` (1 in the clean and bad starts); apply the noise of the leg; write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 1. The time of this write is t0. With `balanced` on all lanes this is a clean start, and the startup window passes by construction; with stuck0 it fails (0 ones against `APT_PROPORTION_LO` = 512, and a run above `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200). In the startup window the leaf runs the lane-byte control of the common rule "Noise injection".
. Define a poll: read `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL`, then `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL`, then `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE`, back to back, in this order.
. Clean start. Run the start with `balanced` on all 12 lanes.
. Poll until `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1. At every poll, apply the rule: if either level reads above 0, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` of the same poll reads 1. Log t_boot, the time from t0 to the first poll that reads 1.
. Keep polling until both levels read above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). Log t_fill, the time from the first poll that reads `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 to the first poll that reads the main FIFO level above 0, and the maximum of each level. Watch `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` from t0 to the first poll that reads both levels above 0 and log the strobe count of each tap; a tap with no strobe logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf.
. Idle leg. Read `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` as the level before the write, then pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0.
. Read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` and require 0 (the module runs; a 1 logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0 and poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Log the clocks as `idle_clk`.
. Poll `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` until it reads 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check), then pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0. For a hold of 3 t_fill, poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` back to back and watch `esrc_compress_vld_o`.
. Bad start. Run the start with stuck0 on all 12 lanes from the first sample. Error injection: stuck-at noise through `esrc_noise_ext_i`. Expected response: no `BOOT_PHASE_DONE`, and no word through the gated paths.
. Poll for a hold of 3 t_boot after t0. At every poll, watch `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o`.
. At the end of the hold, read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`.
. Restart leg. Read the five total-failure counters (`REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`) and log them. `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` must read above 0; otherwise log `CTRL-MISSING` and fail the leaf.
. Return to `balanced` on all lanes. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0 and poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
. Read the five counters and log them as `OBS-` only.
. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 1 and read the five counters at once, as the first reads after the write response.
. Poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 3 t_boot; a timeout fails the check).
. Toggle legs. Run the three moments in drawn order. Every leg uses the start with `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` as drawn, and with `balanced` on all 12 lanes.
. Moment `boot`. Draw a fall instant between 0 and t_boot/2 after t0, so that the boot window is still open at every draw. The instant is not earlier than the first poll that reads `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` = 0. Poll until that instant, and require the last `MAIN_SM_STATUS` read to show `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 0 and `IDLE` = 0. Read the five counters, then write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0.
. Moment `after_boot`. Poll until `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1, then wait a drawn delay between 0 and t_boot. Read the five counters, then write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0.
. Moment `bad_pattern`. Poll until `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1. Error injection: drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes through `esrc_noise_ext_i`. Expected response: `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` rises above 0.
. Poll `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf), wait a drawn delay between 0 and t_boot while the pattern runs, read the five counters, then write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0.
. At the `after_boot` and `bad_pattern` moments, read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` with the five counters, before the write of `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, and require 0 (a 1 logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). After each fall, poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
. Return to `balanced` on all 12 lanes, write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 1, and poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 3 t_boot; a timeout fails the check).
. Read the five counters. Each counter that read non-zero before the fall reads 0.
. Log every check line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BOOT-CLEAN
|Whenever the BIW observe level or the main FIFO level reads above 0, the `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` read of the same poll (read after both levels) is 1: entropy reaches the BIW observe FIFO and the main FIFO only after the boot gate opens (`architecture.adoc`, Entropy Source: the boot gate qualifies the whitener input, the uncompressed-byte path and the BIW observe FIFO). A DUT that releases entropy before `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` shows a level above 0 with the bit at 0. Anchor: readback in poll order. Control: both levels rise above 0 after the gate opens, or `CTRL-MISSING`. Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-CLEAN PASS seed=<n> boot_done_clk=<t> fifo_gt0_implies_done=1 biw_obs_gt0_implies_done=1 fifo_level_max=<f> biw_obs_level_max=<b>`.
|CHK-BOOT-BAD
|With a failing startup window for a hold of 3 t_boot, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` is 0, both levels are 0, `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` are silent, and `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 0 is 1 (the 32-bit health test sees the ungated stream, `architecture.adoc`, Entropy Source). A DUT that passes words into the whitener, the BIW observe FIFO or the main FIFO before the boot gate opens, or that opens the gate on a failing window, fails. Anchor: readback and the two stream taps. Control: the clean start of the same seed raises both levels above 0 after the gate opens and shows strobes on `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o`, and the hold is 3 t_boot. Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-BAD PASS seed=<n> window_clk=<w> boot_done=0 fifo_level=0 biw_obs_level=0 whiten_push=0 stream_vld=0 ht_status_bit0=1 ctrl_fifo_level_max=<f> ctrl_biw_obs_level_max=<b> ctrl_whiten_push=<p> ctrl_stream_vld=<v>`.
|CHK-BOOT-IDLE
|After `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` is 1 and the FIFO takes no new word for the hold of 3 t_fill, which starts after `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` reads 0 and a drain (`entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL`: clearing the field holds the main state machine idle; `SHA256_STATUS`: BUSY covers digest output in flight). A DUT that keeps filling the FIFO after the write, or that does not reach IDLE, fails. Anchor: `MAIN_SM_STATUS` and `FIFO_STATUS` readback and the stream tap. Control: `IDLE` read 0 and the FIFO level read above 0 before the write, the clean start of the same seed shows strobes on `esrc_compress_vld_o`, and the hold is a multiple of the clean fill time of the same seed. Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-IDLE PASS seed=<n> idle_before=0 idle=1 idle_clk=<c> sha_busy_clear=1 level_before=<l> level_stable=1 stream_vld=0 ctrl_stream_vld=<v>`.
|CHK-BOOT-RESTART
|After `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 1, every total-failure counter that read non-zero before the fall reads 0 at once, and `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` returns to 1 (`architecture.adoc`, Reset; `entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL`: the rising edge restarts startup health testing and the total-failure counters). A DUT that keeps the counters or does not restart startup fails. Anchor: counter readback. Control: `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` read non-zero before, or `CTRL-MISSING`. Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-RESTART PASS seed=<n> boot_done_again=1 total_fails_before=<rep_apt_hi_apt_lo_mkv_hi_mkv_lo> rep_before_nonzero=1 nonzero_after_edge=0`.
|CHK-BOOT-TOGGLE
|At each drawn fall moment, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` becomes 1; after the rise with clean noise, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` returns to 1 and every counter that read non-zero before the fall reads 0. A DUT that stays out of IDLE inside the boot window or while the bad pattern runs, or that does not restart after the rise, fails. Anchor: readback. Control: at every moment `IDLE` read 0 before the fall, or `CTRL-MISSING`; at the bad-pattern moment `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` read non-zero before the fall, or `CTRL-MISSING`; at the boot moment the last `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` read before the fall is 0. Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-TOGGLE PASS seed=<n> moment=<boot_or_after_boot_or_bad_pattern> idle_before=0 idle_clk=<c> boot_done_again=1 rep_before=<r> nonzero_after_rise=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage; `sep_esrc_ht_watermark_arming_test` keeps the watermark arming on a `MODULE_ENABLE` edge. Not claimed: the high-side adaptive-proportion and Markov counters, which read 0 under stuck0; an absolute boot time (every bound is a multiple of the clean time of the same seed).

'''''
[[sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 1.45 `sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (ESRC)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the ESRC registers (approved); the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i` (approved); the probes `esrc_decor_bytes_o`, `esrc_decor_valid_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` (approved); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). The ESRC SHA-256 status tap, a read-only tap on the next value of the `SHA256_STATUS` fields sampled on every `clk_i`, is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list (CHK-COND-SHA-COUNT). No inject beyond the noise modes. Expected values come from the rules of the specification only: a lane byte from the constant noise bit and the mask, a word population count from the lane noise and the mask, a count ratio from `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE`.

*Randomization*: RANDCFG. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the legs, the partial mask M (some bits 0 and some bits 1), the drawn rate N_r (2 to 15), the lane noise mask Z (12 bits, at least one 1 and one 0) and its polarity (Z or its complement). `reseed = 4` samples different masks, rates, noise masks and leg orders. The plan shape is the same on every seed: every seed walks the four (`BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR`, `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE`) cells and the rates 0, 1, N_r and 1023. The leaf logs the plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: `DECORRELATOR_MASK` ANDs every decorrelated byte. With `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` set for a lane, a constant noise bit gives a constant byte. With `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` set, the twelve bytes reach the FIFO as three 32-bit words, every (N+1)th decorrelator output when `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N. With `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 1, 16 BIW words give 8 digest words, and `SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT` reports 8 and counts down to 0. With it clear, the BIW words reach the FIFO unchanged. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`CTRL`, `SHA256_STATUS`, `DECORRELATOR_CTRL`, `DECORRELATOR_MASK`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing, SHA-256 Whitening).

*Test Coverage*: The four (`BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR`, `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE`) cells on every seed. The reset mask 0xFF and a drawn partial mask, with constant lane noise and BYPASS on all lanes. `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` 0, 1, a drawn rate 2 to 15 and 1023, each on every seed, with the compressor bypassed, and the rate with the compressor on as the control. Stuck0 on all lanes with BYPASS on all lanes, with the compressor bypassed and not bypassed, with `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` logged only (the 32-bit health test in bypass is blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). Extends `sep_esrc_e2e_smoke_test`, which grades the default path (decorrelator, BIW, SHA-256, seed, DRBG) with one configuration, and `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`, which grades the stored value of the byte mask, the compressor bypass, `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` and `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` and their lock. This test grades only their effect on the data path; the owners of the open tests keep the default path, the stored values and the lock. Functional coverage: `sep_esrc_conditioning_cg` (`cp_byte_mask`, `x_path_matrix`, `cp_downsample_rate`, `cp_sha_counts`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set-up, run at the start of each leg and at the start of each trial named in a leg. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). Write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status"). Write `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0, so that no persistent alert halts the stream. Run the clean start (common rule "Clean start"), which leaves `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and `balanced` on all twelve lanes. Wait for `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). Steps 2 to 26 form five legs (mask, bypass words, downsample, health test, whitener and pass-through). The legs run in the drawn order.
. Mask leg. Run the set-up. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and `DECORRELATOR_MASK` = 0xFF (the reset value).
. Drive lane j with the constant noise bit Z[j] (stuck1 or stuck0), with Z in the drawn polarity. Discard every strobe of `esrc_decor_valid_o` until at least 29 `clk_i` cycles (the 29-stage chain shifts on every `clk_i` cycle) and at least 4 strobes have passed since the noise change. Log both counts.
. Compare the 12 lane bytes of `esrc_decor_bytes_o` at each of the next 16 strobes with (0xFF if Z[j] is 1, else 0x00) AND 0xFF.
. Write the drawn mask M to `DECORRELATOR_MASK`. Discard the next 2 strobes. Compare the 12 lane bytes of the next 16 strobes with (0xFF if Z[j] is 1, else 0x00) AND M.
. Bypass-words leg. Run the set-up. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF, `DECORRELATOR_MASK` = M, `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1, `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = 0 and `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Steps 7 to 11 run four times: for `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` w = 1 and w = 0, each with the lane noise Z and with all lanes stuck1.
. Write `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = w. Drive the lane noise of the pass. Wait for the settle period of step 3.
. Read `FIFO_RDATA` (a 32-bit read) until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0. No word enters the FIFO, because `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0.
. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1. Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads 64 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0.
. Read 64 words from `FIFO_RDATA`.
. For every window of 3 consecutive words compare the population count of the window with popcount(noise) times popcount(M). With all lanes stuck1 compare every word with the mask M in each of its four byte lanes.
. Downsample leg. Run the set-up. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0, so that the FIFO never fills (the stream is not gated by it). Write `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 and `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = 0.
. Define the stride count D_N of a rate N. Word k0 is the seventh word on `esrc_compress_vld_o` after the response of the rate write, so the words of the first two outputs after the change are not counted. D_N is the number of `esrc_decor_valid_o` strobes after the arrival of word k0, up to and including the arrival of word k0 + 12 (K = 4 outputs of three words). The wait is bounded at 8(N + 1) + 8 strobes; a timeout fails the check. The leaf counts strobes and uses no absolute clock count.
. For N = 0, 1, N_r and 1023, in ascending order: write `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N and measure D_N.
. Compare each D_N with 4(N + 1) within ±1 strobe.
. Control. Write `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 0 (whitening on). Write `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = 0 and count the words on `esrc_compress_vld_o` over 16384 strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`. Write `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N_r and count again over 16384 strobes. Compare the ratio of the two counts with 1 within 15 percent.
. Health-test leg, control trial. Run the set-up. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF. Write 0xFF to `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` and read it as 0. Drive stuck0 on all lanes. Wait for `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 0 = 1 (a bounded wait). Log the wait as t_ctrl, or the bound if the bit does not set.
. Health-test leg, bypass trial. Run the set-up. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1. Write 0xFF to `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` and read it as 0. Drive stuck0 on all lanes. Wait for 3 times t_ctrl (the leaf logs t_ctrl and the multiple). Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` and log it with its value before the noise change (`OBS-BLOCKED CHK-COND-HT-OFF`); nothing is graded on it.
. Whitener leg. Run the set-up. Keep `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and the `balanced` pattern of the set-up. Write `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 0 and `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 1. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Read `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0.
. Write `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1, then `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1. Poll `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least 32 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). During the polls read `SHA256_STATUS` and log the values. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0, then `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0.
. Read `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` as the BIW word count and `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` as the FIFO word count. Read `INTR_STATUS.BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW`. If it reads 1, the count is not valid: log `CTRL-MISSING` and fail the leaf. Compare the FIFO word count with half the BIW word count within 8 words. Keep both counts non-zero as the control.
. During step 20 the SHA-256 status tap samples `SHA256_STATUS` on every `clk_i` and requires `OUTPUT_COUNT` to reach 8 and then to fall to 0, and during step 24 it requires `OUTPUT_COUNT` to stay 0.
. Pass-through leg, in the same trial. Read `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0. Read `BIW_OBS_RDATA` until `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0. Write `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 0. Poll `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` until it reads 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check), so that no digest word is in flight when the FIFO write opens. `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` stay 0 during the wait, so both FIFOs stay empty.
. Write `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1, then `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1. Poll `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least 16 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). After each poll read `SHA256_STATUS` and keep `OUTPUT_COUNT`. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0, then `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0.
. Read `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` and require it to be at least 8 (the control). Read all BIW observe words in order, then all FIFO words in order.
. Take the smallest offset d (0 to 8) at which every FIFO word i equals the BIW observe word i + d. No such offset fails the check. Log every kept `OUTPUT_COUNT` as `OBS-COND-PASSTHRU-OUTCOUNT` (not graded).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-COND-MASK
|With constant lane noise and BYPASS set on all lanes, each lane byte equals (0xFF if the lane noise bit is 1, else 0x00) AND `DECORRELATOR_MASK`, once the 29-stage chain is full (`architecture.adoc`, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing: a cleared mask bit reads as zero; BYPASS forces the feedback to zero). The check runs with the reset mask 0xFF and with the drawn partial mask M. Z has at least one 1 and one 0, so a lane swap, an ignored mask or a mask that covers only some lanes changes at least one byte. Anchor: `esrc_decor_bytes_o` at each `esrc_decor_valid_o`, 12 lane bytes per strobe, 16 strobes per mask. The 0xFF run is the control of the M run: the same lanes give the full byte. Evidence: `CHK-COND-MASK PASS seed=<n> mask=0x<m> noise=0x<z> lanes=12 mismatches=0`, logged once per mask.
|CHK-COND-BYPASS
|With `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1, the population count of any 3 consecutive FIFO words equals popcount(lane noise) times popcount(mask); with all lanes at 1 every word is the mask in each of its four byte lanes (`entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR`: the twelve generator bytes reach the FIFO as three 32-bit words). Constant noise repeats the same three words, so any 3 consecutive words give one of each and the check does not depend on the packing order. The check runs with `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 1 and = 0. A wrong DUT that whitens the bypass path, drops a byte or writes four words per output changes the count. The 64 words are the first words written after the FIFO is empty and enabled, so no word from before the mode change is read. Anchor: `FIFO_RDATA`. Evidence: `CHK-COND-BYPASS PASS seed=<n> whiten=<w> mask=0x<m> noise=0x<z> words=<k> triple_popcount=<p> expect=<e>`, and for the all-ones pass `CHK-COND-BYPASS PASS seed=<n> whiten=<w> mask=0x<m> noise=0xFFF words=<k> word_eq_mask_x4=1`.
|CHK-COND-DOWNSAMPLE
|With the compressor bypassed, every (N+1)th decorrelator output reaches the stream as three words (`entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE`: every (N+1)th decorrelator output while BIW extraction is bypassed; `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR`: the twelve generator bytes as three 32-bit words). One strobe of `esrc_decor_valid_o` is one decorrelator output. Over K = 4 outputs, from word k0 to word k0 + 12 on `esrc_compress_vld_o`, the strobe count D_N equals 4(N + 1) within ±1, for N = 0, 1, N_r and 1023. The ±1 is the edge allowance: a strobe in the same `clk_i` cycle as an end word can count on either side. Words k0 and k0 + 12 hold the same position in their outputs, so the count does not depend on the packing order. A DUT that selects every Nth or every (N+2)th output gives 4(N + 1) - 4 or 4(N + 1) + 4, outside the allowance at every rate, N = 1023 included. A DUT that ignores the rate gives 4 at every rate. A DUT that writes other than three words per output gives another multiple of N + 1. The check uses no absolute time. Anchor: the words of `esrc_compress_vld_o` and the strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`, with `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0 (the stream is not gated by it). Control: with the compressor on, the ratio of the word counts over 16384 strobes at rate 0 and at rate N_r is within 15 percent of 1, so the effect needs the bypass. Evidence: `CHK-COND-DOWNSAMPLE PASS seed=<n> n=<N> bypass=1 k=4 strobes=<d> expect=<4(N+1)> tol=1 ctrl_bypass0_ratio=<c>`; `ctrl_bypass0_ratio` carries the control on the N_r line and `n/a` on the others.
|CHK-COND-HT-OFF
|Blocked: the specification does not state one behaviour of the 32-bit health test with the compressor bypassed. `architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source) states that the test is clock-gated off in that mode, and `architecture.adoc` (Health Testing) states that the 32-bit instance monitors the post-BIW or post-bypass stream (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The leaf runs the control trial and the bypass trial, logs `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` of the bypass trial after 3 t_ctrl with t_ctrl, and grades nothing on them. The check is not counted in `checks=<k>`. Evidence: `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-COND-HT-OFF reason=ht32_in_bypass_unstated seed=<n> bypass=1 ht_status=0x<s> t_ctrl_clk=<c> wait_clk=<w>`.
|CHK-COND-SHA
|With whitening on, the FIFO word count equals half the BIW word count within 8 words (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening: 16 input words give 8 digest words). A DUT that passes the words through gives a count equal to the BIW count; a DUT with another ratio leaves the half-count window. The tolerance is one digest (8 words), because a digest can still be in flight at each end of the window. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` and `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` readback after the window, with no pop, no observe overflow and the FIFO empty at the start. Control: both counts are non-zero and logged. Evidence: `CHK-COND-SHA PASS seed=<n> biw_words=<a> fifo_words=<b>`.
|CHK-COND-SHA-COUNT
|With whitening on, `SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT` reaches 8 when a digest is available and falls to 0 as the digest words are accepted (`entropy_source.adoc`, `SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT`). With whitening off it stays 0 at every clock of the pass-through window (`SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT`: remains 0 while bypassed). A DUT that never reports 8, never returns to 0, or reports a digest count while bypassed, fails. Anchor: the ESRC SHA-256 status tap (an observation tap). Control: the BIW observe count is non-zero in the same run, and the reach of 8 in the whitened window of the same seed is the control of the zero in the pass-through window. Evidence: `CHK-COND-SHA-COUNT PASS seed=<n> out_count_max=8 out_count_end=0 biw_words=<a> bypass_out_count_max=0`.
|CHK-COND-PASSTHRU
|With whitening off, every FIFO word equals the BIW observe word at one fixed offset d (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening: the BIW output passes to the FIFO without modification). The polled reads of `SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT` are logged and not graded: no graded window of the leaf shows the field non-zero, so a zero read cannot fail; the zero while bypassed is a cell of CHK-COND-SHA-COUNT. A compliant DUT matches at its true offset, so the outcome does not depend on the word values; a whitened, dropped or reordered word breaks the match at every offset and fails. The offset d (0 to 8) absorbs the few BIW words captured before the FIFO write enable; the leaf logs d. The FIFO write opens only after `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` reads 0 with whitening off, so no digest word of the whitened run enters the compare. Anchor: `FIFO_RDATA`, `BIW_OBS_RDATA` and `SHA256_STATUS` readback. Control: the FIFO LEVEL is non-zero in the same window, so the compare has words. Evidence: `CHK-COND-PASSTHRU PASS seed=<n> offset=<d> compared=<k> mismatches=0`, and `OBS-COND-PASSTHRU-OUTCOUNT seed=<n> reads=<r> nonzero=<z>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Only the new cells are graded; the owners of the open tests keep the rest. The leaf grades the effect of the four options on counts, popcounts and equality. It does not grade an absolute byte period (the unit is blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), the 32-bit health test with the compressor bypassed (blocked, logged only), the whitened word values (the specification states no BIW coefficient), or the packing order of the bypass words. The default path belongs to `sep_esrc_e2e_smoke_test`. The stored values of the options and the lock belong to `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`.

'''''

[[sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test]]
===== 1.46 `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (ESRC)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the ESRC registers (32-bit accesses, approved); the probes `esrc_compress_data_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` (approved); the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i` (approved); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). Added hook: the ESRC FIFO integrity inject, an error inject that forces one stored parity bit of the main FIFO entry at the read pointer, or one bit of one pointer copy of the main FIFO (CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PARITY, CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PTR, CHK-FIFO-INTEG-W1C); the test adds it to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. Expected values come from the specification only: the FIFO depth, the pointer arithmetic, the churn rule and the stream rule.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the pop count k (1 to 64), the non-zero 8-bit mask M of the churn leg (0x01 to 0xFF), the number of idle register reads between two `FIFO_STATUS` samples of the fill (0 to 3), the held word count H of the integrity trials (2 to 40), the parity bit (0 to 3) and the pointer pair, pointer copy and bit of the pointer trial. `reseed = 4` samples different counts, flags, words and injection points. The plan shape is the same on every seed. The leaf logs the plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: The main FIFO has 64 entries. `LEVEL` counts the valid words, and `RPTR` and `WPTR` move modulo 64. A write while full is discarded and sets `FIFO_OVERFLOW`. With `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0 writes stop and reads continue. The status bits clear by write-one (the subset clear is graded in `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`). Churn XORs each incoming word with the entry 32 positions ahead of the write pointer. The stream port carries each word as written into the FIFO. A one-bit error in the stored parity of a word that is read, or a mismatch between a pointer and its inverted copy, sets `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`, which clears by write-one. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`INTR_STATUS`, `FIFO_CTRL`, `FIFO_STATUS`, `FIFO_RDATA`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Output FIFO: four odd-parity bits per entry, "pointer integrity checking", "parity verification on reads", "parity error, and pointer error conditions are all reported to the CSR block").

*Test Coverage*: A fill without pops. Pops with the producer stopped, down to empty and one more. The stream order after a drain and a restart. Churn on and off with a constant incoming word. The integrity trials, each from a TRNG reset: the FIFO holds a drawn number of words (2 to 40); a one-bit error in the stored parity of the word at the read pointer, then a pop of that word; and, in a separate trial, a one-bit error in one copy of a pointer pair, then one write and one pop. The error is released after the pop. Error injection: a write while full and a read while empty (point: the producer and `FIFO_RDATA`; kind: overflow and underflow; expected response: the two status bits); a one-bit parity error and a one-bit pointer-copy error (point: the ESRC FIFO integrity inject; expected response: `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` set). Extends `sep_esrc_e2e_fifo_closed_test`, which grades the set and clear of `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` on a closed FIFO; here an underflow is only the precondition of the mask clear. This test grades only the new cells; the owner of the open test keeps the underflow set and clear. The write-one clear of a proper subset of the two flags is graded in `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`. Functional coverage: `sep_esrc_conditioning_cg` (`cp_fifo_occupancy`, `cp_fifo_ctrl`, `cp_churn_age`), `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`cp_fifo_flags`, `cp_fifo_integrity`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path` hw bins).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set-up. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). Write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status"). Write `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0. Run the clean start (common rule "Clean start"), which leaves `balanced` on all twelve lanes. Wait for `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). The reset values hold: `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1, churn off, compressor not bypassed, whitening on. Steps 2 to 20 run in this order without a reset.
. Fill leg. Count the words on `esrc_compress_vld_o` over a window of 16 consecutive `FIFO_STATUS` reads that starts at the end of the set-up. Keep the count as the before-fill count.
. Read `FIFO_STATUS` repeatedly, with the drawn number of idle register reads between two samples, and no pop. At each sample compare (`WPTR` - `RPTR`) mod 64 with `LEVEL` mod 64. Continue until `LEVEL` reads 64 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Log the time of the fill.
. Read `INTR_STATUS` until `FIFO_OVERFLOW` reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). This is the error injection: the producer writes while the FIFO is full.
. Keep sampling `FIFO_STATUS` for as long as the fill took. Require `LEVEL` = 64 at every sample.
. Count the words on `esrc_compress_vld_o` over a window of 16 consecutive `FIFO_STATUS` reads while the FIFO is full. Log it with the before-fill count as `OBS-FIFO-STREAM-FULL`. Nothing is graded on these counts.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0.
. Pop leg. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Read `FIFO_STATUS`. Pop k words (a 32-bit read of `FIFO_RDATA` each) and read `FIFO_STATUS` after each pop.
. Pop until `LEVEL` reads 0. This is the error injection: then pop once more at `LEVEL` = 0. The data is undefined and is not graded.
. Read `INTR_STATUS`. If `FIFO_OVERFLOW` or `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` reads 0, log `CTRL-MISSING` and fail the leaf. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0.
. Clear leg. Write 1 to both flags in `INTR_STATUS` and read them, logging the values as `OBS-FIFO-FLAGS-CLEAR`. No check grades this clear.
. Resume leg. Read `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` as 0. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1. Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0.
. Order leg (churn off, compressor not bypassed, `balanced` noise). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Pop until `LEVEL` reads 0.
. Arm the recording of the words on `esrc_compress_data_o` at `esrc_compress_vld_o`, then write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1 at once. The recording starts before the write, because the write enable takes effect at the register write and the stream words that pass before the write response returns enter the FIFO.
. Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads 64 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0 and keep recording until the response of that write returns. Pop 64 words. Take the smallest offset d (0 to 8) at which every popped word i equals the recorded word i + d; no such offset fails the check. Log d. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0.
. Churn leg. Draw the non-zero 8-bit mask M (0x01 to 0xFF; `DECORRELATOR_MASK.ENTROPY_BYTE_MASK` is bits 7:0). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Pop until `LEVEL` reads 0. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF, `DECORRELATOR_MASK` = M and `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1. Drive all lanes stuck1. The incoming word C is M in each of its four byte lanes.
. Wait until the last 6 words on `esrc_compress_data_o` all equal C (a bounded wait; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf).
. Write `FIFO_CTRL` with `ENABLE` = 1 and `ENTROPY_CHURN_ENABLE` = 1. Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads 64 (a bounded wait). Write `FIFO_CTRL` with `ENABLE` = 0 and `ENTROPY_CHURN_ENABLE` = 1. Pop 64 words w_0 to w_63 in write order. For every k from 32 to 63 compare w_k XOR w_(k-32) with C. The words w_0 to w_31 are not graded.
. Control. Pop until `LEVEL` reads 0. Write `FIFO_CTRL` with `ENABLE` = 1 and `ENTROPY_CHURN_ENABLE` = 0. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads 64. Write `FIFO_CTRL` with `ENABLE` = 0 and churn off. Pop 64 words and compare each with C.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0. Write 1 to `INTR_TEST.FIFO_ERROR`. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 1. Write 1 to `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and read it as 0.
. Parity trial. Run the set-up (it pulses the TRNG reset). Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least H. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Read `LEVEL` as the held count.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0. Pop one word and read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` again and require 0.
. Force one drawn bit of the stored parity of the entry at the read pointer. Pop that word. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 1. Release the force.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` as 1. Write 1 to it. Read it as 0.
. Pointer trial. Run the set-up (it pulses the TRNG reset). Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least H. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Read `LEVEL` as the held count.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0. Pop one word and log its clock count as the clean pop time. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` again and require 0.
. Force one drawn bit of one drawn copy of the drawn pointer pair. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1 until at least one word passes `esrc_compress_vld_o` (a bounded wait), then write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Pop one word.
. Poll `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` for a bound of 16 times the clean pop time (the leaf logs the bound and the clock count of the first read of 1). Release the force. Write 1 to `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`, read it, and log the value as `OBS-FIFO-PTR-W1C`. It is not graded: the released force can leave the forced value in the pointer copy, and the specification does not state whether the mismatch then persists.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-FIFO-FILL
|Without a pop `LEVEL` rises to 64 and stays, `FIFO_OVERFLOW` is 1, and (`WPTR` - `RPTR`) mod 64 equals `LEVEL` mod 64 at every sample (`entropy_source.adoc`, `FIFO_STATUS`, `INTR_STATUS`; `architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: 64 entries). A DUT with a shorter FIFO, a pointer that does not wrap at 64, or a missing overflow flag fails. The overflow comes from a write while full, so the check reads the flag only after `LEVEL` is 64. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS` and `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: `LEVEL` rises from 0 before it plateaus. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-FILL PASS seed=<n> level_max=64 overflow=1 wptr=<w> rptr=<r> ptr_diff_ok=1`, and `OBS-FIFO-STREAM-FULL seed=<n> full_window_words=<a> before_fill_window_words=<b>` (logged, not graded).
|CHK-FIFO-POP
|With the producer stopped each pop lowers `LEVEL` by 1, raises `RPTR` by 1 modulo 64 and leaves `WPTR` unchanged (`entropy_source.adoc`, `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE`: reads remain available while writes are disabled; `FIFO_RDATA`; `FIFO_STATUS`). A DUT that keeps writing, pops two words or moves `WPTR` fails. The stopped producer makes the level change the effect of the pop alone. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS` readback after each pop. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-POP PASS seed=<n> pops=<k> level_start=<l0> level_end=<l1> rptr_delta=<k> wptr_delta=0`.
|CHK-FIFO-RESUME
|After `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1, `LEVEL` rises again from 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE`: enables FIFO writes). A DUT that stays closed fails the bounded wait. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS` readback. Control: `LEVEL` reads 0 before the write. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-RESUME PASS seed=<n> enable=1 level_rises=1`.
|CHK-FIFO-ORDER
|After a drain and a restart, the 64 popped words equal 64 consecutive words of the stream port recording, in order, at one offset d from 0 to 8 (`architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: the stream port carries each word as written into the FIFO). A DUT that reorders words, drops a word or pops a different word than it wrote fails, because all 64 words must match at the same d. The recording starts before the enabling write and the offset d absorbs the stream words that enter the FIFO before the write response returns, as in `CHK-COND-PASSTHRU`. A compliant DUT matches at its true offset, so the outcome does not depend on the word values. A popped sequence that matches at no offset from 0 to 8 fails. Anchor: `esrc_compress_data_o` at `esrc_compress_vld_o` and `FIFO_RDATA`. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-ORDER PASS seed=<n> words=64 offset=<d> equal_order=1`.
|CHK-FIFO-CHURN
|With churn on and a constant incoming word C, for each k from 32 to 63 w_k XOR w_(k-32) equals C (`entropy_source.adoc`, `FIFO_CTRL.ENTROPY_CHURN_ENABLE`: XORs each incoming word with the entry 32 positions ahead of the write pointer). The entry 32 ahead of write k is the stored word of write k - 32, so a DUT without churn gives 0 and a DUT with another distance gives a value other than C. The words w_0 to w_31 depend on stale entries and are not graded. C is non-zero, because M is a non-zero 8-bit mask. Anchor: `FIFO_RDATA`. Control: with churn off every popped word equals C. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-CHURN PASS seed=<n> churn=1 pairs=<m> c=0x<c> mismatches=0 ctrl_churn_off_words_equal_c=1`.
|CHK-FIFO-NOERR
|After every fill, pop, restart and churn leg `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` reads 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`: set only by an integrity error). A DUT that raises a spurious integrity error fails. This is a "never" check. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback at the end of each leg. Control: a write of 1 to `INTR_TEST.FIFO_ERROR` in the same seed sets the bit, so the bit is observable. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-NOERR PASS seed=<n> legs=<k> fifo_error=0`.
|CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PARITY
|A pop of a word with a one-bit parity error sets `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`; a pop of an intact word of the same trial does not (`architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: "parity verification on reads"). A DUT that does not check parity on reads leaves the bit at 0, and a DUT that flags an intact word sets it at the first pop. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: the pop of the corrupted word in the same trial sets the bit, so the 0 after the intact pop is a read of an observable bit. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PARITY PASS seed=<n> held=<n> intact_pop_error=0 parity_pop_error=1`.
|CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PTR
|A one-bit error in one copy of a pointer pair sets `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` within a bound that is a multiple of the clean pop time (`architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: "pointer integrity checking", normal and inverted pointer pairs with mismatch detection). The text does not state the time of the detection, so the bound is 16 times the clean pop time of the same seed and the leaf logs both. A DUT without pointer checking leaves the bit at 0. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: the pops before the error leave the bit 0, so the rise comes from the pointer error. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PTR PASS seed=<n> pointer=<rptr or wptr> detect_clk=<d> clean_pop_clk=<c> fifo_error=1 ctrl_pre_error=0`.
|CHK-FIFO-INTEG-W1C
|In the parity trial, a write of 1 to `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` after the error is released clears it (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`: write one to clear). The corrupted entry is popped and the FIFO is closed, so no read of that entry follows and no fault remains. The clear of the pointer trial is logged as `OBS-FIFO-PTR-W1C` and not graded. A DUT that holds the bit, or that sets it again with no fault, fails. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: the bit reads 1 before the write. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-INTEG-W1C PASS seed=<n> before=1 after=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Only the new cells are graded; the owner of the open test keeps the rest. Not graded: whether a discarded write appears on the stream while the FIFO is full (blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>; the leaf logs the counts only), the integrity error of the observe FIFOs (not stated), the effect of `FIFO_ERROR` on the data path, and the churn words w_0 to w_31 (they depend on stale entries). The set and clear of `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` on a closed FIFO belongs to `sep_esrc_e2e_fifo_closed_test`. The conditioning options that make the constant word C belong to `sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test`.

'''''

[[sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test]]
===== 1.47 `sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (ESRC)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the ESRC registers (32-bit accesses, approved); the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i` (approved); the probe `esrc_compress_vld_o` (approved); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). Added hook: the ESRC noise-source stuck-at inject, an error inject that forces the output of the noise ring oscillator of each lane to a constant 0 or 1 (a stuck noise source). The constant reaches the pre-decorrelator samples that `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` returns (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`); `esrc_noise_ext_i` forces only the decorrelator input, which the noise observe FIFO does not sample. The test adds the inject to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. No PROPOSED item. Expected values come from the specification only: the selected lane and its constant noise value, and the rules of flush, overflow and lock.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the 16 `LANE_SEL` values (constraint filter: the first value and the value before each of 0 and 12 to 15 come from 1 to 11, so every write changes the effective lane), the value z (0 or 1) that the effective lane carries at each lane change (both values among the lane changes of a seed), the number f of words buffered before the `FLUSH` (4 to 8), and the number k of words buffered at the lock moment (1 to 4). `reseed = 4` samples different orders, values and moments. The plan shape is the same on every seed. The leaf logs the plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: With `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` the samples of the selected lane pack into 32-bit observe words. `LANE_SEL` 0 to 11 selects the lane, and other values select lane 0. Changing lanes and a write of `FLUSH` discard the buffered words. The noise observe FIFO sees the stream before the boot gate. A word offered while the FIFO is full is discarded and sets `NOISE_OBS_OVERFLOW`. The BIW observe FIFO captures after startup completes and sets `BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW` the same way. Neither observe path disturbs the main chain. Both `RAW_ENABLE` bits are held by `FIPS_LOCK`. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`NOISE_OBS_CTRL`, `NOISE_OBS_STATUS`, `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL`, `BIW_OBS_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_RDATA`, `INTR_STATUS`, `FIPS_LOCK`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source, Debug Observation Pin).

*Test Coverage*: Constant lane noise in which the effective lane carries z and every other lane carries the opposite value. A failing startup (stuck0 with BYPASS set on all lanes, `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0) with both observe paths on. On every seed all 16 `LANE_SEL` values in drawn order. The drawn order makes every write change the effective lane, so each value, 12 to 15 included, is graded on every seed. A write that keeps the effective lane (0 and 12 to 15 all select lane 0) is not driven, because whether it flushes is blocked (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). A `FLUSH` write after the lane noise changes. Overflow of the noise FIFO and of the BIW FIFO after startup. Lock legs for each `RAW_ENABLE` field on every seed: locked at 1 and locked at 0. The BIW observe FIFO has no `FLUSH`, so the flush after the lock runs on the noise FIFO only. Error injection: a full observe FIFO (point: no read of the observe FIFO; kind: overflow; expected response: the overflow bit); a stuck noise source on every lane (point: the ESRC noise-source stuck-at inject; kind: stuck-at 0 or 1 per lane; expected response: noise observe words of the stuck value of the selected lane). Extends `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`, which grades that both `RAW_ENABLE` fields are held by the lock and that `LANE_SEL` stays writable under the lock (`CHK-OBS-LANE-SEL`). This test adds capture under the lock, `LEVEL` staying 0 when locked at 0, and `FLUSH` after the lock on the noise FIFO, and grades only those cells; the owner of the open test keeps the held fields and the writable `LANE_SEL`, which this test reads only as controls. Functional coverage: `sep_esrc_conditioning_cg` (`x_obs_capture`, `cp_obs_lane_sel`, `cp_obs_flush`, `cp_obs_overflow`, `cp_obs_lock`), `sep_crypto_reg_lock_cg` (`x_fips_field`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path` hw bins).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set-up, run at the start of each leg and of each trial named in a leg. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). The reset returns both `RAW_ENABLE` fields to 0 and `RING_OSC_CTRL.SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT` to 0xFFF (the shared internal sample clock). Select the external sample clock on every lane: write `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` = 0, then `RING_OSC_CTRL.SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT` = 0, then `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` = 0xFFF (`entropy_source.adoc`, `RING_OSC_CTRL`: a clear bit selects the external sample clock; `architecture.adoc`, Noise Generation: software changes the source of a lane only while its generator is disabled). The bench drives the external sample clock `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i` free-running in both simulators, and the internal ring oscillators do not toggle under Verilator (`hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv`), so this selection lets the stuck-at values reach the pre-decorrelator samples of the noise observe FIFO in both simulators. Steps 2 and 3 make the same selection after their TRNG reset. Write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status"). Write `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0. Run the clean start (common rule "Clean start"), which leaves `balanced` on all twelve lanes. Wait for `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). The legs run in the order below. Every lane value z or its opposite that a later step drives is applied through the ESRC noise-source stuck-at inject and, with the same value, through `esrc_noise_ext_i`; the inject is released at each set-up and after the leaf. The clean start and the stuck0 drive of the failing trial use `esrc_noise_ext_i` only.
. Ungated leg, clean trial. Pulse the TRNG reset as in the set-up. Write `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0. Run the clean start with `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 among its writes. Wait for `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1. Poll `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Log the clock count from `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 to that read as t_clean.
. Ungated leg, failing trial. Pulse the TRNG reset as in the set-up. Write `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0 and `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF. Drive stuck0 on all lanes. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1.
. Poll `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Wait for 4 times t_clean (the leaf logs both). Read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE`, `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` and `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL`.
. Lane leg. Run the set-up. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 with `LANE_SEL` = 0. For each of the 16 `LANE_SEL` values v in drawn order, run steps 6 to 10. The effective lane e(v) is v for v from 0 to 11 and lane 0 for v from 12 to 15.
. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0 with the current `LANE_SEL`, so that no new word arrives, and pop `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` until `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0 (a bounded number of pops: at most the FIFO depth; more fails the leaf). e(v) differs from the effective lane of the current `LANE_SEL` (the drawn order makes it so). Drive lane e(v) with the next value z and every other lane with the opposite value. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 with the current `LANE_SEL`.
. Poll `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least 4 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Read `LEVEL` as the level before the write.
. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.LANE_SEL` = v (with `RAW_ENABLE` = 1). Read `LEVEL` at once as the level after the write.
. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 8 (a bounded wait; the leaf logs the wait). Pop 8 words.
. Compare every popped word with 0xFFFFFFFF if z is 1 and with 0x00000000 if z is 0, and compare the level after the write with the level before.
. Flush leg. Run the set-up. Drive lane a (a drawn lane, 0 to 11) with z and every other lane with the opposite value. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 with `LANE_SEL` = a. Poll `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least f.
. Drive lane a with the opposite value of z and every other lane with z. Poll `LEVEL` until it has risen by at least 2 words since the change. Read `LEVEL` as the level before the flush. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH` = 1 (with `RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `LANE_SEL` = a).
. Read `LEVEL` at once as the level after the flush. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 2, read it as m, pop m words and compare each with the new value of lane a.
. Overflow leg. Run the set-up. Read `INTR_STATUS` and require both overflow bits 0. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1. Pop nothing. Poll `INTR_STATUS` until `NOISE_OBS_OVERFLOW` reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Read `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` as the depth D of the noise FIFO. Log the fill time. Do the same for `BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW` and `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` as the depth of the BIW FIFO.
. Wait for twice the longer of the two fill times. Read both levels and compare each with its depth.
. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0, so that no new word arrives and neither FIFO overflows again. Pop the noise observe FIFO until its `LEVEL` reads 0 (the values are not graded). Write 1 to `INTR_STATUS.NOISE_OBS_OVERFLOW` and read it as 0. Pop the BIW observe FIFO until its `LEVEL` reads 0. Write 1 to `INTR_STATUS.BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW` and read it as 0.
. Main-chain leg. Run the set-up. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0 (the stream is not gated by it). Keep both `RAW_ENABLE` fields at 0.
. Measure W, the `clk_i` time in which `esrc_compress_vld_o` delivers 256 words with the observe paths off. Count the words over a second window of W with the observe paths off.
. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1. Pop nothing. Count the words over a window of W. Compare the two counts within 10 percent.
. Lock leg, for each of the two FIFOs (noise and BIW), first locked at 1, then locked at 0. Locked at 1: run the set-up. Drive lane 0 with z and every other lane with the opposite value. Write the `RAW_ENABLE` of the FIFO = 1 and read it back as 1. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 2 and log the clock count from the `RAW_ENABLE` write as t_cap. The second word needs one whole word period after the first, so t_cap covers at least one word period whatever the phase of the first capture.
. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least k. Write `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` = 1 (`HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE.SIZE` stays at its reset value, which is above the minimum of 1024 that the lock requires) and read the lock as 1. Read `LEVEL` as L (the words held at the lock). The lock holds `RAW_ENABLE` at 1, so capture cannot be stopped for a drain.
. Pop L + 2 words. Before each pop, poll `LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait of a multiple of t_cap; a timeout fails the check). The FIFO holds only L words at the lock, so the last 2 pops need words captured after the lock. For the noise FIFO compare the last 2 popped words with 0xFFFFFFFF if z is 1 and with 0x00000000 if z is 0. For the BIW FIFO compare no value: the L + 2 pops are the check.
. For the noise FIFO only: poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 4. Read `LEVEL` as the level before the flush. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH` = 1 (with `RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `LANE_SEL` = 0). Read `LEVEL` at once as the level after the flush.
. Locked at 0: run the set-up. Drive lane 0 with z and every other lane with the opposite value. Read the `RAW_ENABLE` of the FIFO as 0. Write `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` = 1 and read it as 1. Write the `RAW_ENABLE` of the FIFO = 1 and read it back as 0 (a control).
. Wait for 4 times t_cap of the locked-at-1 pass of the same FIFO (the leaf logs both). Read `LEVEL` as 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-OBS-UNGATED
|With a failing startup (`BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 0) the noise observe `LEVEL` is above 0 (`architecture.adoc`, Entropy Source: the noise observe FIFO sees the stream ungated). A DUT that gates the noise path shows noise `LEVEL` 0. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_STATUS` and `MAIN_SM_STATUS` readback. Control: the BIW observe `LEVEL` of the same trial reads 0, so the trial runs before the boot gate opens, or the leaf logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails. The BIW observe gate itself is graded by `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` (CHK-BOOT-BAD) and is a control here. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-UNGATED PASS seed=<n> boot_done=0 noise_obs_level=<n> ctrl_biw_obs_level=0`.
|CHK-OBS-LANE
|After a write of `LANE_SEL` that changes the effective lane, `LEVEL` falls below its value before the write, and every popped word is all ones or all zeros as the selected lane noise is 1 or 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.LANE_SEL`: values 0 to 11 select the lane, other values select lane 0, changing lanes flushes; `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`: LSB-first samples of the selected lane). The effective lane carries z and every other lane carries the opposite value, both applied at the ring-oscillator output by the ESRC noise-source stuck-at inject, upstream of the pre-decorrelator samples, so a word from a wrong lane has the other value and fails. A DUT that maps a value from 12 to 15 to a lane other than lane 0 fails the same way. All 16 values run on every seed. Before each change the leaf waits for `LEVEL` of at least 4 so that the fall is visible. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS` and `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` readback. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-LANE PASS seed=<n> lane_sel=<s> lane=<l> z=<b> words=<k> expect=0x<e> mismatches=0 level_before=<b> level_after_change=<c>`.
|CHK-OBS-FLUSH
|After a `FLUSH` write or an effective lane change, no word from before it is popped (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH`: write one to discard buffered words; `LANE_SEL`: changing lanes flushes). In the flush leg the buffered old words carry the old value and the new words carry the opposite value; a DUT that does not discard the old words returns them first and fails. In the lane leg the buffered words come from the old lane, which carries the opposite of the new value. Control: the noise value changes before the flush, so old and new words differ. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`, and `LEVEL` below its value before the flush. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-FLUSH PASS seed=<n> via=<flush or lane> old_words_after=0 words=<k>`.
|CHK-OBS-OVERFLOW
|Without pops `LEVEL` plateaus at a depth D, the overflow bit is 1, `LEVEL` stays D after twice the fill time, and a write of 1 clears the bit once capture is off and the FIFO is drained; the BIW FIFO shows the same plateau and bit after startup (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.NOISE_OBS_OVERFLOW` and `BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW`: a word is discarded while full; write one to clear). D is the `LEVEL` at the first read of the overflow bit, so a DUT with no overflow bit, or one that overwrites and keeps rising, fails. The leaf grades the plateau and the bit and not which word is discarded. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_STATUS` and `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: both overflow bits read 0 before the fill. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-OVERFLOW PASS seed=<n> fifo=<noise or biw> depth=<D> overflow=1 level_after_two_fills=<D> w1c=0`.
|CHK-OBS-MAIN
|The word counts on `esrc_compress_vld_o` over equal windows with the observe paths on and off are within 10 percent (`architecture.adoc`, Entropy Source: the observe FIFOs sit beside the datapath). A DUT whose observe capture stalls or consumes the main chain shows a lower count. The observe FIFOs overflow in the on window, so the check covers full observe FIFOs. Anchor: the stream tap. Control: both counts are non-zero, and the off window is measured twice. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-MAIN PASS seed=<n> words_obs_on=<a> words_obs_off=<b>`.
|CHK-OBS-LOCK
|With `RAW_ENABLE` set before the lock, capture continues after the lock: the FIFO delivers L + 2 words, where L is the `LEVEL` read at the lock, and for the noise FIFO the last two popped words equal the lane value; with it clear at the lock, a write of 1 leaves `LEVEL` at 0; for the noise FIFO a `FLUSH` after the lock lowers `LEVEL` (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE`: `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` prevents changes; `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH`). A DUT that stops capture at the lock, or that lets a post-lock write enable capture, fails. The locked-at-0 check is a "never" check: its control is the locked-at-1 pass of the same FIFO and seed with the same noise, which captures after t_cap, and the pass waits for 4 times t_cap. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_STATUS` and `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` readback. The held field and the writable `LANE_SEL` are read as controls only. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-LOCK PASS seed=<n> fifo=<noise or biw> locked_at=<1 or 0> level_at_lock=<L> post_lock_pops=<L+2> noise_flush_level_after=<f or n/a> capture_clk=<t> wait_clk=<w>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Only the new cells are graded; the owner of the open test keeps the rest. Not graded: which word an observe FIFO discards when it is full (blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), whether a `LANE_SEL` write that keeps the effective lane flushes (blocked; not driven), and the word values of the BIW observe FIFO (the specification states no BIW coefficient). The held value of the `RAW_ENABLE` fields under the lock and the writable `LANE_SEL` belong to `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`. The startup gate of the main FIFO and of the BIW observe FIFO belongs to `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test`.

'''''

[[sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test]]
===== 1.48 `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (CSRNG and EDN)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the CSRNG and EDN registers (approved); the interrupt vector probe `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (approved; CSRNG fatal error on vector bit 26, EDN fatal error on vector bit 28, Crypto alert on vector bit 32); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). No PROPOSED item. No inject beyond the register writes of the scenario. Expected values come from the register text and the interrupt map only.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the 12 cells and the extra word count e (2 to 12) of each EDN FIFO trial. `reseed = 4` samples different orders and counts. The plan shape is the same on every seed: every seed walks the 12 source-bit cells and the two EDN FIFO trials. The leaf logs the plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: Writing a defined source bit number to `ERR_CODE_TEST` forces one error. The `ERR_CODE` source bit sets and, except for EDN bit 0, stays set until reset (the register text of EDN bit 0 does not state it). CSRNG sets `INTR_STATE.CS_FATAL_ERR` for a FIFO error or a fatal alert, and EDN sets `edn_fatal_err` for a FIFO error. The sources that the RDL says "signal a fatal alert" pulse the crypto alert (PIC source 33). A write beyond 13 words to the EDN `RESEED_CMD` or `GENERATE_CMD` FIFO is ignored and raises `edn_fatal_err` and a fatal alert. Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc` (`INTR_STATE`, `ERR_CODE`, `ERR_CODE_TEST`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc` (`RESEED_CMD`, `GENERATE_CMD`, `ERR_CODE`, `ERR_CODE_TEST`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map); `hw/sys/sep/doc/crypto.adoc` (OpenTitan Alert Termination).

*Test Coverage*: On every seed the 12 source-bit cells, one per trial: CSRNG bits 0, 1, 20, 21, 22, 25 and 26, and EDN bits 0, 1, 20, 21 and 22, each with the fatal-error enable set. EDN is enabled in software-port mode after CSRNG, so the FIFO-error cells run on an enabled unit and no command is sent. The write-one clear of the interrupt state, and a TRNG reset.
