For each of `RESEED_CMD` and `GENERATE_CMD` on every seed: 1, a drawn 2 to 12, 13 and 14 words. Error injection: `ERR_CODE_TEST` writes and a 14th command word (point: the register of each unit; kind: a forced error and a FIFO overrun; expected response: as the objective states). Extends the open entropy tests (`sep_esrc_e2e_smoke_test` and the others), which read the CSRNG and EDN `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` as zero (`CHK-ALERTS-ZERO`, a passive read). This test grades only the forced errors; the owners of the open tests keep the zero reads. Functional coverage: `sep_drbg_err_inject_cg` (`x_err_source`, `cp_fatal_consequence`, `cp_sticky`, `cp_edn_cmd_fifo`), `sep_edn_mode_cg` (`cp_auto_fifo_words`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_line_gate`, `cp_hw_raise` cs_fatal_err and edn_fatal_err), `sep_crypto_reset_cg` (`cp_reset_evidence`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan. Log `PLAN seed=<n>` with the cell order, the enables and the extra word counts.
. Per trial, set-up. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). Bring up ESRC and enable CSRNG as the open end-to-end test does. Then enable EDN in software-port mode, after CSRNG, in the order of the Programmer's Guide: write `CTRL` with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` False, `AUTO_REQ_MODE` False, `EDN_ENABLE` True and `CMD_FIFO_RST` False. Software-port mode sends no command, so the `RESEED_CMD` and `GENERATE_CMD` FIFOs are not consumed. Read `ERR_CODE` once and log the clock count as t_ref. Every `ERR_CODE` compare of this entry uses the field bits of the register text (CSRNG bits 0, 1, 20 to 22, 25, 26 and 28 to 30; EDN bits 0, 1, 20 to 22 and 28 to 30) and logs the Reserved bits (common rule "Field bits"). Every wait below is a bounded wait of a multiple of t_ref (the leaf logs t_ref and the multiple); a timeout fails the check.
. Source-bit cells, one cell per trial, in drawn order. Run the set-up. Read `ERR_CODE` and the fatal-error bit of `INTR_STATE` of the unit (`CS_FATAL_ERR` of CSRNG, `edn_fatal_err` of EDN) and require 0. Write 1 to the fatal-error bit of `INTR_ENABLE`. Watch vector bits 26, 28 and 32 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` on every `clk_i` from this step to step 7.
. This is the error injection. Write the source bit number to `ERR_CODE_TEST` of the unit.
. Wait for `ERR_CODE` to change (a bounded wait). Read `ERR_CODE` and require the forced source bit set. Log the other source bits (bits 0 to 27 apart from the forced bit) as `OBS-other-src` and the type bits 28 to 30 as `OBS-type`. Nothing is graded on them.
. Read `INTR_STATE` and `INTR_ENABLE` of the unit. For CSRNG (any of the 7 bits) and for EDN bits 0 and 1 compare `CS_FATAL_ERR` or `edn_fatal_err` with 1. For EDN bits 20 to 22 log the state and grade no value. For the cells with a graded state require the vector bit (26 for CSRNG, 28 for EDN) to read 1, and for EDN bits 20 to 22 log it.
. For the fatal-alert sources (CSRNG bits 20, 21, 22, 25 and 26; EDN bits 20, 21 and 22) require at least one cycle with vector bit 32 high during the watch and 0 before the write of step 4. For the FIFO-error bits (0 and 1) log the vector bit 32 cycles as `OBS-DRBG-ALERT` and grade no value.
. Sticky leg. Read the fatal-error bit of `INTR_STATE` of the unit. For the FIFO-error bits (CSRNG bits 0 and 1, EDN bits 0 and 1) require 1 (a control); for the other bits log it. Write 1 to that bit. Read it again. For the FIFO-error bits, which force a single pulse, require 0. For the other bits log the state as `OBS-DRBG-STATE-AFTER-W1C` and grade no value. Read `ERR_CODE` and require its source bit still set for every cell except EDN bit 0; for EDN bit 0 log it as `OBS-EDN-STICKY0`.
. Reset leg. Read `ERR_CODE` as non-zero (a control). Pulse the TRNG reset as in the set-up. Read `ERR_CODE` as 0.
. EDN FIFO trials, one trial each for `RESEED_CMD` and `GENERATE_CMD`, with the drawn extra count e. Run the set-up. Write 1 to `INTR_ENABLE.edn_fatal_err`. Watch vector bits 28 and 32 on every `clk_i` to step 13. Write one test data word to the FIFO. Read `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` and require 0. Require vector bits 28 and 32 low.
. Write test data words until e words are in the FIFO. Read `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` and require 0. Require vector bits 28 and 32 low. Write test data words until 13 words are in the FIFO and repeat the reads.
. This is the error injection. Write the 14th word.
. Wait for `ERR_CODE` to change. Read `ERR_CODE` and require `SFIFO_RESCMD_ERR` (bit 0) for `RESEED_CMD` or `SFIFO_GENCMD_ERR` (bit 1) for `GENERATE_CMD`. Read `INTR_STATE.edn_fatal_err` as 1. Require vector bit 28 high and at least one cycle of vector bit 32 high. Pulse the TRNG reset as in the set-up and read `ERR_CODE` as 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-DRBG-ERRCODE
|After the `ERR_CODE_TEST` write, `ERR_CODE` holds the forced source bit; `CS_FATAL_ERR` (CSRNG) or `edn_fatal_err` (EDN bits 0 and 1) is 1; and with the enable set the vector bit (26 for CSRNG, 28 for EDN) reads 1 (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`, `ERR_CODE_TEST`, `INTR_STATE`: the error is forced at the bit number of `ERR_CODE`; `interrupts.adoc`: PIC source 27 and 29 on vector bits 26 and 28). The text does not say that no other bit sets, and a forced fatal source can set other source bits through the escalation of the unit, so the other source bits and the type bits 28 to 30 are logged only. A DUT that sets no bit at the forced position, or a state that does not reach the vector, fails. Anchor: `ERR_CODE`, `INTR_STATE` and `INTR_ENABLE` readback and the vector probe. Control: `ERR_CODE` and the fatal-error state bit read 0 before the write. Evidence: `CHK-DRBG-ERRCODE PASS seed=<n> unit=<csrng or edn> bit=<b> err_code=0x<c> source_bit_set=1 OBS-other-src=0x<o> OBS-type=0x<t> fatal_state=<s> enable=1 vec=<v>`; `vec` is graded for the cells with a graded state and logged for EDN bits 20 to 22.
|CHK-DRBG-ALERT
|For the sources whose register text says a fatal alert (CSRNG bits 20, 21, 22, 25, 26; EDN bits 20, 21, 22) vector bit 32 pulses (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`: "This error will signal a fatal alert"; `interrupts.adoc`: source 33 is the Crypto alert). A DUT that forces the error and raises no alert fails. This is a pulse that must come from the write: the control is that vector bit 32 reads 0 before the `ERR_CODE_TEST` write. For the FIFO-error bits the pulse is logged and not graded. Anchor: the vector probe, watched on every `clk_i`. Evidence: `CHK-DRBG-ALERT PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> bit=<b> pic33_high_cycles=<c> pre=0 t_ref=<t>`.
|CHK-DRBG-STICKY
|After a write-one clear of the fatal-error state bit the `ERR_CODE` source bit stays set, for every source except EDN bit 0 (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`: "This bit will stay set until the next reset"; the register text of EDN bit 0 does not carry this sentence, so that bit is logged). For the FIFO-error bits (CSRNG bits 0 and 1, EDN bits 0 and 1) the state also reads 0 after the clear (`INTR_STATE`: write one to clear; `ERR_CODE_TEST`: the write forces an error pulse, so no source holds the state). A DUT that clears `ERR_CODE` with the state fails on every graded cell; a DUT that does not clear the state fails on the FIFO-error cells. For the fatal-alert sources the register text does not say that the state stays clear while the forced source is held, so the state after the clear is logged and not graded. Anchor: readback. Control: on the FIFO-error cells the state reads 1 before the clear. Evidence: `CHK-DRBG-STICKY PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> bit=<b> after_w1c err_code=0x<c> source_bit_kept=<1 or na> state_graded=<1 or 0> fatal_state=<s>`; `OBS-EDN-STICKY0 seed=<n> err_code=0x<c>` for EDN bit 0.
|CHK-DRBG-RESET
|After a TRNG reset `ERR_CODE` is 0 (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`: stays set until the next reset; `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc`, Reset: the TRNG reset is a full-IP reset of the entropy source and DRBG). A DUT that keeps the error through the reset fails. Anchor: `ERR_CODE` readback. Control: the bit is set before the reset. Evidence: `CHK-DRBG-RESET PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> after_trng_reset err_code=0x0`.
|CHK-EDN-FIFO13
|Up to 13 words written to `RESEED_CMD` or `GENERATE_CMD` leave `ERR_CODE` at 0 and raise no interrupt (`edn.adoc`, `RESEED_CMD`, `GENERATE_CMD`: up to 13 command words). The leaf checks the counts 1, e and 13. A DUT with a shorter FIFO fails at its limit. EDN is enabled in software-port mode, so the FIFOs take the words and nothing consumes them. The enable of `edn_fatal_err` is 1, so a spurious state shows on vector bit 28. Anchor: `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` readback and the vector probe. Control: the 14th word of the same FIFO sets the error (CHK-EDN-FIFO14). Evidence: `CHK-EDN-FIFO13 PASS seed=<n> fifo=<reseed or generate> words=<n> err_code=0x0`, logged for each count.
|CHK-EDN-FIFO14
|The 14th word sets `SFIFO_RESCMD_ERR` (bit 0) or `SFIFO_GENCMD_ERR` (bit 1) of the FIFO written, `edn_fatal_err` and a pulse on vector bit 32 (`edn.adoc`, `RESEED_CMD`, `GENERATE_CMD`: more than 13 entries are ignored and EDN signals an `edn_fatal_err` interrupt as well as a fatal alert; `ERR_CODE` bits 0 and 1). A DUT that accepts a 14th word silently fails. The unit is enabled, so a healthy EDN reports the FIFO error. Anchor: `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` readback and the vector probe. Control: the same FIFO reads 0 after 13 words (CHK-EDN-FIFO13). Evidence: `CHK-EDN-FIFO14 PASS seed=<n> fifo=<f> words=14 err_code=0x<c> fatal_intr=1 alert_cycles=<a>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Only the forced-error cells are graded; the owners of the open tests keep the zero reads. Not graded: undefined bit numbers (never driven), the other source bits and the type bits 28 to 30 (logged), the EDN interrupt for bits 20 to 22 (logged), the state after the clear for the fatal-alert sources (logged), a pulse on the FIFO-error bits (logged), the `ERR_CODE` source bit of EDN bit 0 after a write-one clear (logged), the receiver range and the receiver index of an alert (owned by `sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test`; see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), and the enable gate of the fatal-error lines (owned by the extension leg of `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test`). The zero reads of `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` belong to the open entropy tests. The EDN command and the CSRNG instantiate paths belong to `sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`.

'''''

[[sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test]]
===== 1.49 `sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (CSRNG and EDN)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; VCS only (`tools = ["vcs"]`), because the reset readback must show no X

*Source Module*: `sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the CSRNG and EDN registers (32-bit accesses, approved); the VCS X check on the readback (approved); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). No inject. No PROPOSED item. Expected values come from the register text, the RDL reset values and the upstream register description (`regwen` binding) only.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the three targets, the values A and B of each target, the data on the inactive 32-bit lane of the 64-bit bus in each write (the strobe covers the register lane only), and the number r of re-set attempts (1 to 3). `reseed = 4` samples different orders, values, fills and attempts. The plan shape is the same on every seed: every seed walks the three targets. The leaf logs the plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: With CSRNG `REGWEN` (RW0C, reset 1) cleared, the CSRNG registers that the register description binds to it (`CTRL`, `FIPS_FORCE` and `ERR_CODE_TEST`) are read-only; this test grades `CTRL` and `FIPS_FORCE` (see the scope limit for `ERR_CODE_TEST`). With EDN `REGWEN` cleared, `CTRL` is read-only. A write of 1 does not set a cleared `REGWEN`. A reset restores it. Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc` (`REGWEN`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc` (`REGWEN`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/data/csrng.hjson` and `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/edn/data/edn.hjson` (the `regwen` binding of each register, the ruling source).

*Test Coverage*: On every seed the three targets CSRNG `CTRL`, CSRNG `FIPS_FORCE` and EDN `CTRL`, each with values A and B that differ from the reset value and from each other. Multi-bit-boolean fields take only True (6) or False (9). `ENABLE` and `EDN_ENABLE` stay False and `CMD_FIFO_RST` stays False, so no module starts and no FIFO clears. 1 to 3 re-set attempts per target. Error injection: none; a refused write is a defined response. Extends `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, which writes and reads back the unlocked storage registers and does not clear `REGWEN`. This test grades only the lock; the owner of the open test keeps the unlocked read and write. Functional coverage: `sep_crypto_reg_lock_cg` (`x_csrng_regwen`, `x_edn_regwen`), `sep_crypto_reset_cg` (`cp_reset_evidence`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan with `SepSeededRng`. For each target draw A and B so that A differs from the RDL reset value in at least one field, B differs from the RDL reset value in at least one field and B differs from A in at least one field. For CSRNG `CTRL` and EDN `CTRL` draw each multi-bit-boolean field of A and B from True and False, with `ENABLE`, `EDN_ENABLE` and `CMD_FIFO_RST` False. For CSRNG `FIPS_FORCE` draw A and B as non-zero 3-bit values that differ. For each write draw the data of the inactive 32-bit lane. Log `PLAN seed=<n>`.
. For each target in drawn order, run steps 3 to 11.
. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). Read `REGWEN` of the unit as 1. Read the target and compare it with its RDL reset value (no X).
. Write A to the target. Read the target and require A (a control, logged as `CTRL-REGWEN-PRE`).
. Write 0 to `REGWEN` of the unit. Read `REGWEN` and require 0.
. Write B to the target. Read the target and compare it with A.
. For the CSRNG targets only: write a drawn value that differs from the reset value to `INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE`. Read it and log both values as `OBS-REGWEN-OWN`. Nothing is graded.
. Write 1 to `REGWEN` of the unit, r times. Read `REGWEN` after each write and compare it with 0.
. Pulse the TRNG reset as in step 3. Read `REGWEN` of the unit and require 1.
. Read the target and compare it with its RDL reset value, with no X.
. Write B to the target. Read the target and require B.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-REGWEN-LOCK
|After 0 is written to `REGWEN` it reads 0, and a write of B to the target leaves A (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `REGWEN`: when false the registers become read-only; the `regwen` binding in `csrng.hjson` and `edn.hjson` names `CTRL`, `FIPS_FORCE` and `ERR_CODE_TEST` for CSRNG and `CTRL` for EDN). A DUT that takes the write, or that locks another register in place of the target, fails. Values A and B differ from the reset value and from each other, so a write that lands, a write that is lost and a reset value are all distinguishable. Anchor: readback of the target. Control: the write of A before the lock reads back A (`CTRL-REGWEN-PRE`). Evidence: `CHK-REGWEN-LOCK PASS seed=<n> unit=<csrng or edn> reg=<r> regwen=0 kept=0x<a> tried=0x<b> read=0x<a>`, and `CTRL-REGWEN-PRE seed=<n> unit=<u> reg=<r> wrote=0x<a> read=0x<a>`.
|CHK-REGWEN-NOREARM
|A write of 1 to a cleared `REGWEN` leaves it at 0 (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `REGWEN`: RW0C, so only a write of 0 changes it and only a reset sets it). A DUT that sets the bit again, which unlocks the registers, fails. The leaf repeats the write r times (1 to 3). Anchor: `REGWEN` readback after each write. Control: `REGWEN` reads 0 before the first write of 1. Evidence: `CHK-REGWEN-NOREARM PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> after_write1=0`.
|CHK-REGWEN-RESET
|After a TRNG reset `REGWEN` is 1, the target is at its reset value with no X, and a new write lands (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `REGWEN`: reset 0x1; `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc`, Reset: the TRNG reset is a full-IP reset of the entropy source and DRBG). A DUT that keeps the lock, keeps the old value A, leaves an X in the target or ignores the new write fails. Anchor: readback of `REGWEN` and of the target, with the VCS X check. Control: `REGWEN` reads 0 before the reset. Evidence: `CHK-REGWEN-RESET PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> regwen=1 reg=0x<reset> post_write_lands=1 x_free=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Only the lock is graded; the owner of the open test keeps the unlocked read and write. Not graded: `INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE` under the CSRNG `REGWEN` (the text is ambiguous; the leaf logs `OBS-REGWEN-OWN seed=<n> reg=INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE wrote=0x<b> read=0x<r>`), `RESEED_INTERVAL` and `INT_STATE_NUM` (blocked: the register text and the `regwen` binding differ, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), and `ERR_CODE_TEST` under a cleared `REGWEN` (the register is bound to `REGWEN`, but a write that the lock fails to refuse forces an error and a fatal alert, so this test does not write it; `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test` writes it only with `REGWEN` set and does not grade the lock). The CTRL values are graded by readback only and not as an alert contract, and the leaf writes only True and False values to them. The unlocked read and write of the storage registers belong to `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`.

'''''
[[sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test]]
===== 1.50 `sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (CSRNG and EDN)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, `reseed = 4`; Verilator and VCS; `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`

*Source Module*: `sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); the open entropy bring-up helper with its entropy golden (bring-up only: this test grades register state, not data). Probes: approved: CPU-LSU master readback of the EDN, CSRNG and `SEP_RESET_CTRL` registers; `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (vector bit 27 graded in the EDN software port leg and vector bit 23 graded in the CSRNG software instance leg; the other bits logged and not graded); `trng_reset_active_probe_o`. Injects: none beyond the command writes of the Test Procedure. No PROPOSED item.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Dimensions: the order of the boot group and the two software legs (the reference boot stays first inside the boot group); which software leg (EDN software port or CSRNG software instance) runs first; the idle gap between polls (0 to 16 clocks). The `INTR_ENABLE` bit of `edn_cmd_req_done` and of `cs_cmd_req_done` is 1 on every seed, because this leaf fills the (state 1, enable 1) cell of these two sources from a real event. The command words, the mode codes and the checked values do not depend on the seed. `reseed = 4` samples four leg orders and poll gaps. The leaf logs its plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: In boot request mode EDN reports `HW_CMD_STS.BOOT_MODE` = 1 and `AUTO_MODE` = 0, also when both `CTRL.BOOT_REQ_MODE` and `CTRL.AUTO_REQ_MODE` are set (boot mode takes precedence). With neither mode set EDN enters software port mode, where `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_REG_RDY`, `CMD_RDY` and `CMD_ACK` follow the stated handshake and a word written to `SW_CMD_REQ` reaches CSRNG. The instantiate word is the reset value of `BOOT_INS_CMD` (0x901). An instantiate sent to an instantiated state is ignored and sets `RECOV_ALERT_STS.CMD_STAGE_INVALID_CMD_SEQ_ALERT` (bit 14), which clears by a write of 0. Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc` (`CTRL`, `BOOT_INS_CMD`, `SW_CMD_REQ`, `SW_CMD_STS`, `HW_CMD_STS`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc` (`CMD_REQ`, `SW_CMD_STS`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS`); `hw/ip/drbg/doc/architecture.adoc` (OpenTitan CSRNG and EDN); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (source numbers).

*Test Coverage*: EDN boot mode with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` true and `AUTO_REQ_MODE` false (this boot also logs the boot time as an observation), and with both true, each on every seed. EDN software port (both modes false, the cell (false, false)): a valid instantiate, then a second instantiate as error injection. The CSRNG software instance: the same two commands. This entry extends `sep_esrc_e2e_smoke_test` and the other open entropy tests. Those tests bring EDN up in boot mode, grade the data chain, and read the CSRNG and EDN `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` as zero after a boot instantiate (`CHK-ALERTS-ZERO`). This entry grades only the cells that those tests do not grade: the boot-mode precedence, the software handshake and the repeated instantiate. The open owners keep the rest. Out of scope: auto mode and a software generate (the words are not stated); the `CMD_STS` codes, the acknowledge of an ignored command, EDN `CSRNG_ACK_ERR`, `HW_EXC_STS` and `CS_HW_INST_EXC` (logged only). Functional coverage: `sep_edn_mode_cg` (`x_mode_fields`, `cp_reported_mode`, `cp_sw_port_hs`), `sep_csrng_cmd_cg` (`x_inst_outcome`, `cp_sw_handshake`), `sep_crypto_alert_cg` (`cp_csrng_recov_seq`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_line_gate`, `cp_hw_raise` for `cs_cmd_req_done` and `edn_cmd_req_done`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set the baseline. Write `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` = 0x20 (TRNG released; Key Manager and every engine held in reset) and log it. Write `SEP_CPU_CTRL.EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` = 0x0 (internal source on all streams). Draw the plan and log it.
. Define the clean start used by every leg. Write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` and wait for the fall of `trng_reset_active_probe_o` (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). Bring up ESRC as the open entropy tests do. Write the CSRNG `CTRL` with `ENABLE` and `SW_APP_ENABLE` true (the multi-bit true code 6) and the other fields at their reset value. Read the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: bit 14 reads 0.
. Run three groups in the drawn order: the boot group (steps 4 and 5), the EDN software port leg (steps 6 to 11) and the CSRNG software instance leg (steps 12 to 16). Each group starts with the clean start of step 2.
. Reference boot. Run the clean start. Write the EDN `CTRL` with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` true (6), `AUTO_REQ_MODE` false (9) and `EDN_ENABLE` false (9), then write it again with `EDN_ENABLE` true (0x9969, then 0x9966). Count the clocks from the end of the `EDN_ENABLE` write until `HW_CMD_STS.BOOT_MODE` reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-BOOT`). Log the count as `t0` in `OBS-EDN-TSEED` (observation only).
. Graded boot, twice. Run the clean start. Write the EDN `CTRL` with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` true and `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true (6) in the first run (0x9669, then 0x9666), and with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` false in the second run (0x9969, then 0x9966), `EDN_ENABLE` true last in both. Poll `HW_CMD_STS` (with the drawn gaps) until `BOOT_MODE` reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-BOOT`). In that read expect `AUTO_MODE` = 0. Log the count of clocks.
. EDN software port leg. Run the clean start. Write the EDN `CTRL` with both modes false (9) and `EDN_ENABLE` true last (0x9999, then 0x9996). Write the EDN `INTR_ENABLE` with the `edn_cmd_req_done` bit 1. Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_REG_RDY` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`) and log both values.
. Write 0x901 (the instantiate word, the RDL reset value of `BOOT_INS_CMD`) to `SW_CMD_REQ`. Log `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_ACK` of the first read after the write in `OBS-EDN-ACK0` (not graded: a fast acknowledge can make it 1). Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_ACK` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`). Read `INTR_STATE`: expect `edn_cmd_req_done` = 1. Wait for vector bit 27 (source 28) to read 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`). Log `CMD_STS` in `OBS-EDN-STS` and vector bit 23 (source 24) in `OBS-EDN-VEC` (not graded). Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done`.
. Control for the injection. Read the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: expect bit 14 = 0 after the first command.
. Error injection: an instantiate to an instantiated instance. Point: `SW_CMD_REQ` of EDN. Kind: an out-of-order command (a second instantiate, word 0x901). Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_REG_RDY` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1 first. Write 0x901. Expected response: the command is ignored and CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14 sets. Poll the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` until bit 14 = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-INVSEQ`).
. Log the effects that the specification does not state: EDN `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_ACK` and `CMD_STS`, EDN `RECOV_ALERT_STS.CSRNG_ACK_ERR`, CSRNG `HW_EXC_STS`, CSRNG `INTR_STATE.cs_hw_inst_exc`, the vector bit 25 (source 26), and `CMD_RDY` afterwards, all in `OBS-EDN-INVSEQ` (not graded).
. Write 0 to the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`. Read it: expect bit 14 = 0.
. CSRNG software instance leg. Run the clean start (EDN stays disabled). Write the CSRNG `INTR_ENABLE` with the `cs_cmd_req_done` bit 1. Poll the CSRNG `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_RDY` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-CSRNG-SW`).
. Write 0x901 to the CSRNG `CMD_REQ`. Log `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_ACK` of the first read after the write in `OBS-CSRNG-ACK0` (not graded). Poll the CSRNG `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_ACK` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-CSRNG-SW`). Read the CSRNG `INTR_STATE`: expect `cs_cmd_req_done` = 1. Wait for vector bit 23 (source 24) to read 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-CSRNG-SW`). Log `CMD_STS` in `OBS-CSRNG-STS` (not graded). Write 1 to `cs_cmd_req_done`.
. Control for the injection. Read the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: expect bit 14 = 0 after the first command.
. Error injection: an instantiate to an instantiated instance. Point: `CMD_REQ` of CSRNG. Kind: an out-of-order command (a second instantiate, word 0x901). Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_RDY` = 1 first. Write 0x901. Expected response: the command is ignored and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14 sets. Poll until bit 14 = 1 (a bounded wait). Log `CMD_STS` and `HW_EXC_STS` in `OBS-CSRNG-INVSEQ` (not graded).
. Write 0 to the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`. Read it: expect bit 14 = 0.
. Log the `RESULT` line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-EDN-BOOT
|With `CTRL.BOOT_REQ_MODE` true and `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true or false, the read of `HW_CMD_STS` that first shows `BOOT_MODE` = 1 shows `AUTO_MODE` = 0, and `BOOT_MODE` = 1 shows within a bounded wait (`edn.adoc`, `CTRL`: boot mode takes precedence over auto mode; `HW_CMD_STS`). A DUT that gives auto mode precedence, reports `AUTO_MODE` = 1 in boot mode, or never enters boot mode (the wait times out), fails. The clock counts are logged and not graded. Anchor: `HW_CMD_STS` readback. Evidence: `OBS-EDN-TSEED seed=<n> tseed_clk=<t0>` and, for each graded boot, `CHK-EDN-BOOT PASS seed=<n> boot_mode=1 auto_mode=0 ctrl=0x<c> boot_clk=<t>`.
|CHK-EDN-SW-HS
|In software port mode `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_REG_RDY` and `CMD_RDY` read 1 before the write of 0x901 (`edn.adoc`: both reset to 0 and are polled before a write). After the write, within a bounded wait, `CMD_ACK` reads 1 and `CMD_RDY` reads 1, and `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done` reads 1 (`edn.adoc`: `CMD_ACK` goes high once CSRNG acknowledges the command). With `INTR_ENABLE.edn_cmd_req_done` = 1, vector bit 27 (source 28) rises to 1 within a bounded wait after the state reads 1 (`interrupts.adoc`, source N on bit N - 1). A DUT that never raises `CMD_REG_RDY` or `CMD_RDY`, never acknowledges, does not raise the interrupt state, or does not raise the line with the state and the enable at 1, fails. The `CMD_ACK` of the first read after the write is logged only: `CMD_ACK` resets to 0, so that read cannot separate a DUT that holds it high, and its value depends on the acknowledge timing that the specification does not state. Anchor: `SW_CMD_STS` and `INTR_STATE` readback. Evidence: `CHK-EDN-SW-HS PASS seed=<n> cmd_reg_rdy=1 cmd_rdy=1 ack_done=1 rdy_done=1 edn_done=1 enable=1 vec27=1`; `OBS-EDN-ACK0 seed=<n> ack_first_read=<a>`.
|CHK-EDN-INVSEQ
|The second 0x901 through EDN sets CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14, and a write of 0 clears it (`csrng.adoc`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: an instantiate to an instantiated state sets the bit; writing zero resets it). A DUT that accepts the second instantiate silently, or does not clear the bit, fails. Anchor: CSRNG register readback. Control: bit 14 reads 0 after the first command, in the same log line, so the 1 follows the second command. Evidence: `CHK-EDN-INVSEQ PASS seed=<n> ctrl_first_invseq=0 csrng_invseq=1 cleared_by_write0=1`; the unstated effects go to `OBS-EDN-INVSEQ seed=<n> cmd_ack=<a> cmd_sts=<s> ack_err=<e> hw_exc=0x<h> hw_inst_exc=<i> vec25=<v> cmd_rdy_after=<r>`.
|CHK-CSRNG-SW
|For the CSRNG software instance `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_RDY` reads 1 before the first 0x901 (`csrng.adoc`: it resets to 0 and is polled before a write). After the write, within a bounded wait, `CMD_ACK` reads 1 and `CMD_RDY` reads 1, and `INTR_STATE.cs_cmd_req_done` = 1; the second 0x901 sets `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14 (`csrng.adoc`, `SW_CMD_STS`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS`). With `INTR_ENABLE.cs_cmd_req_done` = 1, vector bit 23 (source 24) rises to 1 within a bounded wait after the state reads 1 (`interrupts.adoc`, source N on bit N - 1). A DUT that does not become ready, does not acknowledge the first command, takes the second one without the alert, or does not raise the line with the state and the enable at 1, fails. The `CMD_ACK` of the first read after the write is logged only, for the reason given in `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`. Anchor: CSRNG register readback. Control: bit 14 reads 0 after the first command. Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-SW PASS seed=<n> cmd_rdy=1 ack=1 rdy_done=1 done=1 enable=1 vec23=1 ctrl_first_invseq=0 invseq=1`; `OBS-CSRNG-ACK0 seed=<n> ack_first_read=<a>`; the unstated effects go to `OBS-CSRNG-INVSEQ seed=<n> cmd_sts=<s> hw_exc=0x<h>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The leaf does not claim auto mode or a software generate, because the specification states no reseed word and no generate word for them. It does not grade the `CMD_STS` code of any command, the acknowledge of an ignored command, EDN `CSRNG_ACK_ERR`, `HW_EXC_STS` or `CS_HW_INST_EXC`: the text states only that bit 14 sets and that the command is ignored. Of `RECOV_ALERT_STS` only bit 14 is graded. The leaf uses the reset values of `BOOT_INS_CMD` and `BOOT_GEN_CMD` and drives no `clen` other than 0. It does not grade the reset value of `BOOT_INS_CMD`, the boot time, or the `CMD_ACK` value of the first read after a command write. It drives no multi-bit boolean value other than the true and false codes and does not write `CMD_FIFO_RST` (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The open entropy tests keep the data chain and the `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` zero check after a boot instantiate (`CHK-ALERTS-ZERO`).

'''''

[[sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test]]
===== 1.51 `sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (TRNG and entropy pool)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, `reseed = 4`; external stream driver (added hook, top-port drive), external stream response monitor and internal DRBG endpoint probe (observation taps); Verilator and VCS; `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`

*Source Module*: `sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master). Probes and injects. Approved: CPU-LSU master readback (select, lock, pool `STATUS` and `DATA`); `axis1_*`, `axis2_*` and `km_entropy_*`; `crypto_edn_req_o[i]`, `crypto_edn_ack_o[i]` and `crypto_edn_fips_o[i]`; `pool_edn_ack_o` and `pool_edn_fips_o`; `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o`; `trng_reset_active_probe_o`; the TRNG reset bit of `SW_RESET_N`; the `rst_ni` pulse. Added hook: the external stream driver, a top-port drive of `ext_trng_axis_req_i[2:0]` (with a source reset) and of `ext_trng_irq_i`, which the test adds to the port table of the HDL Top section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. The external stream response monitor (`ext_trng_axis_rsp_o[2:0]`) and the internal DRBG endpoint probe (`drbg_int_axis_req[i]`, `drbg_int_axis_rsp[i]`) are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The checks `CHK-SEL-RW`, `CHK-SEL-LOCK`, `CHK-SEL-LOCKFIRST` and `CHK-SEL-GLOBAL` use approved items only. `CHK-SEL-HOLD` drives no external beat and uses the endpoint tap. Every other check uses the external stream driver (`CHK-EXT-IRQ` uses its `ext_trng_irq_i` driver).

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Dimensions and ranges: the order of streams 1 and 2 (both run on every seed); the beat count of the transfer leg (stream 1: 8 to 96; stream 2: an even number from 8 to 64, so that the empty 32-entry pool takes every beat); the gap pattern (back-to-back, random gaps of 0 to 20 clocks, or one long gap); the partial strobe (0x0 to 0xE); the partial-beat hold H (16 to 64 clocks); the hold window of the hold leg (64 to 256 clocks); the beat words (unique, non-zero, never equal to a neighbour); the select values d (any value other than the value held before the write: 0x7 on the first pass), s (the bit of the stream under test set, the other bits drawn, and not equal to d), a (not 0x7) and b (not a); the idle gap between the select write and the lock write (0 to 32 clocks); the initial pool fill (0 to 32 pairs); the random operation sequence (20 to 60 operations, in addition to the fixed corner run of the pop golden leg) and the feed positions. `reseed = 4` samples four sets of these draws. The leaf logs its plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: The external stream has `tvalid`, `tready`, 32-bit `tdata`, 4-bit `tstrb` and no `tuser`; a source holds `tvalid` and `tdata` until `tready`. The mux gives each external beat a `tuser` of 1. The selected sink receives each accepted word unchanged and in order. The crypto and pool adapters accept only strobe 0xF, and a partial strobe stalls them. `ext_trng_irq_i` is routed to PIC source 17. `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL.sel` resets to 0b111. Bit N selects the source of stream N (0 Key Manager, 1 crypto, 2 pool), 0 for the internal source and 1 for the external source. `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK.lock` (RW1S) freezes the select until the CPU-control bank resets, and a TRNG reset alone does not clear it. An unselected external input receives ready high and its beats are discarded. An unselected internal endpoint is back-pressured and keeps its queued data. A 64-bit read of the pool `DATA` pops one word. Spec: `hw/sys/sep/doc/trng.adoc` (Distribution and Source Selection); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc` (`EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL`, `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (source 17); `hw/sys/sep/doc/entropy_pool.adoc` (Access Policy); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_entropy_pool.adoc` (`STATUS`, `DATA`).

*Test Coverage*: Streams 1 and 2, each with a drawn select value and then a select with the stream external. Full-strobe beats with drawn gap patterns; beats with `tstrb` 0x0 to 0xE as error injection (a partial strobe), each followed by a TRNG reset with a source reset of the driver and then a 0xF beat. The select bit of the stream cleared while beats arrive. All three streams with back-to-back beats under the drawn select bits. The pool filled to 32 entries with pairs {a, a}, then popped; a drawn fill, sequence of pops, `STATUS` reads and feeds. The internal endpoint held while the external source is selected, then reselected. `ext_trng_irq_i` high and low. The lock after a select write, before any select write, and after a `rst_ni` pulse. This entry extends `sep_trng_reset_recovery_test`, which grades that a non-reset select survives a TRNG reset; `sep_entropy_pool_aperture_test`, which grades the pool access shapes and `IRQ_CAUSE`; and `sep_address_map_test` and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, which read the reset values of the select and the lock. This entry grades only the cells that those tests do not grade: the readback of drawn select values (the open test reads back one fixed non-reset value), the lock, the external stream and a pool pop with a driven golden. The open owners keep the rest. Out of scope: a source that drops `tvalid` of a live beat, an idle selected stream with a waiting consumer, the packing order inside a pair, and the `tready` fall of stream 0 (logged only). Functional coverage: `sep_entropy_delivery_cg` (`x_sel_stream`, `cp_lock_order`, `x_ext_beat`, `cp_unselected_drop`, `cp_ext_stall`, `cp_hold_internal`, `cp_pool_pop`, `cp_ext_irq`, `cp_ext_tuser`), `sep_crypto_reset_cg` (`cp_reset_evidence`, bin `cpu_lock_survives`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set the baseline. Read `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` and `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK` as the first accesses of the leaf, before any write, and log them in `OBS-SEL-RESET` (the open tests grade the reset values). Write `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` = 0x24 (TRNG and AES released; Key Manager and the other engines held in reset) and log it. The baseline of every TRNG reset below is this value.
. Run steps 3 to 12 once for stream 1 and once for stream 2, in the drawn order. Then run steps 13 to 16 once.
. Reset the TRNG domain: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` and wait for the fall of `trng_reset_active_probe_o` (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). The internal chain stays idle, because ESRC, CSRNG and EDN are not initialized, and the post-mux adapters and the pool are empty.
. Select leg. Write the select value d and read it back: expect d. Write the select value s (the bit of the stream under test set, the other bits drawn) and read it back: expect s. Steps 5 to 12 use s unless a step says otherwise.
. Transfer leg. For stream 1 trigger one AES PRNG reseed, so that a client request is pending on `crypto_edn_req_o[0]`. Offer the drawn number of beats with `tstrb` = 0xF and the drawn gap pattern, one beat at a time; the driver holds `tvalid` and `tdata` until `tready`. For stream 1 the leg also ends when a beat stays pending for H clocks (the adapter is full). Expect every beat that completes at the external port to appear at the post-mux probe (`axis1_*` or `axis2_*`) in the same order and with the same data, at least one beat to complete, and `edn_fips` = 1 for each word that the consuming adapter accepts (`crypto_edn_ack_o[0]` with `crypto_edn_fips_o[0]`, or `pool_edn_ack_o` with `pool_edn_fips_o`).
. Strobe leg: the control and the error injection. Reset the TRNG domain (step 3) so that the adapter is empty. Control: for stream 2 read `STATUS.fifo_level`, send one pair {a, a} with both beats at 0xF and expect `fifo_level` to rise by 1; for stream 1 send one 0xF beat, expect it to complete, and then reset the TRNG domain again (step 3), so that the adapter is empty before the partial beat. Error injection: a partial strobe. Point: `ext_trng_axis_req_i.tstrb` of the stream under test. Kind: a beat that the adapter does not accept (a legal source beat with a `tstrb` value from 0x0 to 0xE). For stream 2 send a pair whose first beat is 0xF and whose second beat is the partial beat; for stream 1 send the partial beat. Expected response: a stalled adapter. Sample the post-mux probe for H clocks: expect `tvalid` = 1 and `tready` = 0 at every clock, and for stream 2 expect `fifo_level` unchanged from its value before the partial pair.
. Recover from the stall. Write 0 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N`. At the rise of `trng_reset_active_probe_o` apply the source's own reset to the driver; the sink reset and the source reset happen together, the driver forgets the held partial beat, and a running source does not drop `tvalid`. Log it. Write 1 and wait for the fall of the probe. Send one 0xF beat (stream 2: one 0xF pair) and expect it to complete. The select and the lock stay as written.
. Drop leg. Write s with the bit of the stream under test cleared and read it back. Send the drawn number of beats with `tstrb` = 0xF. Expect `tready` = 1 at every clock of the leg, no word of these beats at the post-mux probe, and for stream 2 `fifo_level` unchanged. Control: the transfer leg of step 5 completed beats in the same seed with the select bit set.
. Back-pressure leg (stream 2). Write s. Reset the TRNG domain (step 3). Offer pairs {a, a} with unique a, one beat at a time, until a beat stays pending for H clocks with `tready` low; a bound of 64 pairs (a test parameter) applies, and reaching it without a stall fails `CHK-EXT-BP`. Log N_cal, the number of beats accepted before the stall. At the stall read `STATUS`: expect `fifo_level` = 32. Then pop `DATA` until every pair that completed at the external port, and the pending pair, has been popped. Before each pop poll `STATUS` until `fifo_level` is at least 1 (a bounded wait). Expect each pop to answer OKAY and the pairs to appear once each and in the order sent.
. Ready leg. Write s. Reset the TRNG domain (step 3). Drive all three external streams with back-to-back valid beats (full strobe, unique words). For stream 1 and stream 2 with the select bit 1 and no sink draining, continue until a beat stays pending for H clocks with `tready` low, with a bound of 2000 accepted beats (a test parameter); reaching the bound without a fall fails `CHK-SEL-READY`. Log the accepted beats N_i. For stream 2 expect at least 64 accepted beats before `tready` first falls (32 pool entries of two beats each). For stream 1, after the fall write `AES.TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED` = 1 and expect the pending beat to complete (`tready` rises) within a bounded wait (a timeout fails `CHK-SEL-READY`). For stream 0 with the select bit 1 log `tready` in `OBS-SEL-READY0` and grade nothing.
. Hold leg. Bring up ESRC, CSRNG and EDN as the open entropy tests do, so that the internal endpoints fill. The bring-up writes `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` itself, so write 0x7 (every stream external) after it and read it back: expect 0x7. For stream 2 pop `DATA` until `STATUS.fifo_level` = 0 (a bounded wait; the internal bring-up can fill the pool, and an empty pool can take words). The external driver stays silent for the whole leg. Wait until the internal endpoint probe of the stream under test shows `tvalid` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-SEL-HOLD`) and record the head word w. Sample the endpoint for the hold window: expect `tvalid` = 1, `tready` = 0 and `tdata` = w at every clock. Write 0x7 with the bit of the stream under test cleared (the internal source for that stream); for stream 1 write `AES.TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED` = 1 so that a consumer is active (`aes.adoc`, `TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED`: a reseed from the connected entropy source), and for stream 2 the pool is the consumer. Expect the first beat that completes at the post-mux probe to carry w. In a bounded wait that starts at the response of the reselect write and ends at the timeout, expect at least one beat to drain for stream 1 and at least two beats (one pool entry of two beats) for stream 2, and log the count of drained beats (control: with the internal source selected and a consumer active the endpoint drains).
. Pop golden leg (stream 2 only). Reset the TRNG domain (step 3), so that only the test fills the pool. Write the select with bit 2 set and bit 1 clear. Draw the initial fill L (0 to 32 pairs {a, a}) and send it. Poll `STATUS` until two consecutive reads agree and expect `fifo_level` = L. Then run the corner run, then the drawn random operations. Each operation is a `DATA` pop (64-bit read at offset 0x10), a `STATUS` read (offset 0x0) or a feed of one pair. The corner run is a run of feeds to model level 32, one feed offered at level 32 (the pair can complete at the external port into the adapter or stay pending; the pool level stays 32 until a pop) and then a pop; it has 34 - L operations or more, and it does not count toward the drawn 20 to 60. A pop at model level 0 is replaced by a `STATUS` read. In the drawn operations a feed at model level 32 is also replaced by a `STATUS` read, so that no pair is staged above a full pool behind another staged pair (the specification states no staging depth); the corner run holds the only feed offered at level 32. After each operation wait for its settle event (the second beat of a feed completes at the external port, or, for a feed offered at level 32, either that or a beat that stays pending for H clocks with `tready` low; the response of a pop returns, and the completion of a pending feed if one exists), then read `STATUS` until two consecutive reads agree. The model level is the smaller of 32 and the pairs completed at the external port minus the pops; a pair above 32 is staged in the adapter and enters the pool at a later pop. Expect each pop to answer OKAY with the next pair in order, and the settled `fifo_level` to equal the model level.
. IRQ leg. Read the vector probe: expect bit 16 = 0 (control). Drive `ext_trng_irq_i` = 1 and expect vector bit 16 = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EXT-IRQ`). Drive `ext_trng_irq_i` = 0 and expect vector bit 16 = 0 (a bounded wait).
. Lock leg. Write a select value a (not 0x7) and read it back: expect a (control: the pre-lock write lands). Wait the drawn idle gap, then write 1 to `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK` and read it: expect 1. Write 0 to the lock and read it: expect 1. Write a select value b (not a) and read the select: expect a. Reset the TRNG domain (step 3). Read the lock: expect 1. Read the select: expect a.
. Global reset leg. Pulse `rst_ni` (assert, release, and wait until the CPU-LSU master reads again as the open tests do). Read the lock: expect 0. Read the select: expect 0x7. Control: the lock read 1 and the select read a, with a not 0x7, before the pulse.
. Lock-first leg. Write 1 to the lock before any select write and read it: expect 1. Write the select 0x0 and read the select: expect 0x7. Pulse `rst_ni`. Read the lock: expect 0. Write the select 0x0 and read it: expect 0x0 (control: the write lands when the lock is clear).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-SEL-RW
|A drawn select value (d and s) reads back as written (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`, `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL.sel`, RW). A DUT that ignores the write, or stores another value, fails. Anchor: register readback. Evidence: `CHK-SEL-RW PASS seed=<n> wrote=0x<w> read=0x<w>` for each write; `OBS-SEL-RESET seed=<n> sel=0x<s> lock=<l>` for the reset read.
|CHK-EXT-XFER
|Every beat that completes at the external port of the stream appears at the post-mux probe in the same order and with the same data, and the `edn_fips` bit of each word that the consuming adapter accepts is 1 (`trng.adoc`: the external mux supplies `tuser` = 1; the stream contract table). A DUT that loses, reorders or alters a word, or tags it `edn_fips` = 0, fails. At least one beat completes and at least one accepted word is graded. Anchor: external stream response monitor (an observation tap), `axis1_*`, `axis2_*`, `crypto_edn_fips_o[i]` with `crypto_edn_ack_o[i]`, `pool_edn_fips_o` with `pool_edn_ack_o`. Evidence: `CHK-EXT-XFER PASS seed=<n> stream=<i> beats=<n> xfers=<n> order_ok=1 fips=1 fips_words=<k>`.
|CHK-EXT-STRB
|A beat with a partial strobe (0x0 to 0xE) stays pending at the mux output (`tvalid` 1, `tready` 0) for the hold H, and a pair with a partial second beat leaves `fifo_level` unchanged (`trng.adoc`: the crypto and pool adapters accept only 0xF; partial strobes stall them). A DUT that accepts the partial beat shows `tready` 1 or a level change. After the TRNG reset and the source reset a 0xF beat completes. Control: a 0xF pair sent first raises `fifo_level` by 1 (stream 1: a 0xF beat completes, and a TRNG reset then empties the adapter before the partial beat), so the stall comes from the strobe and not from a full adapter. Anchor: post-mux probe and pool `STATUS`. Evidence: `CHK-EXT-STRB PASS seed=<n> stream=<i> tstrb=0x<s> pending=1 hold_clk=<H> pool_delta=<0\|na> ctrl_f_delta=<d> source_reset=1 post_reset_f_beat=1`.
|CHK-EXT-DROP
|With the select bit of the stream cleared every beat sees `tready` 1, the post-mux probe shows none of them, and `fifo_level` does not change (`trng.adoc`: an unselected external input receives ready high and its beats are discarded). A DUT that holds `tready` low, or passes a beat on, fails. Control: the transfer leg of the same stream and seed, with the bit set, delivers beats. Anchor: port monitor (an observation tap), post-mux probe and `STATUS`. Evidence: `CHK-EXT-DROP PASS seed=<n> stream=<i> sel_bit=0 tready_low=0 post_mux_beats=0 pool_delta=0 ctrl_xfers=<c>`.
|CHK-EXT-BP
|With the pool full (`fifo_level` 32) `tready` falls with a beat pending; after the pops every pair appears once and in order (`trng.adoc`: the selected source receives the ready of the sink; the pool holds 32 entries of 64 bits). A DUT that keeps `tready` high at a full pool loses or duplicates a pair; a DUT that stalls at a lower level fails the level read. Anchor: port monitor (an observation tap) and `DATA` readback. Evidence: `CHK-EXT-BP PASS seed=<n> stream=2 tready_low_cycles=<c> level_at_stall=32 lost=0 dup=0 order_ok=1`.
|CHK-EXT-IRQ
|`ext_trng_irq_i` = 1 gives vector bit 16 = 1, and 0 gives 0 (`interrupts.adoc`: source 17 is the external TRNG interrupt; source N is on vector bit N - 1). A DUT with the line unconnected or inverted fails one of the two reads. Control: bit 16 reads 0 before the drive. Anchor: vector probe. Evidence: `CHK-EXT-IRQ PASS seed=<n> irq_i=<1\|0> vec16=<1\|0>` for each drive.
|CHK-SEL-READY
|For streams 1 and 2 with the select bit 1 and the sink not draining, `tready` falls with a beat pending; for stream 2 it stays high for at least 64 accepted beats (32 pool entries of two beats); for stream 1 an AES PRNG reseed after the fall completes the pending beat (`tready` rises) within a bounded wait (`trng.adoc`: the selected source receives the ready of the sink; `entropy_pool.adoc`; `aes.adoc`, `TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED`). A DUT that ties `tready` to 1 for a selected stream, or that keeps it low when the sink drains, fails. The unselected input (ready high, beats discarded) is graded by CHK-EXT-DROP; the rise for stream 2 is graded by CHK-EXT-BP. Control: the first beat of the stream is accepted with `tready` high before the fall, so the fall is not a port that is held low. Anchor: port monitor (an observation tap). Evidence: `CHK-SEL-READY PASS seed=<n> stream=<i> sel_bit=1 tready_low_cycles=<c> accepted=<N_i> ncal=<N_cal> rise_after_drain=<1\|na>`.
|CHK-SEL-HOLD
|With the external source selected and the internal endpoint filled, the endpoint holds `tvalid` 1, `tready` 0 and stable `tdata` for the hold window; after the reselect the held word is delivered first (`trng.adoc`: an unselected internal endpoint is back-pressured and retains queued data; selection has no automatic flush). A DUT that drains or flushes the endpoint, or changes the head word, fails. Control: with the internal source selected and a consumer active the endpoint drains. Anchor: internal DRBG endpoint probe (an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list) and the post-mux probe. Evidence: `CHK-SEL-HOLD PASS seed=<n> stream=<i> tvalid=1 tready=0 tdata_changes=0 first_after_reselect==held=1 ctrl_drain_beats=<b>`.
|CHK-SEL-LOCK
|After 1 is written to `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK`, a write of 0 leaves it at 1, a write of another select value leaves the old value, and a TRNG reset leaves the lock at 1 (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`: `lock` is RW1S and holds until reset; `trng.adoc`: a TRNG reset alone does not unlock). A DUT that clears the lock, accepts the later select write, or clears the lock at the TRNG reset fails. Control: the pre-lock select write landed, and the select reads unchanged after the TRNG reset (control of the open check). Anchor: register readback. Evidence: `CHK-SEL-LOCK PASS seed=<n> lock=1 write0_lock=1 sel_kept=0x<a> tried=0x<b> after_trng_reset_lock=1 ctrl_sel=0x<a> pre_lock_sel=0x<a>`.
|CHK-SEL-LOCKFIRST
|With the lock set before any select write, a select write leaves the select at 0x7 (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`: the lock prevents selection changes). A DUT that takes the write fails. Control: after a `rst_ni` pulse the lock is 0 and a select write lands. Anchor: register readback. Evidence: `CHK-SEL-LOCKFIRST PASS seed=<n> lock=1 write=0x0 sel=0x7 ctrl_after_reset_write_lands=1`.
|CHK-SEL-GLOBAL
|After a `rst_ni` pulse the lock is 0 and the select is 0x7 (`sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc`: reset values). Before the pulse the lock reads 1 and the select reads a value other than 0x7, so a reset that leaves either one fails. Control: the lock read 1 before the pulse. Anchor: register readback. Evidence: `CHK-SEL-GLOBAL PASS seed=<n> lock=0 sel=0x7 pre_lock=1 pre_sel=0x<a>`.
|CHK-POOL-DATA
|Each pop of a non-empty pool answers OKAY with the next driven pair in order, and the settled `fifo_level` equals the model level, the smaller of 32 and the pairs completed at the external port minus the pops, after every operation (`entropy_pool.adoc`, Access Policy; `sep_entropy_pool.adoc`, `STATUS`, `DATA`; `reset_controller.adoc`: the post-mux adapters hold buffered state, so a pair above 32 is staged and not in the pool). A DUT that pops twice, returns another word, loses a staged pair, or counts wrongly fails. The corner cells are a feed that brings the level to 32 followed at once by a pop, and a feed offered at level 32 (the level stays 32 and the pair appears in order after later pops). Control: the model level rises only on feeds that the leaf made, because the TRNG reset before the leg leaves only test data in the pool. Anchor: `DATA` and `STATUS` readback. Evidence: `CHK-POOL-DATA PASS seed=<n> pop=<k> data=0x<w><w> expect=0x<w><w> level_before=<a> level_after=<b> expect_level=<b>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The leaf does not claim a source that drops `tvalid` of a live beat, an idle selected stream with a waiting consumer, the packing order inside a pair (pairs are {a, a}), or the `tready` fall of stream 0. It does not claim the pool access-shape cells (half-word, unmapped and write accesses, empty pop) or `IRQ_CAUSE`: `sep_entropy_pool_aperture_test` owns them. The reset values of the select and the lock belong to `sep_address_map_test` and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`; the survival of a non-reset select across a TRNG reset belongs to `sep_trng_reset_recovery_test`. The tuser of stream 1 is graded only for words that a client accepts. The source reset of the driver that goes with a TRNG reset is the source's own reset after a stall, set-up and not a claim.

'''''

[[sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test]]
===== 1.52 `sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (TRNG and entropy pool)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, no `+esrc_noise_force`, `reseed = 4`; external stream driver (added hook, top-port drive); Verilator and VCS; `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`. The leaf takes the words of its AES, KMAC and OTBN operations from the external stream, selects the external source and leaves ESRC, CSRNG and EDN uninitialized.

*Source Module*: `sep_trng_ext_sink_reset_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); independent Python references for the AES and KMAC goldens. Probes and injects. Approved: CPU-LSU master readback (pool `STATUS` and `DATA`, OTBN DMEM, AES `DATA_OUT`, KMAC `STATE`); `trng_reset_active_probe_o`; `crypto_edn_req_o[i]` and `crypto_edn_ack_o[i]` (client 0 AES, 1 KMAC, 2 OTBN RND, 3 OTBN URND); `axis1_*`, `axis2_*` and `km_entropy_*`; the TRNG reset bit of `SW_RESET_N`. Added hook: the external stream driver, a top-port drive of `ext_trng_axis_req_i[2:0]` with a source reset, which the test adds to the port table of the HDL Top section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. The external stream response monitor on `ext_trng_axis_rsp_o[2:0]` is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. Every check of this entry uses the driven external words.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Dimensions and ranges: the reset moment (an offset of 0 to the pool fill time T_fill, in clocks, after the beat stream restarts); the extra reset hold after the pool read returns (0 to 200 clocks); the fill count N (4 to 24 pairs); the count M of new pairs (2 to 8); the client order of the pre-reset leg and of the consumer trials; the delay between the request of a client and the TRNG reset in a trial (0 to 32 clocks); the beat words (unique and non-zero) and the AES and KMAC data. `reseed = 4` samples four sets of these draws. The leaf logs its plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: While the TRNG reset is active, the post-mux crypto and pool adapters and the pool clear, also when external inputs are selected. External traffic pauses during the clear and resumes when it ends, independently of the internal generators. The pool aperture stays responsive. An AES, KMAC or OTBN request that is pending during the clear gets no acknowledge and is served with fresh data afterwards. Spec: `hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc` (Resetting the TRNG); `hw/sys/sep/doc/trng.adoc` (Reset and Recovery Boundaries); `hw/sys/sep/doc/entropy_pool.adoc`.

*Test Coverage*: The external source on the crypto and pool streams, with the Key Manager stream idle and the Key Manager in reset. A pool fill of 4 to 24 pairs, with beats offered on both the crypto and the pool stream across the reset. AES, KMAC, OTBN RND and OTBN URND served from the external words. A TRNG reset at a drawn moment with the pool non-empty and beats arriving. A pool `STATUS` read issued and answered inside the clear window. M new pairs after the release. Four consumer trials on every seed (AES, KMAC, OTBN RND, OTBN URND), each with a request pending across a TRNG reset. This entry extends `sep_trng_reset_recovery_test`, which drives the reset with the internal source. This entry grades only the cells that the open test does not grade: the adapter clear with an external source, and the external source selected for a sink. The open owner keeps the rest. Out of scope: the word order inside an OTBN RND value (sets are compared); the isolation phase before the reset (it is not the clear window). Functional coverage: `sep_crypto_reset_cg` (`cp_clear_ext_sel`, `cp_xfer_phase`, `cp_pool_in_clear`, `cp_pool_scrub` non-zero cell, `cp_client_in_clear`), `sep_entropy_delivery_cg` (`x_sel_stream`, `x_ext_beat`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set the baseline. Write `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` = 0x36 (TRNG, KMAC, AES and OTBN released; Key Manager, HMAC and ABR held in reset) and log it. Every TRNG reset below changes only bit 5 from this value. Write `SEP_CPU_CTRL.EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` = 0x6 (streams 1 and 2 external; stream 0 internal and idle). Do not initialize ESRC, CSRNG or EDN. Draw the plan and log it.
. Pre-reset client leg, for each client in the drawn order. The driver supplies unique words on stream 1 for the whole operation; the sent set S is every word that the driver has offered. For AES: select ECB with a fixed table key, `PRNG_RESEED_RATE` PER_1, and encrypt a drawn number of blocks. For KMAC: hash a fixed-table message with the entropy source set to EDN. For OTBN RND: run an OTBN program that reads RND a drawn number of times and writes the values to DMEM. For OTBN URND: run an OTBN program that reads URND a drawn number of times. Expect each word that the client accepts (`crypto_edn_ack_o[i]` with the beat probe), and every 32-bit word of each OTBN RND value, to be a member of S, and the AES ciphertext and the KMAC digest to equal the independent golden. Log the client and the counts.
. Pool fill. Reset the TRNG domain (write 0 then 1 to bit 5 and wait for the fall of `trng_reset_active_probe_o`), so that the adapters and the pool are empty. The driver sends N pairs {a, a} with unique a on stream 2, one beat at a time, and it also offers unique words on stream 1, so both streams are active across the reset on every seed. Log t_xfer, the clocks of one clean handshake of the first beat on an empty adapter (observation only), and T_fill, the clocks until the 2N-th beat completes. Poll the pool `STATUS` until two consecutive reads agree and expect `fifo_level` = N. Log N as `level_before`.
. Restart the beat stream. The driver offers pairs {a, a} (new unique values) on stream 2 and unique words on stream 1, one beat at a time. Read the pool `STATUS` and log `fifo_level` as `pre_reset_level` (at least 1). After the drawn offset write 0 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N`.
. Wait for the rise of `trng_reset_active_probe_o` (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). At the rise the driver applies its source's own reset together with the sink reset: it forgets every beat it held and any partial pair, and a running source does not drop `tvalid`. Log it. From then on every beat that the driver offers belongs to the M new pairs. The driver offers the first beat of the first new pair on each active stream and holds it.
. Pool read in the clear. While the probe is high, issue one pool `STATUS` read. Wait for its response (a bounded wait; a hang fails `CHK-XRST-POOL`). Sample the probe at the request and at the response. Sample the external port at every clock of the window. Hold the reset for the drawn extra hold after the response. Log `read_clk` and the window length.
. Write 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` and wait for the fall of the probe (a bounded wait). Do not set up ESRC, CSRNG or EDN. Expect the first transfer on each active stream to complete (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-XRST-RESUME`).
. After the release the driver sends all M new pairs (and unique words on stream 1). Wait until the monitor shows 2M beats complete on stream 2. Poll `STATUS` until two consecutive reads agree and expect `fifo_level` = M. Pop `DATA` M times: expect each pop to answer OKAY and the pairs to be the M new pairs in order, and none of the N pre-reset pairs.
. Consumer trials, for each client in the drawn order. Reset the TRNG domain (write 0 then 1 to bit 5 and wait for the fall of the probe), so that the adapters are empty. Start the client operation as in step 2. For KMAC the operation starts with a write of `CMD.entropy_req` = 1 while KMAC is idle, so that KMAC requests a new seed in the trial (`kmac.hjson`, `CMD.entropy_req`: manual reseed in EDN mode; KMAC took its first seed in step 2). For AES, KMAC and OTBN URND the driver stays silent. For OTBN RND the driver offers unique words, one beat at a time: it offers a beat only on a clock with `crypto_edn_req_o[3]` (OTBN URND) high, `crypto_edn_ack_o[3]` low and `crypto_edn_req_o[2]` low, and the next beat only after `crypto_edn_ack_o[3]` of the previous one, so no spare word stays in the four-entry staging FIFO of the crypto adapter (`hw/sys/sep/doc/trng.adoc`, Distribution and Source Selection). It stays silent otherwise. These words belong to S. The leaf logs whether the request of client 3 came before the request of client 2 as `OBS-XRST-URNDFIRST`. Wait until `crypto_edn_req_o[i]` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-XRST-OTBN`). After the drawn delay write 0 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N`. The trial counts only if `crypto_edn_req_o[i]` = 1 and `crypto_edn_ack_o[i]` has not risen since its request at the rise of the probe; otherwise log `OBS-XRST-SERVED` and repeat the trial (at most 4 repeats, a test parameter; reaching the bound fails `CHK-XRST-OTBN`).
. At the rise of the probe sample `crypto_edn_req_o[i]` and `crypto_edn_ack_o[i]` at every clock of the window: expect the request to stay 1 and no acknowledge. Hold the reset for the drawn extra hold, write 1 and wait for the fall of the probe. The driver then sends unique post-clear words Q for the client. Wait until the client completes (a bounded wait). Expect the acknowledge to rise after the release, every word that the client accepts after the release to be a member of Q and not of S, every OTBN RND word to be a member of Q, and the AES ciphertext and the KMAC digest to equal their goldens. Control: step 2 shows an acknowledge for the same client.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-XRST-POOL
|A pool `STATUS` read that is issued and answered while `trng_reset_active_probe_o` is high returns OKAY and `fifo_level` 0 (`reset_controller.adoc`: the pool clears its FIFO contents while the TRNG reset is active and its AXI responder stays accessible). A DUT that keeps the pool contents, or hangs the read, fails. The leaf samples the probe at the request and at the response. Control: `fifo_level` = N before the reset. Anchor: `STATUS` readback and `trng_reset_active_probe_o`. Evidence: `CHK-XRST-POOL PASS seed=<n> level_before=<l> level_in_clear=0 resp=OKAY req_in_window=1 resp_in_window=1 window_clk=<w> read_clk=<r>`.
|CHK-XRST-PAUSE
|No transfer completes at any active external stream while `trng_reset_active_probe_o` is high (`trng.adoc`: external crypto and pool traffic pauses during the adapter clear). The driver holds `tvalid` = 1 on the stream during the window, so the check does not pass on an idle port. A DUT that accepts a beat in the window fails. Control: transfers complete before and after the window in the same seed. Anchor: port monitor (an observation tap) and the probe. Evidence: `CHK-XRST-PAUSE PASS seed=<n> stream=<i> xfers_in_clear=0 tvalid_in_clear=1 clear_clk=<c> xfers_before=<b> xfers_after=<a>`.
|CHK-XRST-RESUME
|Transfers resume after the window with no ESRC, CSRNG or EDN set-up, within a bounded wait counted from the fall of the probe (`trng.adoc`: external traffic can resume when the clear ends, independently of internal generator initialization). A DUT that waits for the internal chain, or never resumes (the wait times out), fails. The leaf logs t_xfer and the resume time as observations only. Anchor: port monitor (an observation tap). Evidence: `CHK-XRST-RESUME PASS seed=<n> stream=<i> resumed_clk=<r> t_xfer=<t> internal_init=0`.
|CHK-XRST-STALE
|After the release `fifo_level` equals the M new pairs, and every popped pair is one of them, in order (`reset_controller.adoc`: the pool clears its half-packed word, FIFO contents and fill-stall state). A DUT that keeps an old pair or a half-packed word shows a higher level or an old word. Control: the N pre-reset pairs sit in the pool before the reset (`pre_reset_level` at least 1). Anchor: `STATUS` and `DATA` readback. Evidence: `CHK-XRST-STALE PASS seed=<n> source_reset=1 pops=<k> level=<M> stale_pairs=0 pre_reset_level=<n>`.
|CHK-XRST-CLIENT
|Before the reset each client is served with words from the sent set: the OTBN RND words, and the words that the adapter delivers to AES, KMAC and OTBN URND, are members of the sent set, and the AES ciphertext and the KMAC digest equal their goldens (`trng.adoc`: the crypto adapter distributes the selected stream to the native clients). A DUT that fabricates a word, or delivers one that the source did not send, fails. Anchor: OTBN DMEM, AES `DATA_OUT`, KMAC `STATE`, and `crypto_edn_ack_o[i]` with the beat probe. Evidence: `CHK-XRST-CLIENT PASS seed=<n> client=<aes\|kmac\|otbn_rnd\|otbn_urnd> words_in_sent_set=<k>/<k> result==golden`.
|CHK-XRST-OTBN
|A request of each client (AES, KMAC, OTBN RND, OTBN URND) that is pending during the clear gets no acknowledge in the window and is served after the release with words sent after the clear (`reset_controller.adoc`: requests receive no acknowledgement while the post-mux adapter clears, stay asserted, and are served with fresh entropy). A DUT that acknowledges in the window, drops the request, or serves it with a word sent before the clear fails. Control: the same client gets an acknowledge before the reset (step 2). Anchor: `crypto_edn_req_o[i]`, `crypto_edn_ack_o[i]` and the beat probe. Evidence: `CHK-XRST-OTBN PASS seed=<n> client=<aes\|kmac\|otbn_rnd\|otbn_urnd> req_in_clear=1 ack_in_clear=0 ack_after_release=1 words_from_post_reset=1 ctrl_ack_before=1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The leaf does not claim the word order inside an OTBN RND value (sets are compared), the Key Manager stream (idle, with the Key Manager in reset), or the isolation phase before the reset: the clear window is the window of `trng_reset_active_probe_o`. `sep_trng_reset_recovery_test` keeps the reset with the internal source, the isolate and clear synchronizer timing, and the survival of a non-reset select across a TRNG reset. The source reset of the driver at the rise of the probe is the source's own reset together with the sink reset; the leaf does not claim a source that drops `tvalid` of a live beat.

'''''

[[sep_drbg_sink_mix_rand_test]]
===== 1.53 `sep_drbg_sink_mix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (TRNG and DRBG sinks)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), real fuse sense, `+esrc_noise_force`, with the firmware image and plusargs of the open multi-sink test `sep_drbg_real_sink_multi_km_aes_test` (`firmware = { name = "rom_main", mode = "km_rom_main" }`, `+km_rom_hex=rom_main.rom.parhex`), `reseed = 3`; the external Key Manager leg also uses the external stream driver (added hook, top-port drive); Verilator and VCS; `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`. Each seed runs two runs of the same firmware: the internal run, which holds one sink-set round, and the external Key Manager run.

*Source Module*: `sep_drbg_sink_mix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); entropy golden (bring-up only); Probes and injects. Approved: CPU-LSU master readback (`DATA_OUT`, `STATE`, `STATUS`, `DATA`); `crypto_edn_*`, `km_entropy_*`, `axis1_*`, `axis2_*` and `pool_edn_*`. Added hook: the external stream driver on stream 0, a top-port drive of `ext_trng_axis_req_i[0]` that the test adds to the port table of the HDL Top section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`, for `CHK-MIX-EXT-KM` only. The external stream response monitor on stream 0 and the internal DRBG endpoint probe of streams 0 to 2 (`drbg_int_axis_req[i]`, `drbg_int_axis_rsp[i]`, before the source mux) are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. `CHK-MIX-SINK` uses approved items only.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Dimensions and ranges: the first-request delay of each sink (0 to half of the shortest clean busy time of the set); the counts (AES blocks 4 to 64, KMAC hashes 1 to 8, OTBN RND reads 1 to 8, OTBN URND reads 1 to 8, pool pops 1 to 32, Key Manager sampler words 1 to 16); the AES and KMAC data. The internal run has one round with a drawn sink set: the full set of six sinks with weight 1/2, else the Key Manager sampler plus a drawn subset of 2 to 4 of the other five sinks (3 to 5 sinks). The external Key Manager run draws the gap pattern (gaps of 0 to 32 clocks, or one gap of 64 clocks, all below the reset value of the sampler `CFG.TIMEOUT`, 256 active clocks, `hw/ip/key_manager/doc/architecture.adoc`) and the unique beat words; the number of beats that complete follows from the words that the Key Manager operation reads. `reseed = 3` samples different sink sets and the draws above. The Key Manager sampler is in the set on every seed. Each of the other five sinks is in the set of a leaf with a probability of 0.8, so the sink bins close on a merge of regressions. The leaf logs its plan first (`PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: With the Key Manager sampler, the crypto sinks and the pool active in one run, every requesting sink completes its count. With `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` bit 0 set the Key Manager sampler receives the external stream: each word accepted at external port 0 appears unchanged and in order on the Key Manager stream after the mux, the external source sees the ready of the Key Manager, and no word reaches the crypto or pool stream. Spec: `hw/sys/sep/doc/trng.adoc` (Distribution and Source Selection: stream 0 goes directly to the Key Manager sampler; "The selected source receives the sink's ready signal"; an unselected external input receives ready high and its beats are discarded).

*Test Coverage*: On every seed one drawn sink set: the full set of six sinks (the Key Manager sampler, AES, KMAC, OTBN RND, OTBN URND and pool) with weight 1/2, else the Key Manager sampler plus 2 to 4 of the other five, with drawn start delays and counts and forced overlap of every pair. An external Key Manager run: unique external beats with full strobe and drawn bounded gaps on stream 0, offered until the Key Manager operation ends, with the sampler as the only sink; the beats that complete are graded and at least one completes. This entry extends `sep_crypto_edn_round_robin_grant_test`, which drives the four crypto sinks and the pool in one run and grades the routing, no starvation and the grant; `sep_drbg_real_sink_multi_km_aes_test`, which grades that every stream 1 and Key Manager word is a member of the generated bit multiset; and `sep_crypto_edn_multisink_arbitration_test`, which arbitrates several sinks. This entry grades only the cells that those tests do not grade: the Key Manager sampler together with the crypto sinks and the pool in one run with drawn start delays, counts and forced overlap, and an external stream into the Key Manager sampler. It does not repeat the membership check or the routing check. The open owners keep the rest. Out of scope: the grant order and the endpoint depth; the value of a delivered word (the specification gives no BIW coefficients); a partial strobe on stream 0 (the specification names the strobe rule for the crypto and pool adapters only); a TRNG reset during a sample; the `tuser` of the Key Manager stream (no probe). Functional coverage: `sep_entropy_delivery_cg` (`cp_active_sinks`, `x_sel_stream`, `cp_km_ext_stream`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan and log it. The internal run has one round. Draw its sink set: the full set of six sinks with weight 1/2, else the Key Manager sampler plus a drawn subset of 2 to 4 of AES, KMAC, OTBN RND, OTBN URND and pool. Draw the count of each sink of the set. When both OTBN sinks are in the set, one OTBN program alternates RND and URND reads, so that their busy windows overlap, and they share one start delay.
. Internal run. Write `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` = 0x0 (internal source on all streams). Write `SW_RESET_N` with Key Manager and the engines of the sink set released and the other engines held in reset; log it. Bring up ESRC, CSRNG and EDN as the open entropy tests do, and boot the Key Manager with the firmware image of the open multi-sink test.
. Run each sink of the set alone, one after the other. For AES use ECB with a fixed table key and `PRNG_RESEED_RATE` PER_1; for KMAC use the entropy source EDN, and start each KMAC run, here and in step 5, with a write of `CMD.entropy_req` = 1 while KMAC is idle, so that KMAC requests EDN words in every run and not only for its first seed (`kmac.hjson`, `CMD.entropy_req`: manual reseed in EDN mode); that write is the start of the KMAC sink; for the pool pop the drawn number of words after it fills. Log the clean busy time t_s of each sink: the clocks from its first request to its last completion. Log the request latency r_s of each sink: the clocks from its start to its first request.
. Draw each first-request delay d_s from 0 to half of the shortest t_s of the set, so that every pair of sinks overlaps.
. Run all sinks of the set. Let R be the largest r_s of the set. Start each sink at R - r_s + d_s after a common start marker, so that its first request comes about R + d_s after the marker whatever its request latency. Record every word that completes at the post-mux streams (`km_entropy_*`, `axis1_*`, `axis2_*`) and at the sinks (`crypto_edn_*`, `km_entropy_*`, `pool_edn_*`). The busy window of a sink is the span from its first request to its last completion.
. Expect each sink of the set to reach its count and complete. Compute the overlap of every pair of busy windows and log it. A pair with no overlap logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf.
. External Key Manager run (a separate run of the same firmware). Write `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` = 0x0, bring up ESRC, CSRNG and EDN and boot the Key Manager as in step 2; release only the Key Manager and hold every engine in reset. Draw the gap pattern and unique beat words. The sink set of this run is the Key Manager sampler alone.
. Control. With bit 0 clear, start the Key Manager operation that makes the sampler request words (the operation of step 9). The driver offers unique beats on stream 0, one beat at a time, until the operation ends (the firmware reports completion as in the open multi-sink test; a bounded wait), while the sampler takes its words from the internal source. Expect every beat to see `tready` = 1, and on every clock with `km_entropy_tvalid_o` high expect `km_entropy_tdata_o` to equal the `tdata` of the internal DRBG endpoint of stream 0, whose `tvalid` is high on the same clock. Log the count of discarded beats.
. Write `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` = 0x1 (bit 0 set, bits 1 and 2 clear). Start the Key Manager operation that makes the sampler request words. The driver offers unique beats with full strobe and the drawn gaps, one beat at a time, holding `tvalid` and `tdata` until `tready`, until the operation ends (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-MIX-EXT-KM`). A beat that is still held when the operation ends is not graded. Expect at least one beat to complete.
. Expect every beat that completes at external port 0 to appear at `km_entropy_*` on a clock with `km_entropy_tvalid_o` and `km_entropy_tready_o` high, with the same data and in order; the external `tready` to equal `km_entropy_tready_o` at every sampled clock; and on every clock with `tvalid` high at `axis1_*` or `axis2_*`, the `tdata` to equal the `tdata` of the internal DRBG endpoint of the same stream, whose `tvalid` is high on the same clock (bits 1 and 2 clear select the internal source, and the selection is combinational). Count the clocks with `tvalid` high at `axis1_*` and at `axis2_*` over the run as the control of that last read.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-MIX-SINK
|Every sink of the set reaches its count and completes: the drawn number of AES blocks, KMAC hashes, OTBN reads, pool pops or Key Manager sampler words finishes, and the sink received words at its probe (`trng.adoc`: stream 0 to the Key Manager sampler, stream 1 to the crypto clients with round-robin arbitration, stream 2 to the pool). A DUT that starves a sink fails. Control: the logged overlap of every pair of sinks, so that the sinks request together (`CTRL-MISSING` and a failed leaf on 0). Anchor: the post-adapter probes and the sink results. Evidence: one line per sink, `CHK-MIX-SINK PASS seed=<n> sink=<km\|aes\|kmac\|otbn_rnd\|otbn_urnd\|pool> words=<n> ops=<c> count=<c> done=1`, and one `CTRL-MIX-OVERLAP seed=<n> pair=<a,b> overlap_clk=<k>` line for every pair.
|CHK-MIX-EXT-KM
|Every beat that completes at external port 0 appears at `km_entropy_*` on a clock with `km_entropy_tvalid_o` and `km_entropy_tready_o` high, with the same data and in order; the external `tready` equals `km_entropy_tready_o` on every sampled clock; on every clock with `tvalid` high at `axis1_*` or `axis2_*`, the word equals the word of the internal DRBG endpoint of the same stream, whose `tvalid` is high on that clock (`trng.adoc`: stream 0 goes directly to the Key Manager sampler; the selected source receives the ready of the sink; bits 1 and 2 clear select the internal source; selection is combinational). A DUT that drops or reorders a word, ties the external `tready` high, or routes a driven word to the crypto or pool stream fails. At least one beat completes. Control: with bit 0 clear the same driven beats see `tready` 1, and on every clock with `km_entropy_tvalid_o` high the word at `km_entropy_*` equals the word of the internal DRBG endpoint of stream 0; in the same run `axis1_*` and `axis2_*` show `tvalid` high, so both compares are live (no such clock on either probe logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). Anchor: external stream response monitor and internal DRBG endpoint probe (observation taps), `km_entropy_*`, `axis1_*`, `axis2_*`. Evidence: `CHK-MIX-EXT-KM PASS seed=<n> beats=<n> xfers=<n> order_ok=1 data_ok=1 tready_eq=1 axis1_eq=1 axis2_eq=1 ctrl_internal_discard=<n> ctrl_km_eq=1 ctrl_axis1_valid_clk=<a> ctrl_axis2_valid_clk=<b>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: The leaf does not claim the grant order, the endpoint depth, or the value of a delivered word. It does not claim a partial strobe on stream 0, a TRNG reset during a sample, or the `tuser` of the Key Manager stream. `sep_crypto_edn_round_robin_grant_test` keeps the routing, no starvation and the grant of the crypto sinks and the pool; `sep_drbg_real_sink_multi_km_aes_test` keeps the membership of every stream 1 and Key Manager word in the generated bit multiset; `sep_crypto_edn_round_robin_grant_test` also keeps the routing of a beat to one sink; `sep_crypto_edn_multisink_arbitration_test` keeps the arbitration of several sinks.

'''''
